### [1] [SORS: A Modular, High-Fidelity Simulator for Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15994)
*Manuel Mekkattu,Mike Y. Michelis,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SORS是一个用于软体机器人的高保真仿真器，采用基于有限元的能量框架，通过约束非线性优化处理接触，在多种真实实验中验证了其物理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人在多物理场环境中的部署需要先进的仿真框架，现有仿真器难以处理软体机器人的大非线性变形、材料不可压缩性和接触交互等挑战，缺乏可扩展性和应用相关性。

Method: 提出SORS仿真器，采用基于有限元法的能量框架，支持模块化扩展，集成基于序列二次规划的约束非线性优化来处理物理一致的接触交互。

Result: 通过悬臂梁偏转、软体机械臂压力驱动、PokeFlex数据集接触交互等多种真实实验验证，仿真器能够准确捕捉材料基本行为和复杂驱动动力学，并展示了在软体机器人腿控制优化中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: SORS通过填补软体机器人生态系统中可扩展性、保真度和可用性的空白，为下一代软体机器人原型设计提供了经过验证的工具，有效缩小了仿真与现实的差距。

Abstract: The deployment of complex soft robots in multiphysics environments requires advanced simulation frameworks that not only capture interactions between different types of material, but also translate accurately to real-world performance. Soft robots pose unique modeling challenges due to their large nonlinear deformations, material incompressibility, and contact interactions, which complicate both numerical stability and physical accuracy. Despite recent progress, robotic simulators often struggle with modeling such phenomena in a scalable and application-relevant manner. We present SORS (Soft Over Rigid Simulator), a versatile, high-fidelity simulator designed to handle these complexities for soft robot applications. Our energy-based framework, built on the finite element method, allows modular extensions, enabling the inclusion of custom-designed material and actuation models. To ensure physically consistent contact handling, we integrate a constrained nonlinear optimization based on sequential quadratic programming, allowing for stable and accurate modeling of contact phenomena. We validate our simulator through a diverse set of real-world experiments, which include cantilever deflection, pressure-actuation of a soft robotic arm, and contact interactions from the PokeFlex dataset. In addition, we showcase the potential of our framework for control optimization of a soft robotic leg. These tests confirm that our simulator can capture both fundamental material behavior and complex actuation dynamics with high physical fidelity. By bridging the sim-to-real gap in these challenging domains, our approach provides a validated tool for prototyping next-generation soft robots, filling the gap of extensibility, fidelity, and usability in the soft robotic ecosystem.

</details>


### [2] [Digital-Twin Losses for Lane-Compliant Trajectory Prediction at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05546)
*Kuo-Yi Chao,Erik Leo Haß,Melina Gegg,Jiajie Zhang,Ralph Raßhofer,Alois Christian Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出数字孪生驱动的V2X轨迹预测管道，结合车辆和基础设施协同感知预测信号交叉口多智能体运动，通过双损失训练方案（MSE+孪生损失）确保安全合规预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在V2X支持的复杂城市环境中，准确且安全意识的轨迹预测是智能交通系统的关键技术，需要解决多智能体交互、交通规则遵守和碰撞避免等问题

Method: 创建数字孪生驱动的V2X轨迹预测管道，结合车辆和基础设施协同感知；采用Bi-LSTM生成器，设计结构化训练目标：标准MSE损失+新型孪生损失（编码基础设施约束、碰撞避免、预测模式多样性和数字孪生衍生的规则先验）

Result: 在真实世界V2X数据上训练评估，除了标准轨迹指标（ADE、FDE），引入ITS相关安全指标（基础设施和规则违规率）；实验结果表明，所提训练方案显著减少关键违规，同时保持可比预测精度和实时性能

Conclusion: 数字孪生驱动的多损失学习在V2X智能交通系统中具有潜力，能够实现场景一致且安全合规的预测，平衡预测准确性与安全约束

Abstract: Accurate and safety-conscious trajectory prediction is a key technology for intelligent transportation systems, especially in V2X-enabled urban environments with complex multi-agent interactions. In this paper, we created a digital twin-driven V2X trajectory prediction pipeline that jointly leverages cooperative perception from vehicles and infrastructure to forecast multi-agent motion at signalized intersections. The proposed model combines a Bi-LSTM-based generator with a structured training objective consisting of a standard mean squared error (MSE) loss and a novel twin loss.
  The twin loss encodes infrastructure constraints, collision avoidance, diversity across predicted modes, and rule-based priors derived from the digital twin. While the MSE term ensures point-wise accuracy, the twin loss penalizes traffic rule violations, predicted collisions, and mode collapse, guiding the model toward scene-consistent and safety-compliant predictions.
  We train and evaluate our approach on real-world V2X data sent from the intersection to the vehicle and collected in urban corridors. In addition to standard trajectory metrics (ADE, FDE), we introduce ITS-relevant safety indicators, including infrastructure and rule violation rates. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed training scheme significantly reduces critical violations while maintaining comparable prediction accuracy and real-time performance, highlighting the potential of digital twin-driven multi-loss learning for V2X-enabled intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [3] [Introducing the transitional autonomous vehicle lane-changing dataset: Empirical Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05716)
*Abhinav Sharma,Zijun He,Danjue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了NC-tALC数据集，这是一个高精度轨迹数据集，用于分析过渡期自动驾驶车辆在换道操作中的交互行为，包含152次试验，采样频率20Hz，厘米级RTK-GPS精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着过渡期自动驾驶车辆与人工驾驶车辆共享道路，在复杂操作如换道时可能出现新的交通模式，影响交通稳定性和安全性。评估这些动态需要高分辨率轨迹数据，但目前缺乏捕捉tAV换道行为的数据集。

Method: 研究开发了NC-tALC数据集，包含两个受控实验系列：1) tAV换道实验：tAV在配备自适应巡航控制的目标车辆存在时执行换道，分析换道执行；2) tAV响应实验：两个tAV作为跟随者响应另一个tAV切入操作，分析跟随者响应动态。数据集包含152次试验（72次换道和80次响应试验），采样频率20Hz，采用厘米级RTK-GPS精度。

Result: 创建了NC-tALC数据集，这是一个高保真轨迹数据集，专门用于表征tAV在换道操作中的交互行为。数据集提供了受控强制换道场景下评估tAV决策和交互动态的严格实证基础。

Conclusion: NC-tALC数据集填补了tAV换道行为数据集的空白，为研究过渡期自动驾驶车辆在换道操作中的交互动态提供了重要的实证资源，有助于评估交通稳定性和安全性影响。

Abstract: Transitional autonomous vehicles (tAVs), which operate beyond SAE Level 1-2 automation but short of full autonomy, are increasingly sharing the road with human-driven vehicles (HDVs). As these systems interact during complex maneuvers such as lane changes, new patterns may emerge with implications for traffic stability and safety. Assessing these dynamics, particularly during mandatory lane changes, requires high-resolution trajectory data, yet datasets capturing tAV lane-changing behavior are scarce.
  This study introduces the North Carolina Transitional Autonomous Vehicle Lane-Changing (NC-tALC) Dataset, a high-fidelity trajectory dataset designed to characterize tAV interactions during lane-changing maneuvers. The dataset includes two controlled experimental series. In the first, tAV lane-changing experiments, a tAV executes lane changes in the presence of adaptive cruise control (ACC) equipped target vehicles, enabling analysis of lane-changing execution. In the second, tAV responding experiments, two tAVs act as followers and respond to cut-in maneuvers initiated by another tAV, enabling analysis of follower response dynamics. The dataset contains 152 trials (72 lane-changing and 80 responding trials) sampled at 20 Hz with centimeter-level RTK-GPS accuracy. The NC-tALC dataset provides a rigorous empirical foundation for evaluating tAV decision-making and interaction dynamics in controlled mandatory lane-changing scenarios.

</details>


### [4] [Open-Source Based and ETSI Compliant Cooperative, Connected, and Automated Mini-Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06343)
*Lorenzo Farina,Federico Gavioli,Salvatore Iandolo,Francesco Moretti,Giuseppe Perrone,Matteo Piccoli,Francesco Raviglione,Marco Rapelli,Antonio Solida,Paolo Burgio,Carlo Augusto Grazia,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出使用1:10比例缩小的合作、自主、互联迷你车作为从仿真到实际实验的中间步骤，降低自动驾驶系统开发成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车行业正从人类控制向完全自动化、互联和协作发展，但新算法和协议的实地测试成本高昂，从仿真到实际实验需要巨大投资，可能成为障碍

Method: 开发1:10比例迷你车平台，配备Jetson Orin板运行ROS2用于自主操作，Raspberry Pi板运行开源OScar实现完整的ETSI C-ITS兼容堆栈

Result: 通过实施Day-1交叉口碰撞预警应用案例研究，验证了该平台的可行性和潜力

Conclusion: 提出的迷你车平台为自动驾驶系统开发提供了经济高效的中间测试步骤，有助于降低从仿真到实际实验的过渡成本

Abstract: The automotive sector is following a revolutionary path from vehicles controlled by humans to vehicles that will be fully automated, fully connected, and ultimately fully cooperative. Along this road, new cooperative algorithms and protocols will be designed and field tested, which represents a great challenge in terms of costs. In this context, in particular, moving from simulations to practical experiments requires huge investments that are not always affordable and may become a barrier in some cases. To solve this issue and provide the community with an intermediate step, we here propose the use of 1:10 scaled cooperative, autonomous, and connected mini-cars. The mini-car is equipped with a Jetson Orin board running the open Robot Operating System 2 (ROS2), sensors for autonomous operations, and a Raspberry Pi board for connectivity mounting the open source Open Stack for Car (OScar). A key aspect of the proposal is the use of OScar, which implements a full ETSI cooperative-intelligent transport systems (C-ITS) compliant stack. The feasibility and potential of the proposed platform is here demonstrated through the implementation of a case study where the Day-1 intersection collision warning (ICW) application is implemented and validated.

</details>


### [5] [TEGA: A Tactile-Enhanced Grasping Assistant for Assistive Robotics via Sensor Fusion and Closed-Loop Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05552)
*Hengxu You,Tianyu Zhou,Fang Xu,Kaleb Smith,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TEGA是一个闭环辅助遥操作系统，融合肌电意图-力推断和视觉触觉感知，通过可穿戴触觉背心提供实时振动触觉反馈，实现直观的比例力调节，显著提升抓握稳定性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遥操作系统主要关注手指位置控制以实现抓握配置，但忽视了同样关键的抓握力调节任务。这对于处理不同硬度、纹理和形状的物体至关重要。这一局限性对用户（特别是上肢残疾者）构成重大挑战，因为他们缺乏自然触觉反馈，只能依赖间接线索推断合适的力水平。

Method: 提出触觉增强抓握助手(TEGA)，这是一个闭环辅助遥操作框架：1) 融合基于肌电的意图-力推断；2) 将视觉触觉感知映射为实时振动触觉反馈；3) 通过可穿戴触觉背心提供反馈，使用户能够在操作过程中动态调整抓握力。

Result: 用户研究证实，该系统显著提高了抓握稳定性和任务成功率，证明了其在辅助机器人应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: TEGA框架通过整合肌电意图推断和触觉反馈，解决了传统遥操作系统中抓握力调节的不足，为辅助机器人应用提供了有效的解决方案，特别适合上肢残疾用户。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled sophisticated manipulation of dexterous robotic hands, with most systems concentrating on guiding finger positions to achieve desired grasp configurations. However, while accurate finger positioning is essential, it often overlooks the equally critical task of grasp force modulation, vital for handling objects of diverse hardness, texture, and shape. This limitation poses a significant challenge for users, especially individuals with upper limb disabilities who lack natural tactile feedback and rely on indirect cues to infer appropriate force levels. To address this gap, We present the tactile enhanced grasping assistant (TEGA), a closed loop assistive teleoperation framework that fuses EMG based intent2force inference with visuotactile sensing mapped into real time vibrotactile feedback via a wearable haptic vest, enabling intuitive, proportional force adjustment during manipulation. A wearable haptic vest delivers real time tactile feedback, allowing users to dynamically refine grasp force during manipulation. User studies confirm that the system substantially improves grasp stability and task success, underscoring its potential for assistive robotic applications.

</details>


### [6] [Improved hopping control on slopes for small robots using spring mass modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05902)
*Heston Roberts,Pronoy Sarker,Sm Ashikul Islam,Min Gyu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文分析了斜坡上跳跃机器人因地面倾斜导致着陆时产生不必要旋转而失去平衡的问题，提出了通过调整身体触地角度和施加小扭矩修正的简单解决方案，显著提高了斜坡跳跃稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳跃机器人在斜坡上经常失去平衡，因为倾斜的地面在着陆时会产生不想要的旋转力矩，导致机器人不稳定。这种效应限制了机器人在不平坦地形（如山坡、碎石等自然环境）中的可靠导航能力。

Method: 使用简单的弹簧质量模型分析斜坡引起的冲击效应，提出两种简单修正方法：1）根据斜坡角度调整身体触地角度；2）在起飞前施加小的修正扭矩。这两种措施共同作用，有效抵消倾斜地形引起的不必要旋转。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使是小的斜坡感知调整也能显著提高着陆稳定性。该方法足够简单，可以在低成本机器人平台上实现，无需复杂的传感或计算，使机器人能够在陡峭斜坡上平稳着陆并保持稳定跳跃。

Conclusion: 通过结合分析模型和最小控制动作，该方法为实现在不平坦地形上的可靠跳跃提供了实用途径，适用于未来需要在自然环境中导航的自主野外机器人。

Abstract: Hopping robots often lose balance on slopes because the tilted ground creates unwanted rotation at landing. This work analyzes that effect using a simple spring mass model and identifies how slope induced impulses destabilize the robot. To address this, we introduce two straightforward fixes, adjusting the bodys touchdown angle based on the slope and applying a small corrective torque before takeoff. Together, these steps effectively cancel the unwanted rotation caused by inclined terrain, allowing the robot to land smoothly and maintain stable hopping even on steep slopes. Moreover, the proposed method remains simple enough to implement on low cost robotic platforms without requiring complex sensing or computation. By combining this analytical model with minimal control actions, this approach provides a practical path toward reliable hopping on uneven terrain. The results from simulation confirm that even small slope aware adjustments can dramatically improve landing stability, making the technique suitable for future autonomous field robots that must navigate natural environments such as hills, rubble, and irregular outdoor landscapes.

</details>


### [7] [Task Parameter Extrapolation via Learning Inverse Tasks from Forward Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05576)
*Serdar Bahar,Fatih Dogangun,Matteo Saveriano,Yukie Nagai,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于任务逆学习框架的联合学习方法，通过构建前向和逆任务的共同表示，利用新配置的前向演示来执行相应的逆任务，实现准确高效的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中技能策略泛化到新条件仍是一个关键挑战。模仿学习方法虽然数据高效，但局限于训练区域，在输入数据超出该区域时会导致不可预测的策略失败。迁移学习方法虽然对环境或任务变化具有鲁棒性，但数据需求大且在零样本泛化中缺乏准确性。

Method: 在任务逆学习框架下提出一种新颖的联合学习方法，构建前向和逆任务的共同表示，利用来自新配置的辅助前向演示来成功执行相应的逆任务，无需任何直接监督。

Result: 通过消融研究和在模拟及真实环境中的实验展示了框架的外推能力，实验涉及需要复杂操作技能的各种物体和工具，结果表明该方法优于基于扩散的替代方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过任务逆学习框架解决了机器人学习中技能泛化的挑战，实现了准确高效的知识迁移，在复杂操作任务中表现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Generalizing skill policies to novel conditions remains a key challenge in robot learning. Imitation learning methods, while data-efficient, are largely confined to the training region and consistently fail on input data outside it, leading to unpredictable policy failures. Alternatively, transfer learning approaches offer methods for trajectory generation robust to both changes in environment or tasks, but they remain data-hungry and lack accuracy in zero-shot generalization. We address these challenges by framing the problem in the context of task inversion learning and proposing a novel joint learning approach to achieve accurate and efficient knowledge transfer. Our method constructs a common representation of the forward and inverse tasks, and leverages auxiliary forward demonstrations from novel configurations to successfully execute the corresponding inverse tasks, without any direct supervision. We show the extrapolation capabilities of our framework via ablation studies and experiments in simulated and real-world environments that require complex manipulation skills with a diverse set of objects and tools, where we outperform diffusion-based alternatives.

</details>


### [8] [Control Lyapunov Functions for Underactuated Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05638)
*Huy Pham,Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于欠驱动软体机器人任务空间控制的通用框架，在输入受限条件下保证稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人固有的欠驱动特性和严格的执行器限制使得任务空间控制具有挑战性，现有非线性控制策略通常假设全驱动且无执行器限制

Method: 将快速指数稳定控制Lyapunov函数作为凸不等式约束，同时满足欠驱动全身动力学和执行器边界约束

Result: 在多种欠驱动平台上验证，相比基线方法提高了任务空间精度，在输入限制下保持Lyapunov收敛性，实现了优越的定点控制和轨迹跟踪性能

Conclusion: 该框架为欠驱动软体机器人提供了在输入受限条件下的稳定任务空间控制解决方案

Abstract: Soft and soft-rigid hybrid robots are inherently underactuated and operate under tight actuator limits, making task-space control with stability guarantees challenging. Common nonlinear strategies for soft robots (e.g., those based on PD control) often rely on the assumption of full actuation with no actuator limits. This paper presents a general control framework for task-space regulation and tracking of underactuated soft robots under bounded inputs. The method enforces a rapidly exponentially stabilizing control Lyapunov function as a convex inequality constraint while simultaneously satisfying underactuated full-body dynamics and actuator bounds. We validate the approach in simulation on several platforms spanning increasing underactuation: a simple two link tendon-driven "finger", a trimmed helicoid manipulator, and a highly underactuated spiral robot. We compare against a number of baseline methods from the literature. Results show improved task-space accuracy and consistent Lyapunov convergence under input limits, achieving superior set-point and trajectory-tracking performance.

</details>


### [9] [Control Barrier Corridors: From Safety Functions to Safe Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06494)
*Ömür Arslan,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的控制屏障走廊概念，将控制屏障函数和安全运动走廊这两种安全方法统一起来，通过将控制屏障函数转换为局部安全目标区域，用于反馈控制系统中的参考目标选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人安全自主性在非结构化复杂环境中至关重要。控制屏障函数和安全运动走廊是两种广泛使用但技术不同的安全方法，前者是功能性的，后者是几何性的。论文旨在统一这两种方法，以增强安全运动规划和控制的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出控制屏障走廊的新概念，将控制屏障函数转换为局部安全目标区域，用于参考目标选择。该方法适用于完全驱动系统、运动学独轮车和线性输出调节系统，要求控制收敛速率与屏障衰减速率匹配。

Result: 证明对于凸屏障函数，只要控制收敛速率与屏障衰减速率匹配，单个状态安全性可以扩展到控制屏障走廊上的局部安全性。这揭示了安全性和响应性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 控制屏障走廊能够在系统运动过程中持续变化的屏障走廊上实现安全可达的持续目标选择，为未知环境中的自主探索提供了可验证的安全和持续路径跟踪能力。

Abstract: Safe autonomy is a critical requirement and a key enabler for robots to operate safely in unstructured complex environments. Control barrier functions and safe motion corridors are two widely used but technically distinct safety methods, functional and geometric, respectively, for safe motion planning and control. Control barrier functions are applied to the safety filtering of control inputs to limit the decay rate of system safety, whereas safe motion corridors are geometrically constructed to define a local safe zone around the system state for use in motion optimization and reference-governor design. This paper introduces a new notion of control barrier corridors, which unifies these two approaches by converting control barrier functions into local safe goal regions for reference goal selection in feedback control systems. We show, with examples on fully actuated systems, kinematic unicycles, and linear output regulation systems, that individual state safety can be extended locally over control barrier corridors for convex barrier functions, provided the control convergence rate matches the barrier decay rate, highlighting a trade-off between safety and reactiveness. Such safe control barrier corridors enable safely reachable persistent goal selection over continuously changing barrier corridors during system motion, which we demonstrate for verifiably safe and persistent path following in autonomous exploration of unknown environments.

</details>


### [10] [RFM-HRI : A Multimodal Dataset of Medical Robot Failure, User Reaction and Recovery Preferences for Item Retrieval Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05641)
*Yashika Batra,Giuliano Pioldi,Promise Ekpo,Arman Sayatqyzy,Purnjay Maruur,Shalom Otieno,Kevin Ching,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RFM-HRI数据集：首个医疗人机交互中机器人故障的多模态数据集，包含41名参与者在实验室和医院环境中对四种通信故障类型的反应数据，揭示了故障显著降低情感效价和感知控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中机器人交互故障不可避免，但用户如何通过言语和非言语行为应对这些故障在医疗人机交互中研究不足。医疗场景中交互故障直接影响任务表现和用户信任，需要系统研究。

Method: 采用Wizard-of-Oz方法，在实验室和医院环境中对41名参与者进行研究，系统诱导四种故障类型（语音、时序、理解和搜索），记录214个交互样本，包括面部动作单元、头部姿态、语音转录和事后自我报告。

Result: 故障显著降低情感效价和感知控制；故障与困惑、恼怒和沮丧强烈相关，而成功交互则表现为惊讶、宽慰和任务完成信心；情绪反应随重复故障演变，困惑减少而沮丧增加。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：公开可用的多模态数据集RFM-HRI、用户对不同故障类型反应的分析及偏好恢复策略、支持系统比较恢复策略的医疗推车检索场景。为具身人机交互中的故障检测和恢复方法奠定基础。

Abstract: While robots deployed in real-world environments inevitably experience interaction failures, understanding how users respond through verbal and non-verbal behaviors remains under-explored in human-robot interaction (HRI). This gap is particularly significant in healthcare-inspired settings, where interaction failures can directly affect task performance and user trust. We present the Robot Failures in Medical HRI (RFM-HRI) Dataset, a multimodal dataset capturing dyadic interactions between humans and robots embodied in crash carts, where communication failures are systematically induced during item retrieval tasks.
  Through Wizard-of-Oz studies with 41 participants across laboratory and hospital settings, we recorded responses to four failure types (speech, timing, comprehension, and search) derived from three years of crash-cart robot interaction data. The dataset contains 214 interaction samples including facial action units, head pose, speech transcripts, and post-interaction self-reports. Our analysis shows that failures significantly degrade affective valence and reduce perceived control compared to successful interactions.
  Failures are strongly associated with confusion, annoyance, and frustration, while successful interactions are characterized by surprise, relief, and confidence in task completion. Emotional responses also evolve across repeated failures, with confusion decreasing and frustration increasing over time.
  This work contributes (1) a publicly available multimodal dataset (RFM-HRI), (2) analysis of user responses to different failure types and preferred recovery strategies, and (3) a crash-cart retrieval scenario enabling systematic comparison of recovery strategies with implications for safety-critical failure recovery. Our findings provide a foundation for failure detection and recovery methods in embodied HRI.

</details>


### [11] [Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Dynamics For Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06548)
*Edward Morgan,Nenyi K Dadson,Corina Barbalata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于水下航行器-机械臂系统的线性参数化不确定性感知自适应动力学模型框架，通过移动视界估计结合物理一致性约束，实现在线参数估计和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器-机械臂系统受时变水动力效应影响，需要精确且自适应的动力学模型。现有方法难以同时保证物理一致性、在线适应性和不确定性量化。

Method: 提出线性参数化的不确定性感知自适应动力学模型框架，采用移动视界估计堆叠历史回归器，并嵌入惯性、阻尼、摩擦和静水力的凸物理一致性约束，在线估计参数并量化其演化不确定性。

Result: 在BlueROV2 Heavy和4自由度机械臂上的实验显示：机械臂拟合R²达0.88-0.98，斜率接近1；航行器纵荡、垂荡和横摇在强耦合和噪声下保持良好保真度；中值求解时间约0.023秒/更新；相比固定参数模型，各自由度MAE和RMSE均降低；参数物理合理，置信区间覆盖率近100%。

Conclusion: 该框架能在线估计物理一致的参数并量化不确定性，实现快速收敛和校准预测，为水下环境中的可靠前馈控制和仿真提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate and adaptive dynamic models are critical for underwater vehicle-manipulator systems where hydrodynamic effects induce time-varying parameters. This paper introduces a novel uncertainty-aware adaptive dynamics model framework that remains linear in lumped vehicle and manipulator parameters, and embeds convex physical consistency constraints during online estimation. Moving horizon estimation is used to stack horizon regressors, enforce realizable inertia, damping, friction, and hydrostatics, and quantify uncertainty from parameter evolution. Experiments on a BlueROV2 Heavy with a 4-DOF manipulator demonstrate rapid convergence and calibrated predictions. Manipulator fits achieve R2 = 0.88 to 0.98 with slopes near unity, while vehicle surge, heave, and roll are reproduced with good fidelity under stronger coupling and noise. Median solver time is approximately 0.023 s per update, confirming online feasibility. A comparison against a fixed parameter model shows consistent reductions in MAE and RMSE across degrees of freedom. Results indicate physically plausible parameters and confidence intervals with near 100% coverage, enabling reliable feedforward control and simulation in underwater environments.

</details>


### [12] [Relational Semantic Reasoning on 3D Scene Graphs for Open World Interactive Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05642)
*Imen Mahdi,Matteo Cassinelli,Fabien Despinoy,Tim Welschehold,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT：基于场景图探索与学习效用的开放世界交互式物体搜索方法，通过离线程序蒸馏从大语言模型提取结构化关系知识，实现高效语义推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中的开放世界交互式物体搜索需要理解物体与周围环境的语义关系以高效引导探索。现有方法要么依赖视觉语言嵌入相似性（无法可靠捕捉任务相关的关系语义），要么使用大语言模型（太慢且昂贵，不适合实时部署）。

Method: SCOUT方法直接在3D场景图上搜索，通过关系探索启发式（如房间-物体包含关系和物体-物体共现关系）为房间、边界和物体分配效用分数。采用离线程序蒸馏框架从大语言模型中提取结构化关系知识到轻量级模型，实现机器人端推理。同时提出SymSearch符号基准用于评估交互式物体搜索中的语义推理能力。

Result: 在符号和仿真环境中的广泛评估表明，SCOUT优于基于嵌入相似性的方法，匹配大语言模型级别的性能，同时保持计算效率。真实世界实验展示了在物理环境中的有效迁移，能够在实际感知和导航约束下实现开放世界交互式物体搜索。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过将大语言模型的关系知识蒸馏到轻量级模型中，实现了高效、实时的开放世界交互式物体搜索，在保持语义推理能力的同时解决了现有方法的计算效率问题。

Abstract: Open-world interactive object search in household environments requires understanding semantic relationships between objects and their surrounding context to guide exploration efficiently. Prior methods either rely on vision-language embeddings similarity, which does not reliably capture task-relevant relational semantics, or large language models (LLMs), which are too slow and costly for real-time deployment. We introduce SCOUT: Scene Graph-Based Exploration with Learned Utility for Open-World Interactive Object Search, a novel method that searches directly over 3D scene graphs by assigning utility scores to rooms, frontiers, and objects using relational exploration heuristics such as room-object containment and object-object co-occurrence. To make this practical without sacrificing open-vocabulary generalization, we propose an offline procedural distillation framework that extracts structured relational knowledge from LLMs into lightweight models for on-robot inference. Furthermore, we present SymSearch, a scalable symbolic benchmark for evaluating semantic reasoning in interactive object search tasks. Extensive evaluations across symbolic and simulation environments show that SCOUT outperforms embedding similarity-based methods and matches LLM-level performance while remaining computationally efficient. Finally, real-world experiments demonstrate effective transfer to physical environments, enabling open-world interactive object search under realistic sensing and navigation constraints.

</details>


### [13] [TransMASK: Masked State Representation through Learned Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05670)
*Sagar Parekh,Preston Culbertson,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TransMASK：一种自监督方法，学习一个掩码来将观测状态转换为偏向相关元素的潜在表示，从而提高模仿学习的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类期望机器人在新环境中执行已学习的任务，但机器人需要区分状态中的相关与无关元素（如桌子颜色、背景杂物）。现有方法缺乏自动识别相关状态的能力，导致泛化能力不足

Method: 提出TransMASK自监督方法：学习一个掩码矩阵，与观测状态相乘得到潜在表示；通过策略梯度训练，使掩码与专家策略的雅可比矩阵对齐；可无缝集成到各种模仿学习框架（如扩散策略）中

Result: TransMASK能够自动识别并增强相关状态元素，抑制无关元素；与现有方法相比，在提取相关状态用于下游模仿学习方面表现更优

Conclusion: TransMASK通过自监督学习状态掩码，使机器人策略专注于任务相关状态，提高了模仿学习的泛化能力和环境适应性

Abstract: Humans train robots to complete tasks in one environment, and expect robots to perform those same tasks in new environments. As humans, we know which aspects of the environment (i.e., the state) are relevant to the task. But there are also things that do not matter; e.g., the color of the table or the presence of clutter in the background. Ideally, the robot's policy learns to ignore these irrelevant state components. Achieving this invariance improves generalization: the robot knows not to factor irrelevant variables into its control decisions, making the policy more robust to environment changes. In this paper we therefore propose a self-supervised method to learn a mask which, when multiplied by the observed state, transforms that state into a latent representation that is biased towards relevant elements. Our method -- which we call TransMASK -- can be combined with a variety of imitation learning frameworks (such as diffusion policies) without any additional labels or alterations to the loss function. To achieve this, we recognize that the learned policy updates to better match the human's true policy. This true policy only depends on the relevant parts of the state; hence, as the gradients pass back through the learned policy and our proposed mask, they increase the value for elements that cause the robot to better imitate the human. We can therefore train TransMASK at the same time as we learn the policy. By normalizing the magnitude of each row in TransMASK, we force the mask to align with the Jacobian of the expert policy: columns that correspond to relevant states have large magnitudes, while columns for irrelevant states approach zero magnitude. We compare our approach to other methods that extract relevant states for downstream imitation learning. See our project website: https://collab.me.vt.edu/TransMASK/

</details>


### [14] [Contact-Grounded Policy: Dexterous Visuotactile Policy with Generative Contact Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05687)
*Zhengtong Xu,Yeping Wang,Ben Abbatematteo,Jom Preechayasomboon,Sonny Chan,Nick Colonnese,Amirhossein H. Memar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CGP是一种基于接触的灵巧操作策略，通过预测机器人状态和触觉反馈来建立多点接触基础，并使用学习到的接触一致性映射将预测转换为控制器可执行目标，支持密集触觉阵列和视觉触觉传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决灵巧操作中需要精细、接触丰富的控制问题，传统方法难以处理多点接触和触觉反馈的复杂交互，需要一种能够有效利用触觉信息并转换为控制器可执行指令的方法。

Method: 通过预测实际机器人状态和触觉反馈来建立多点接触基础，使用学习到的接触一致性映射将预测转换为控制器可执行目标，支持密集触觉阵列和视觉触觉传感器，通过遥操作收集仿真和物理机器人演示数据。

Result: 在多个灵巧操作任务中评估了CGP，展示了其能够实现精细、接触丰富的灵巧操作，支持不同类型的触觉传感器配置。

Conclusion: CGP提供了一种有效的基于接触的灵巧操作策略框架，能够利用触觉反馈实现精细控制，为灵巧操作任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-Grounded Policy (CGP) enables fine-grained, contact-rich dexterous manipulation by grounding multi-point contacts through predicting the actual robot state and tactile feedback, and by using a learned contact-consistency mapping to convert these predictions into controller-executable targets for a compliance controller. CGP supports both dense tactile arrays and vision-based tactile sensors mounted on the hand. We collect demonstrations via teleoperation in both simulation and on a physical robot, and evaluate CGP across multiple dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [15] [Environment-Aware Path Generation for Robotic Additive Manufacturing of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05748)
*Mahsa Rabiei,Reza Moini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个环境感知路径生成框架，用于在线生成增材制造结构路径，评估四种路径规划算法在障碍环境中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前增材制造设计方法依赖离线预定义工具路径，无法适应动态施工环境（如地面和地外环境中的障碍物），需要在线路径生成方法

Method: 提出环境感知路径生成框架，集成四种路径规划算法（两种搜索基、两种采样基），开发结构和计算性能指标，在密集障碍环境中评估算法可行性

Result: 评估了四种路径规划器在挑战性环境中的可行性，确定了结构和计算性能的关键指标，识别了在挑战性环境下最有前景的路径规划器

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了环境感知的在线路径生成，为机器人增材制造在复杂障碍环境中的应用提供了有效的路径规划解决方案

Abstract: Robotic Additive Manufacturing (AM) has emerged as a scalable and customizable construction method in the last decade. However, current AM design methods rely on pre-conceived (A priori) toolpath of the structure, often developed via offline slicing software. Moreover, considering the dynamic construction environments involving obstacles on terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments, there is a need for online path generation methods. Here, an environment-aware path generation framework (PGF) is proposed for the first time in which structures are designed in an online fashion by utilizing four path planning (PP) algorithms (two search-based and two sampling-based). To evaluate the performance of the proposed PGF in different obstacle arrangements (periodic, random) for two types of structures (closed and open), structural (path roughness, turns, offset, Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), deviation) and computational (run time) performance metrics are developed. Most challenging environments (i.e., dense with high number of obstacles) are considered to saturate the feasibility limits of PP algorithms. The capability of each of the four path planners used in the PGF in finding a feasible path is assessed. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed structural performance metrics is evaluated individually and comparatively, and most essential metrics necessary for evaluation of toolpath of the resulting structures are prescribed. Consequently, the most promising path planners in challenging environments are identified for robotic additive manufacturing applications.

</details>


### [16] [Vision-Language System using Open-Source LLMs for Gestures in Medical Interpreter Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05751)
*Thanh-Tung Ngo,Emma Murphy,Robert J. Ross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于医疗翻译机器人的隐私保护视觉语言框架，通过LLM检测特定言语行为（同意和指令）并生成对应机器人手势，在本地部署开源模型实现高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗环境中，跨语言沟通至关重要，非语言线索和手势是关键要素。需要开发能够理解临床对话意图并生成适当手势的机器人系统，同时保护患者隐私。

Method: 基于本地部署的开源模型构建隐私保护框架，使用LLM进行少样本提示的意图检测，开发包含临床对话言语行为标注和手势配对的创新数据集，实现言语行为识别和手势生成模块。

Result: 识别模块达到0.90准确率、0.93加权精确率和0.91加权F1分数。用户研究表明，系统在手势人类相似性方面优于基线方法，同时保持相当的适当性，显著提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了医疗翻译机器人的隐私保护手势生成，在意图识别准确性和手势自然度方面表现优异，为跨语言医疗沟通提供了有效的技术解决方案。

Abstract: Effective communication is vital in healthcare, especially across language barriers, where non-verbal cues and gestures are critical. This paper presents a privacy-preserving vision-language framework for medical interpreter robots that detects specific speech acts (consent and instruction) and generates corresponding robotic gestures. Built on locally deployed open-source models, the system utilizes a Large Language Model (LLM) with few-shot prompting for intent detection. We also introduce a novel dataset of clinical conversations annotated for speech acts and paired with gesture clips. Our identification module achieved 0.90 accuracy, 0.93 weighted precision, and a 0.91 weighted F1-Score. Our approach significantly improves computational efficiency and, in user studies, outperforms the speech-gesture generation baseline in human-likeness while maintaining comparable appropriateness.

</details>


### [17] [Safe-Night VLA: Seeing the Unseen via Thermal-Perceptive Vision-Language-Action Models for Safety-Critical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05754)
*Dian Yu,Qingchuan Zhou,Bingkun Huang,Majid Khadiv,Zewen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Safe-Night VLA是一个多模态机器人操作框架，通过集成长波红外热感知和安全约束，实现热感知操作并确保安全执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型主要依赖RGB感知，无法捕捉热信号等不可见模态；端到端生成策略缺乏明确的安全约束，在遇到障碍和训练分布外场景时脆弱

Method: 将长波红外热感知集成到预训练视觉语言骨干中，实现基于热力学特性的语义推理；通过控制屏障函数的安全过滤器提供确定性工作空间约束执行

Result: 在Franka机械臂上进行真实世界实验，引入温度条件操作、地下目标定位和反射消歧等新评估范式，在推理时保持约束执行；性能优于仅RGB基线

Conclusion: Safe-Night VLA展示了基础模型可以有效利用非可见物理模态进行鲁棒操作，同时通过安全约束确保在分布外条件下的安全执行

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely primarily on RGB perception, preventing them from capturing modalities such as thermal signals that are imperceptible to conventional visual sensors. Moreover, end-to-end generative policies lack explicit safety constraints, making them fragile when encountering obstacles and novel scenarios outside the training distribution. To address these limitations, we propose Safe-Night VLA, a multimodal manipulation framework that enables robots to see the unseen while enforcing rigorous safety constraints for thermal-aware manipulation in unstructured environments. Specifically, Safe-Night VLA integrates long-wave infrared thermal perception into a pre-trained vision-language backbone, enabling semantic reasoning grounded in thermodynamic properties. To ensure safe execution under out-of-distribution conditions, we incorporate a safety filter via control barrier functions, which provide deterministic workspace constraint enforcement during policy execution. We validate our framework through real-world experiments on a Franka manipulator, introducing a novel evaluation paradigm featuring temperature-conditioned manipulation, subsurface target localization, and reflection disambiguation, while maintaining constrained execution at inference time. Results demonstrate that Safe-Night VLA outperforms RGB-only baselines and provide empirical evidence that foundation models can effectively leverage non-visible physical modalities for robust manipulation.

</details>


### [18] [EmboAlign: Aligning Video Generation with Compositional Constraints for Zero-Shot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05757)
*Gehao Zhang,Zhenyang Ni,Payal Mohapatra,Han Liu,Ruohan Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VGM-VLM框架通过视觉语言模型提取任务约束，指导视频生成模型的物理合理轨迹选择与优化，实现零样本机器人操控


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型(VGMs)生成的轨迹存在物理不合理性，且通过几何重定向转换为机器人动作时会因深度估计和关键点跟踪误差而累积错误，需要一种无需训练数据的方法来改善零样本机器人操控的物理合理性和执行精度

Method: 提出VGM-VLM框架：1) 使用视觉语言模型(VLM)从语言指令中自动提取任务特定的组合约束；2) 约束引导的轨迹选择：对VGM生成的一批轨迹进行评分和筛选，保留最物理合理的候选；3) 基于约束的轨迹优化：以选定轨迹为初始化，在同一约束集下优化机器人轨迹以纠正重定向误差

Result: 在六个需要精确、约束敏感执行的真实机器人操控任务上评估，相比最强基线，总体成功率提高了43.3个百分点，且无需任何任务特定训练数据

Conclusion: VGM-VLM框架通过结合VGM的丰富动态捕捉能力和VLM的结构化空间推理能力，有效解决了VGM轨迹的物理不合理性和几何重定向误差问题，显著提升了零样本机器人操控的成功率

Abstract: Video generative models (VGMs) pretrained on large-scale internet data can produce temporally coherent rollout videos that capture rich object dynamics, offering a compelling foundation for zero-shot robotic manipulation. However, VGMs often produce physically implausible rollouts, and converting their pixel-space motion into robot actions through geometric retargeting further introduces cumulative errors from imperfect depth estimation and keypoint tracking. To address these challenges, we present \method{}, a data-free framework that aligns VGM outputs with compositional constraints generated by vision-language models (VLMs) at inference time. The key insight is that VLMs offer a capability complementary to VGMs: structured spatial reasoning that can identify the physical constraints critical to the success and safety of manipulation execution. Given a language instruction, \method{} uses a VLM to automatically extract a set of compositional constraints capturing task-specific requirements, which are then applied at two stages: (1) constraint-guided rollout selection, which scores and filters a batch of VGM rollouts to retain the most physically plausible candidate, and (2) constraint-based trajectory optimization, which uses the selected rollout as initialization and refines the robot trajectory under the same constraint set to correct retargeting errors. We evaluate \method{} on six real-robot manipulation tasks requiring precise, constraint-sensitive execution, improving the overall success rate by 43.3\% points over the strongest baseline without any task-specific training data.

</details>


### [19] [Task-Level Decisions to Gait Level Control: A Hierarchical Policy Approach for Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05783)
*Sijia Li,Haoyu Wang,Shenghai Yuan,Yizhuo Yang,Thien-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TDGC分层策略架构，通过高层决策与底层步态控制的解耦，解决四足机器人导航中尺度不匹配和环境变化不稳定的问题，实现鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界四足导航面临高层导航决策与底层步态执行之间的尺度不匹配问题，以及环境分布外变化导致的不稳定性挑战。现有方法缺乏明确的适应接口，容易在环境变化时引发摔倒。

Method: 提出TDGC分层架构：底层策略通过强化学习在仿真中训练，提供步态条件化运动，将任务需求映射到可控行为参数集；高层策略基于稀疏语义或几何地形线索做出任务中心决策，并转化为底层目标。采用结构化课程学习，通过性能驱动逐步增加环境难度和干扰范围。

Result: 实验显示在混合地形和分布外测试中获得了更高的任务成功率。架构提供明确的部署时调优、故障诊断和策略精炼接口。

Conclusion: TDGC架构通过解耦决策与步态控制，提供了可追踪的决策流程，无需密集地图或高分辨率地形重建，相比端到端方法具有更好的适应性、可调试性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world quadruped navigation is constrained by a scale mismatch between high-level navigation decisions and low-level gait execution, as well as by instabilities under out-of-distribution environmental changes. Such variations challenge sim-to-real transfer and can trigger falls when policies lack explicit interfaces for adaptation. In this paper, we present a hierarchical policy architecture for quadrupedal navigation, termed Task-level Decision to Gait Control (TDGC). A low-level policy, trained with reinforcement learning in simulation, delivers gait-conditioned locomotion and maps task requirements to a compact set of controllable behavior parameters, enabling robust mode generation and smooth switching. A high-level policy makes task-centric decisions from sparse semantic or geometric terrain cues and translates them into low-level targets, forming a traceable decision pipeline without dense maps or high-resolution terrain reconstruction. Different from end-to-end approaches, our architecture provides explicit interfaces for deployment-time tuning, fault diagnosis, and policy refinement. We introduce a structured curriculum with performance-driven progression that expands environmental difficulty and disturbance ranges. Experiments show higher task success rates on mixed terrains and out-of-distribution tests.

</details>


### [20] [CDF-Glove: A Cable-Driven Force Feedback Glove for Dexterous Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05804)
*Huayue Liang,Ruochong Li,Yaodong Yang,Long Zeng,Yuanpei Chen,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CDF-Glove是一种低成本、轻量级的电缆驱动力反馈手套，用于提高灵巧操作的遥操作演示质量，相比无反馈遥操作将任务成功率提高了4倍，成本仅约230美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的遥操作演示是灵巧操作模仿学习的主要瓶颈。触觉反馈能为操作者提供实时接触信息，实现实时手指姿势调整，从而提高演示质量。现有的灵巧遥操作平台通常缺乏触觉反馈，且体积庞大、价格昂贵。

Method: 开发了CDF-Glove，一种轻量级、低成本的电缆驱动力反馈手套。该手套实时监测20个手指自由度（其中16个直接传感，4个通过运动学约束被动耦合推断）。开发了手套的运动学模型和控制堆栈，并在多种具有不同运动学和自由度的机器人手上进行了验证。

Result: CDF-Glove实现了0.4度的远端关节重复精度，提供约200毫秒的力反馈延迟，相比无反馈遥操作将任务成功率提高了4倍。收集了两个双手遥操作数据集，基于这些数据训练的Diffusion Policy基线相比运动学教学，平均成功率提高了55%，平均完成时间减少了约15.2秒（相对减少47.2%）。

Conclusion: CDF-Glove是一种低成本、高性能的力反馈手套，显著提高了灵巧操作遥操作演示的质量，为模仿学习提供了高质量的数据集，且代码和设计已开源。

Abstract: High-quality teleoperated demonstrations are a primary bottleneck for imitation learning (IL) in dexterous manipulation. However, haptic feedback provides operators with real-time contact information, enabling real-time finger posture adjustments, and thereby improving demonstration quality. Existing dexterous teleoperation platforms typically omit haptic feedback and remain bulky and expensive. We introduce CDF-Glove, a lightweight and low cost cable-driven force-feedback glove. The real-time state is available for 20 finger degrees of freedom (DoF), of which 16 are directly sensed and 4 are passively coupled (inferred from kinematic constraints). We develop a kinematic model and control stack for the glove, and validate them across multiple robotic hands with diverse kinematics and DoF. The CDF-Glove achieves distal joint repeatability of 0.4 degrees, and delivers about 200 ms force feedback latency, yielding a 4x improvement in task success rate relative to no-feedback teleoperation. We collect two bimanual teleoperation datasets, on which we train and evaluate Diffusion Policy baselines. Compared to kinesthetic teaching, the policies trained in our teleoperated demonstrations increase the average success rate by 55% and reduce the mean completion time by approximately 15.2 seconds (a 47.2% relative reduction). In particular, the CDF-Glove costs approximately US$230. The code and designs are released as open source at https://cdfglove.github.io/.

</details>


### [21] [Hierarchical Latent Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05815)
*Hanjung Kim,Lerrel Pinto,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiLAM提出了一种分层潜在动作模型，通过建模长期时间信息来发现潜在技能，解决了现有LAMs仅关注短期帧转换而忽略长期时间结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型(LAMs)通常只关注短期帧转换，捕捉低级运动而忽略了长期时间结构。然而，无动作视频通常包含时间扩展的高级技能，需要能够建模长期依赖关系的模型。

Method: HiLAM采用分层架构，使用预训练的LAM作为低级提取器来捕获短期动态模式，然后将潜在动作序列聚合成高级潜在技能，从而建模长期时间依赖关系。

Result: 实验表明HiLAM在动态技能发现方面优于基线方法，表现出鲁棒的技能发现能力。

Conclusion: HiLAM通过分层建模长期时间信息，能够从无动作数据中发现高级潜在技能，为从动作数据学习提供了更有效的框架。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) enable learning from actionless data for applications ranging from robotic control to interactive world models. However, existing LAMs typically focus on short-horizon frame transitions and capture low-level motion while overlooking longer-term temporal structure. In contrast, actionless videos often contain temporally extended and high-level skills. We present HiLAM, a hierarchical latent action model that discovers latent skills by modeling long-term temporal information. To capture these dependencies across long horizons, we utilize a pretrained LAM as a low-level extractor. This architecture aggregates latent action sequences, which contain the underlying dynamic patterns of the video, into high-level latent skills. Our experiments demonstrate that HiLAM improves over the baseline and exhibits robust dynamic skill discovery.

</details>


### [22] [OpenHEART: Opening Heterogeneous Articulated Objects with a Legged Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05830)
*Seonghyeon Lim,Hyeonwoo Lee,Seunghyun Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于腿式机械臂操作异构铰接物体的鲁棒且样本高效的强化学习框架，包含SAFE特征提取和ArtIEst信息估计器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机械臂在操作门、抽屉、柜子等异构铰接物体时面临挑战，现有基于强化学习的方法依赖高维感官输入导致样本效率低下，需要更鲁棒且高效的解决方案

Method: 提出SAFE（基于采样的抽象特征提取）将手柄和面板几何编码为紧凑的低维表示，以及ArtIEst（铰接信息估计器）自适应混合本体感觉与外感觉来估计每个物体的开启方向和运动范围

Result: 该框架在仿真和真实机器人系统中成功部署，能够操作各种异构铰接物体，提高了跨域泛化能力和样本效率

Conclusion: 提出的SAFE和ArtIEst框架为腿式机械臂操作异构铰接物体提供了鲁棒且样本高效的解决方案，在仿真和真实环境中均验证了其有效性

Abstract: Legged manipulators offer high mobility and versatile manipulation. However, robust interaction with heterogeneous articulated objects, such as doors, drawers, and cabinets, remains challenging because of the diverse articulation types of the objects and the complex dynamics of the legged robot. Existing reinforcement learning (RL)-based approaches often rely on high-dimensional sensory inputs, leading to sample inefficiency. In this paper, we propose a robust and sample-efficient framework for opening heterogeneous articulated objects with a legged manipulator. In particular, we propose Sampling-based Abstracted Feature Extraction (SAFE), which encodes handle and panel geometry into a compact low-dimensional representation, improving cross-domain generalization. Additionally, Articulation Information Estimator (ArtIEst) is introduced to adaptively mix proprioception with exteroception to estimate opening direction and range of motion for each object. The proposed framework was deployed to manipulate various heterogeneous articulated objects in simulation and real-world robot systems. Videos can be found on the project website: https://openheart-icra.github.io/OpenHEART/

</details>


### [23] [Terrain characterization and locomotion adaptation in a small-scale lizard-inspired robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05837)
*Duncan Andrews,Landon Zimmerman,Evan Martin,Joe DiGennaro,Baxi Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了小型智能蜥蜴机器人SILA Bot，通过关节扭矩感知颗粒深度，采用线性反馈控制器实现地形自适应运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型机器人难以在真实复杂地形中部署，缺乏对机器人-地形交互的系统理解，需要解决感知和控制问题以实现有效的地形自适应运动

Method: 开发SILA Bot机器人，使用不同深度的颗粒介质作为受控地形范式；通过K最近邻分类器利用关节扭矩等本体感觉信号估计颗粒深度；设计线性反馈控制器调节身体相位

Result: 最优身体运动模式可参数化为颗粒深度的线性函数；关节扭矩感知颗粒深度准确率达95%；线性反馈控制器显著提升未知深度地形上的运动性能

Conclusion: 建立小型运动感知与控制的原则性框架，实现有效的地形自适应运动同时保持低计算复杂度

Abstract: Unlike their large-scale counterparts, small-scale robots are largely confined to laboratory environments and are rarely deployed in real-world settings. As robot size decreases, robot-terrain interactions fundamentally change; however, there remains a lack of systematic understanding of what sensory information small-scale robots should acquire and how they should respond when traversing complex natural terrains. To address these challenges, we develop a Small-scale, Intelligent, Lizard-inspired, Adaptive Robot (SILA Bot) capable of adapting to diverse substrates. We use granular media of varying depths as a controlled yet representative terrain paradigm. We show that the optimal body movement pattern (ranging from standing-wave bending that assists limb retraction on flat ground to traveling-wave undulation that generates thrust in deep granular media) can be parameterized and approximated as a linear function of granular depth. Furthermore, proprioceptive signals, such as joint torque, provide sufficient information to estimate granular depth via a K-Nearest Neighbors classifier, achieving 95% accuracy. Leveraging these relationships, we design a simple linear feedback controller that modulates body phase and substantially improves locomotion performance on terrains with unknown depth. Together, these results establish a principled framework for perception and control in small-scale locomotion and enable effective terrain-adaptive locomotion while maintaining low computational complexity.

</details>


### [24] [Expert Knowledge-driven Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Racing via Trajectory Guidance and Dynamics Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05842)
*Bo Leng,Weiqi Zhang,Zhuoren Li,Lu Xiong,Guizhe Jin,Ran Yu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TraD-RL方法，通过轨迹引导和动力学约束解决自动驾驶赛车环境中强化学习训练不稳定和不安全的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在自动驾驶领域有潜力，但在高动态、强非线性的赛车环境中存在训练不稳定和不安全动作输出的缺陷，需要改进

Method: 提出TraD-RL方法：1)利用专家赛车线构建增强状态表示和奖励塑形；2)通过控制屏障函数嵌入车辆动力学先验形成安全操作包络；3)采用多阶段课程学习策略，从专家引导过渡到自主探索

Result: 在Tempelhof机场街道赛道高保真仿真环境中评估，TraD-RL有效提高了自动驾驶赛车的圈速和驾驶稳定性，实现了赛车性能与安全的协同优化

Conclusion: TraD-RL方法通过整合领域知识和安全约束，解决了强化学习在自动驾驶赛车环境中的训练不稳定和安全问题，使学习策略能够超越专家级性能

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has demonstrated significant potential in the field of autonomous driving. However, it suffers from defects such as training instability and unsafe action outputs when faced with autonomous racing environments characterized by high dynamics and strong nonlinearities. To this end, this paper proposes a trajectory guidance and dynamics constraints Reinforcement Learning (TraD-RL) method for autonomous racing. The key features of this method are as follows: 1) leveraging the prior expert racing line to construct an augmented state representation and facilitate reward shaping, thereby integrating domain knowledge to stabilize early-stage policy learning; 2) embedding explicit vehicle dynamic priors into a safe operating envelope formulated via control barrier functions to enable safety-constrained learning; and 3) adopting a multi-stage curriculum learning strategy that shifts from expert-guided learning to autonomous exploration, allowing the learned policy to surpass expert-level performance. The proposed method is evaluated in a high-fidelity simulation environment modeled after the Tempelhof Airport Street Circuit. Experimental results demonstrate that TraD-RL effectively improves both lap speed and driving stability of the autonomous racing vehicle, achieving a synergistic optimization of racing performance and safety.

</details>


### [25] [AnyCamVLA: Zero-Shot Camera Adaptation for Viewpoint Robust Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05868)
*Hyeongjun Heo,Seungyeon Woo,Sang Min Kim,Junho Kim,Junho Lee,Yonghyeon Lee,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出零样本相机自适应框架，无需额外演示数据、策略微调或架构修改，通过虚拟调整测试时相机观测以匹配训练配置，提升视觉语言动作模型在视角变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言动作模型在机器人操作中需要针对特定环境微调，但微调后的模型对相机视角变化高度敏感，这在非结构化环境中经常发生。现有方法需要额外数据、策略微调或架构修改，缺乏高效的无监督视角适应方案。

Method: 提出零样本相机自适应框架，使用前馈式新视角合成模型实时虚拟调整测试时相机观测，使其匹配训练时的相机配置。该方法处理相机外参和内参，作为即插即用方案保持预训练模型能力，适用于任何基于RGB的策略。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，方法持续优于使用数据增强进行策略微调或添加3D感知特征的基线。在真实世界机器人操作场景中，包括不同相机外参、内参和自由移动手持相机设置，均能持续提升视角鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的零样本相机自适应框架有效解决了视觉语言动作模型对视角变化的敏感性问题，无需额外数据或模型修改，在仿真和真实环境中均展现出优越的视角鲁棒性，为机器人操作提供了实用的视角适应解决方案。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) for robot manipulation, these large pre-trained models require fine-tuning to be deployed in specific environments. These fine-tuned models are highly sensitive to camera viewpoint changes that frequently occur in unstructured environments. In this paper, we propose a zero-shot camera adaptation framework without additional demonstration data, policy fine-tuning, or architectural modification. Our key idea is to virtually adjust test-time camera observations to match the training camera configuration in real-time. For that, we use a recent feed-forward novel view synthesis model which outputs high-quality target view images, handling both extrinsic and intrinsic parameters. This plug-and-play approach preserves the pre-trained capabilities of VLAs and applies to any RGB-based policy. Through extensive experiments on the LIBERO benchmark, our method consistently outperforms baselines that use data augmentation for policy fine-tuning or additional 3D-aware features for visual input. We further validate that our approach constantly enhances viewpoint robustness in real-world robotic manipulation scenarios, including settings with varying camera extrinsics, intrinsics, and freely moving handheld cameras.

</details>


### [26] [Iterative Convex Optimization with Control Barrier Functions for Obstacle Avoidance among Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05916)
*Shuo Liu,Zhe Huang,Calin A. Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于精确多面体距离计算的迭代凸MPC-DCBF框架，用于多面体机器人在多面体障碍物环境中的实时避障控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要问题：1）使用光滑几何近似（如超球面或椭球面）会扭曲真实几何形状并限制可行集；2）将精确多面体距离集成到非线性MPC中会导致非凸优化问题，限制实时性能。

Method: 通过凸多面体间的精确最近点计算推导支撑超平面，构建线性离散时间控制屏障函数（DCBF）约束。提出迭代凸MPC-DCBF框架，通过系统动力学和机器人几何的局部线性化确保每次迭代中有限时域优化的凸性。

Result: 该框架降低了计算复杂度，实现了安全关键控制和轨迹规划的快速在线实现，可扩展到多机器人和三维环境。数值实验在杂乱迷宫场景中展示了无碰撞导航，求解时间达到毫秒级。

Conclusion: 提出的迭代凸MPC-DCBF框架有效解决了多面体机器人在多面体障碍物环境中的避障问题，通过精确距离计算和凸优化确保了实时性能，为非线性动力学系统的安全控制提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Obstacle avoidance of polytopic obstacles by polytopic robots is a challenging problem in optimization-based control and trajectory planning. Many existing methods rely on smooth geometric approximations, such as hyperspheres or ellipsoids, which allow differentiable distance expressions but distort the true geometry and restrict the feasible set. Other approaches integrate exact polytope distances into nonlinear model predictive control (MPC), resulting in nonconvex programs that limit real-time performance. In this paper, we construct linear discrete-time control barrier function (DCBF) constraints by deriving supporting hyperplanes from exact closest-point computations between convex polytopes. We then propose a novel iterative convex MPC-DCBF framework, where local linearization of system dynamics and robot geometry ensures convexity of the finite-horizon optimization at each iteration. The resulting formulation reduces computational complexity and enables fast online implementation for safety-critical control and trajectory planning of general nonlinear dynamics. The framework extends to multi-robot and three-dimensional environments. Numerical experiments demonstrate collision-free navigation in cluttered maze scenarios with millisecond-level solve times.

</details>


### [27] [PROBE: Probabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding with Analytical Translation Robustness for 3D Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05965)
*Jinseop Lee,Byoungho Lee,Gichul Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROBE是一种无需学习的LiDAR地点识别描述符，通过将BEV单元的占用建模为伯努利随机变量，并利用极坐标雅可比分析性地边缘化连续笛卡尔平移，实现距离自适应的角度不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR地点识别方法通常依赖离散点云扰动或需要大量训练数据，缺乏能够跨传感器通用且无需数据集特定调优的鲁棒描述符。PROBE旨在创建一种传感器无关的物理量表示方法，实现跨传感器泛化。

Method: PROBE将每个BEV单元的占用建模为伯努利随机变量，通过极坐标雅可比分析性地边缘化连续笛卡尔平移，产生距离自适应的角度不确定性σ_θ=σ_t/r。主要参数σ_t表示以米为单位的预期平移不确定性，这是传感器无关的物理量。相似性计算结合了伯努利-KL Jaccard距离、指数不确定性门控和基于FFT的高度余弦相似度进行旋转对齐。

Result: 在涵盖四种不同LiDAR类型的四个数据集上评估，PROBE在多会话评估中实现了手工描述符中最高的准确率，在单会话性能方面与手工和基于监督的基线方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PROBE作为一种无需学习的LiDAR地点识别描述符，通过传感器无关的物理量表示和连续平移的边缘化处理，实现了跨传感器泛化和鲁棒的性能表现，为实际应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present PROBE (PRobabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding), a learning-free LiDAR place recognition descriptor that models each BEV cell's occupancy as a Bernoulli random variable. Rather than relying on discrete point-cloud perturbations, PROBE analytically marginalizes over continuous Cartesian translations via the polar Jacobian, yielding a distance-adaptive angular uncertainty $σ_θ= σ_t / r$ in $\mathcal{O}(R \times S)$ time. The primary parameter $σ_t$ represents the expected translational uncertainty in meters, a sensor-independent physical quantity allowing cross-sensor generalization without per-dataset tuning. Pairwise similarity combines a Bernoulli-KL Jaccard with exponential uncertainty gating and FFT-based height cosine similarity for rotation alignment. Evaluated on four datasets spanning four diverse LiDAR types, PROBE achieves the highest accuracy among handcrafted descriptors in multi-session evaluation and competitive single-session performance against both handcrafted and supervised baselines. The source code and supplementary materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/probe-pr.

</details>


### [28] [Proprioceptive Shape Estimation of Tensegrity Manipulators Using Energy Minimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05976)
*Tufail Ahmad Bhat,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 仅使用各杆件相对于重力的倾角信息，实现了连续弯曲张拉整体机械臂的完整形状估计，精度达到总长度的2.1%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续弯曲张拉整体机械臂的形状估计是其控制的基础，但现有方法存在局限性。外感受传感器方法成本高且环境受限，而本体感受方法虽无这些限制，但尚未有大规模张拉整体结构作为机械臂的成功应用实例。

Method: 提出仅使用各杆件相对于重力的倾角信息来估计整个张拉整体机械臂形状的方法。倾角信息是可通过在每个杆件上安装惯性测量单元(IMU)获得的固有感知数据。

Result: 在具有20个杆件、总长1160毫米的五层张拉整体机械臂上进行实验，结果表明：该方法能从任意初始条件在静态条件下以总机械臂长度2.1%的精度估计形状，并在外部干扰下保持稳定的形状估计。

Conclusion: 证明了仅使用杆件倾角信息即可实现大规模张拉整体机械臂的完整形状估计，为低成本、环境适应性强的张拉整体机械臂控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Shape estimation is fundamental for controlling continuously bending tensegrity manipulators, yet achieving it remains a challenge. Although using exteroceptive sensors makes the implementation straightforward, it is costly and limited to specific environments. Proprioceptive approaches, by contrast, do not suffer from these limitations. So far, several methods have been proposed; however, to our knowledge, there are no proven examples of large-scale tensegrity structures used as manipulators. This paper demonstrates that shape estimation of the entire tensegrity manipulator can be achieved using only the inclination angle information relative to gravity for each strut. Inclination angle information is intrinsic sensory data that can be obtained simply by attaching an inertial measurement unit (IMU) to each strut. Experiments conducted on a five-layer tensegrity manipulator with 20 struts and a total length of 1160 mm demonstrate that the proposed method can estimate the shape with an accuracy of 2.1 \% of the total manipulator length, from arbitrary initial conditions under both static conditions and maintains stable shape estimation under external disturbances.

</details>


### [29] [HarvestFlex: Strawberry Harvesting via Vision-Language-Action Policy Adaptation in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05982)
*Ziyang Zhao,Shuheng Wang,Zhonghua Miao,Ya Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个将视觉-语言-动作策略迁移到真实温室桌面草莓采摘的研究，使用三视角RGB传感构建端到端闭环系统，通过VR遥操作演示微调模型，在50次真实温室试验中达到74%成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决温室草莓采摘这一长期、非结构化任务的挑战，包括遮挡和镜面反射问题，探索在避免深度云和显式几何标定的情况下，使用少量真实数据实现闭环采摘的可能性

Method: 在HarvestFlex平台上构建端到端闭环系统，采用三视角RGB传感（两个固定场景视角加一个腕部视角），收集3.71小时VR遥操作演示数据（227个片段），对pi_0、pi_0.5和WALL-OSS模型进行全微调和LoRA微调

Result: 在统一的50次真实温室试验协议下，pi_0.5全微调模型达到74.0%成功率、32.6秒/采摘和4.1%损伤率；异步推理-控制解耦相比同步部署进一步提升了性能

Conclusion: 研究表明仅用不到四小时的真实数据即可实现非平凡的闭环采摘，但仍受限于近距离可观测性损失和接触动力学不匹配问题，为VLA策略在农业机器人应用提供了实证

Abstract: This work presents the first study on transferring vision-language-action (VLA) policies to real greenhouse tabletop strawberry harvesting, a long-horizon, unstructured task challenged by occlusion and specular reflections. We built an end-to-end closed-loop system on the HarvestFlex platform using three-view RGB sensing (two fixed scene views plus a wrist-mounted view) and intentionally avoided depth clouds and explicit geometric calibration. We collected 3.71 h of VR teleoperated demonstrations (227 episodes) and fine-tuned pi_0, pi_0.5, and WALL-OSS with full fine-tuning and LoRA. Under a unified 50 trials real-greenhouse protocol and metrics spanning completion, pi_0.5 with full fine-tuning achieved success rate of 74.0% with 32.6 s/pick and damage rate of 4.1%. Asynchronous inference-control decoupling further improved performance over synchronous deployment. Results showed non-trivial closed-loop picking with fewer than four hours of real data, while remaining limited by close-range observability loss and contact-dynamics mismatch. A demonstration video is available at: https://youtu.be/bN8ZowZKPMI.

</details>


### [30] [MagRobot:An Open Simulator for Magnetically Navigated Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05992)
*Heng Wang,Haoyu Song,Jiatao Zheng,Yuxiang Han,Kunli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个通用开源仿真平台，用于磁导航机器人的研究、设计和基准测试，支持刚性/软体机器人，涵盖磁驱动和磁跟踪任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁导航系统在微创医学中具有巨大潜力，但机器人设计严重依赖实验原型，耗时耗力，且缺乏一致的实验环境来比较不同系统的硬件和算法。

Method: 开发了首个通用开源仿真平台，具有直观的图形用户界面，可模拟刚性和软体机器人的磁导航系统，支持第三方解剖模型加载，硬件和算法可定制化。

Result: 通过体模和离体实验验证了仿真器的保真度，实现了支气管镜、血管内介入和胃肠内镜三个应用案例，证明可灵活设计和优化磁导航系统的配置和算法。

Conclusion: 该仿真平台为磁导航机器人的研究、设计和基准测试提供了有效的工具，能够显著减少对实验原型的依赖，促进不同系统间的比较和优化。

Abstract: Magnetic navigation systems, including magnetic tracking systems and magnetic actuation systems, have shown great potential for occlusion-free localization and remote control of intracorporeal medical devices and robots in minimally invasive medicine, such as capsule endoscopy and cardiovascular intervention. However, the design of magnetically navigated robots remains heavily reliant on experimental prototyping, which is time-consuming and costly. Furthermore, there is a lack of a consistent experimental environment to compare and benchmark the hardware and algorithms across different magnetic navigation systems. To address these challenges, we propose the first universal open-source simulation platform to facilitate research, design and benchmarking of magnetically navigated robots. Our simulator features an intuitive graphical user interface that enables the user to efficiently design, visualize, and analyze magnetic navigation systems for both rigid and soft robots. The proposed simulator is versatile, which can simulate both magnetic actuation and magnetic tracking tasks in diverse medical applications that involve deformable anatomies. The proposed simulator provides an open development environment, where the user can load third-party anatomical models and customize both hardware and algorithms of magnetic navigation systems. The fidelity of the simulator is validated using both phantom and ex vivo experiments of magnetic navigation of a continuum robot and a capsule robot with diverse magnetic actuation setups. Three use cases of the simulator, i.e., bronchoscopy, endovascular intervention, and gastrointestinal endoscopy, are implemented to demonstrate the functionality of the simulator. It is shown that the configuration and algorithms of magnetic navigation systems can be flexibly designed and optimized for better performance using the simulator.

</details>


### [31] [Moving Through Clutter: Scaling Data Collection and Benchmarking for 3D Scene-Aware Humanoid Locomotion via Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05993)
*Beichen Wang,Yuanjie Lu,Linji Wang,Liuchuan Yu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Moving Through Clutter (MTC)框架，用于在杂乱3D环境中收集场景感知的人形机器人运动数据，填补了该领域公开数据集的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人运动研究主要在开放平坦环境中展示动态行为，但现实世界环境（如家庭、办公室）是杂乱、三维且几何受限的，需要场景感知的全身协调、精确平衡控制和空间约束推理。然而，杂乱3D环境中的人形机器人运动研究不足，且缺乏将全身人体运动与场景几何系统耦合的公开数据集。

Method: 开发了基于虚拟现实(VR)的数据收集和评估框架MTC，通过程序化生成可控杂乱程度的场景，通过沉浸式VR导航捕获具身一致的全身人体运动，并自动重定向到人形机器人模型。同时引入了量化环境杂乱程度和运动性能（包括稳定性和碰撞安全性）的基准测试。

Result: 使用该框架编译了包含145个多样化3D杂乱场景中348条轨迹的数据集。该数据集为研究人形机器人运动中几何诱导的适应性以及开发场景感知的规划和控制方法提供了基础。

Conclusion: MTC框架填补了杂乱3D环境中人形机器人运动数据集的空白，为研究场景感知的全身运动协调和开发更适应现实世界环境的机器人控制方法提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid locomotion have enabled dynamic behaviors such as dancing, martial arts, and parkour, yet these capabilities are predominantly demonstrated in open, flat, and obstacle-free settings. In contrast, real-world environments such as homes, offices, and public spaces, are densely cluttered, three-dimensional, and geometrically constrained, requiring scene-aware whole-body coordination, precise balance control, and reasoning over spatial constraints imposed by furniture and household objects. However, humanoid locomotion in cluttered 3D environments remains underexplored, and no public dataset systematically couples full-body human locomotion with the scene geometry that shapes it. To address this gap, we present Moving Through Clutter (MTC), an opensource Virtual Reality (VR) based data collection and evaluation framework for scene-aware humanoid locomotion in cluttered environments. Our system procedurally generates scenes with controllable clutter levels and captures embodiment-consistent, whole-body human motion through immersive VR navigation, which is then automatically retargeted to a humanoid robot model. We further introduce benchmarks that quantify environment clutter level and locomotion performance, including stability and collision safety. Using this framework, we compile a dataset of 348 trajectories across 145 diverse 3D cluttered scenes. The dataset provides a foundation for studying geometry-induced adaptation in humanoid locomotion and developing scene-aware planning and control methods.

</details>


### [32] [TADPO: Reinforcement Learning Goes Off-road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05995)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Raymond Song,Vedant Mundheda,Luis E. Navarro-Serment,Christof Schoenborn,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TADPO：一种基于PPO的新型策略梯度方法，通过离策略轨迹提供教师指导、在线策略轨迹进行学生探索，实现了端到端视觉强化学习在高速越野驾驶中的应用，并首次在真实全尺寸越野车上实现了零样本仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野自动驾驶面临未测绘地形、多变动态环境等挑战，需要有效的长时程规划和自适应控制。强化学习虽然能直接从交互中学习控制策略，但由于越野驾驶是长时程任务且奖励信号稀疏，标准RL方法难以应用。

Method: 提出TADPO（Teacher-Assisted Distilled PPO）方法，扩展了PPO算法，利用离策略轨迹提供教师指导，在线策略轨迹进行学生探索。在此基础上开发了基于视觉的端到端RL系统，用于高速越野驾驶。

Result: 在仿真中展示了高性能，更重要的是在真实全尺寸越野车上实现了零样本的仿真到现实迁移。据作者所知，这是首次在真实全尺寸越野平台上部署基于RL的策略。

Conclusion: TADPO方法成功解决了越野驾驶中长时程、稀疏奖励的RL训练难题，实现了端到端视觉控制，并通过零样本迁移证明了其在实际越野场景中的有效性，为自动驾驶领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Off-road autonomous driving poses significant challenges such as navigating unmapped, variable terrain with uncertain and diverse dynamics. Addressing these challenges requires effective long-horizon planning and adaptable control. Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a promising solution by learning control policies directly from interaction. However, because off-road driving is a long-horizon task with low-signal rewards, standard RL methods are challenging to apply in this setting. We introduce TADPO, a novel policy gradient formulation that extends Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), leveraging off-policy trajectories for teacher guidance and on-policy trajectories for student exploration. Building on this, we develop a vision-based, end-to-end RL system for high-speed off-road driving, capable of navigating extreme slopes and obstacle-rich terrain. We demonstrate our performance in simulation and, importantly, zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a full-scale off-road vehicle. To our knowledge, this work represents the first deployment of RL-based policies on a full-scale off-road platform.

</details>


### [33] [Restoring Linguistic Grounding in VLA Models via Train-Free Attention Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06001)
*Ninghao Zhang,Bin Zhu,Shijie Zhou,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型存在"语言盲视"问题：在语言指令与场景矛盾时仍执行视觉合理的动作。研究提出ICBench基准和IGAR方法，无需训练即可缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型作为通用机器人策略基础备受关注，但其在分布外指令下的可靠性尚未充分探索。研究发现VLA模型存在"语言盲视"现象，即当语言指令与视觉场景矛盾时，模型仍会执行视觉合理的动作，这揭示了模型在动作生成过程中过度依赖视觉先验而忽视指令语义的问题。

Method: 1) 提出ICBench诊断基准，基于LIBERO数据集构建，通过注入受控的分布外指令矛盾来探测语言-动作耦合；2) 提出指令引导注意力重校准(IGAR)，一种无需训练的推理时机制，通过重新平衡注意力分布来恢复语言指令的影响，可直接应用于现有VLA模型而无需重新训练或架构修改。

Result: 评估了三种代表性VLA架构(Pi0、Pi0.5和OpenVLA OFT)，发现这些模型在逻辑上不可能的指令下仍频繁成功完成任务，揭示了动作生成中的强烈视觉偏差。在30个LIBERO任务上的实验表明，IGAR显著减少了在分布外矛盾指令下的错误执行，同时保持了基线任务性能。在真实Franka机械臂上的验证显示，IGAR能有效防止由不一致指令触发的错误操作。

Conclusion: VLA模型存在语言盲视问题，即过度依赖视觉先验而忽视指令语义。提出的ICBench基准能系统诊断此问题，而IGAR方法无需训练即可有效缓解语言盲视，提高VLA模型在分布外指令下的可靠性，为更安全的机器人部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to perform manipulation tasks directly from natural language instructions and are increasingly viewed as a foundation for generalist robotic policies. However, their reliability under Out-of-Distribution (OOD) instructions remains underexplored. In this paper, we reveal a critical failure mode in which VLA policies continue executing visually plausible actions even when the language instruction contradicts the scene. We refer to this phenomenon as linguistic blindness, where VLA policies prioritize visual priors over instruction semantics during action generation. To systematically analyze this issue, we introduce ICBench, a diagnostic benchmark constructed from the LIBERO dataset that probes language-action coupling by injecting controlled OOD instruction contradictions while keeping the visual environment unchanged. Evaluations on three representative VLA architectures, including Pi0, Pi0.5 and OpenVLA OFT, show that these models frequently succeed at tasks despite logically impossible instructions, revealing a strong visual bias in action generation. To mitigate this issue, we propose Instruction-Guided Attention Recalibration (IGAR), a train-free inference-time mechanism that rebalances attention distributions to restore the influence of language instructions. IGAR operates without retraining or architectural modification and can be directly applied to existing VLA models. Experiments across 30 LIBERO tasks demonstrate that IGAR substantially reduces erroneous execution under OOD contradictory instructions while preserving baseline task performance. We additionally validate the approach on a real Franka robotic arm, where IGAR effectively prevents manipulation triggered by inconsistent instructions.

</details>


### [34] [RODEO: RObotic DEcentralized Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06058)
*Milan Groshev,Eduardo Castelló Ferrer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RODEO框架：基于区块链的机器人去中心化自治组织，通过ROS-ETH桥接、组织模板和验证机制实现机器人服务的可信、可审计和自主经济运作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人自主性提升但缺乏可审计的行动、透明的决策过程和新的人机交互模型，需要建立信任和问责机制以实现扩展的机器人自主性

Method: 提出RODEO框架，形式化服务机器人的去中心化自治组织（DAOs）：1）建立DAO与机器人间的ROS-ETH桥接；2）提供组织集成模板；3）实现机器人行动的可审计证明验证机制

Result: 在实验室垃圾收集场景中，机器人收入翻倍并通过再投资延长运行时间；证明验证时间约1分钟，机器人收入成功资助了未来88小时的自主运行

Conclusion: 该研究展示了机器人与组织如何通过可审计执行证明和链上结算协调任务和支付，为可信机器人自主性提供了可行方案

Abstract: Robots are improving their autonomy with minimal human supervision. However, auditable actions, transparent decision processes, and new human-robot interaction models are still missing requirements to achieve extended robot autonomy. To tackle these challenges, we propose RODEO (RObotic DEcentralized Organization), a blockchain-based framework that integrates trust and accountability mechanisms for robots. This paper formalizes Decentralized Autonomous Organizations (DAOs) for service robots. First, it provides a ROS-ETH bridge between the DAO and the robots. Second, it offers templates that enable organizations (e.g., companies, universities) to integrate service robots into their operations. Third, it provides proof-verification mechanisms that allow robot actions to be auditable. In our experimental setup, a mobile robot was deployed as a trash collector in a lab scenario. The robot collects trash and uses a smart bin to sort and dispose of it correctly. Then, the robot submits a proof of the successful operation and is compensated in DAO tokens. Finally, the robot re-invests the acquired funds to purchase battery charging services. Data collected in a three day experiment show that the robot doubled its income and reinvested funds to extend its operating time. The proof validation times of approximately one minute ensured verifiable task execution, while the accumulated robot income successfully funded up to 88 hours of future autonomous operation. The results of this research give insights about how robots and organizations can coordinate tasks and payments with auditable execution proofs and on-chain settlement.

</details>


### [35] [Multimodal Behavior Tree Generation: A Small Vision-Language Model for Robot Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06084)
*Cristiano Battistini,Riccardo Andrea Izzo,Gianluca Bardaro,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用开源多模态模型生成机器人任务规划的行为树，通过构建视觉-指令-行为树数据集并微调视觉语言模型，实现高效的行为树生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合语言模型的任务规划能力和视觉语言模型的视觉理解能力，解决现有缺乏连接视觉观察、指令与可执行行为树的数据集问题，为机器人任务规划提供更高效的解决方案

Method: 1) 从现有机器人数据集构建视觉-指令-行为树数据集；2) 使用大模型作为教师进行多阶段生成；3) 通过参数高效微调(PEFT)微调500M到4B参数的视觉语言模型；4) 生成与BehaviorTree.CPP兼容的行为树

Result: 微调后的4B参数视觉语言模型接近最先进闭源模型性能，在家庭任务执行中达到87%成功率，同时仅需少量计算资源

Conclusion: 该方法成功构建了视觉-指令-行为树数据集，通过微调开源多模态模型实现了高效的行为树生成，为机器人任务规划提供了计算效率高且性能优异的解决方案

Abstract: Large and small language models have been widely used for robotic task planning. At the same time, vision-language models (VLMs) have successfully tackled problems such as image captioning, scene understanding, and visual question answering. In this work, we combine these two approaches by deploying a compact, open-source multimodal model to generate behavior trees for robotic task planning. The main obstacle to achieving this goal is the lack of an existing dataset that links visual observations and instructions to executable behavior trees. We propose a method to construct such a dataset starting from existing robotic episodes (i.e., Open X-Embodiment), in which a large model serves as a teacher in a multi-stage generation pipeline. We use this dataset to fine-tune VLMs ranging from 500M to 4B parameters via parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). The generated behavior trees, compatible with the BehaviorTree.CPP library, are evaluated both offline, using structural and lexical metrics, and online through the execution of household tasks in a state-of-the-art embodied simulator. Our results demonstrate that our fine-tuned 4B-parameter VLM approaches the performance of state-of-the-art closed-source models, achieving an 87\% success rate while requiring only a fraction of the computational resources.

</details>


### [36] [Sticky-Glance: Robust Intent Recognition for Human Robot Collaboration via Single-Glance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06121)
*Yuzhi Lai,Shenghai Yuan,Peizheng Li,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对象中心化的注视点接地框架，通过粘性注视算法稳定意图识别，在动态多目标环境中实现高精度注视意图识别和连续共享控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为运动能力严重受限的残障人士提供可靠的注视通信方式，解决多目标环境中注视噪声、微眼跳、视角变化和动态对象带来的意图识别挑战

Method: 采用对象中心化的注视点接地框架，结合粘性注视算法联合建模几何距离和方向趋势，实现意图稳定锚定；引入连续共享控制和多模态交互范式

Result: 动态目标跟踪率达到0.94，静态目标选择准确率达到0.98；任务持续时间减少近10%；在动态跟踪、多视角对齐、用户研究和消融实验中均表现出优于基准方法的鲁棒性、效率和低工作负荷

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够有效稳定注视意图识别，即使在仅有3个注视样本的短暂注视下也能保持意图锚定，为残障人士提供了高效可靠的注视交互解决方案

Abstract: Gaze is a valuable means of communication for impaired people with extremely limited motor capabilities. However, robust gaze-based intent recognition in multi-object environments is challenging due to gaze noise, micro-saccades, viewpoint changes, and dynamic objects. To address this, we propose an object-centric gaze grounding framework that stabilizes intent through a sticky-glance algorithm, jointly modeling geometric distance and direction trends. The inferred intent remains anchored to the object even under short glances with minimal 3 gaze samples, achieving a tracking rate of 0.94 for dynamic targets and selection accuracy of 0.98 for static targets. We further introduce a continuous shared control and multi-modal interaction paradigm, enabling high-readiness control and human-in-loop feedback, thereby reducing task duration for nearly 10 \%. Experiments across dynamic tracking, multi-perspective alignment, a baseline comparison, user studies, and ablation studies demonstrate improved robustness, efficiency, and reduced workload compared to representative baselines.

</details>

Main category: cs.RO
