<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定频率控制在复杂逆运动学任务中容易产生轨迹振荡，需要一种能够适应患者认知状态、优化人机协同的安全高效康复控制策略。

Method: 1) 事件驱动控制架构：仅当末端执行器进入目标路点许可球时触发控制动作；2) DAMMRL框架：人类智能体选择许可球半径（速度-精度权衡），机器人智能体动态调整3D笛卡尔步长；3) 半虚拟验证：物理压力传感器连接MuJoCo仿真。

Result: 有效抑制路点抖动，平衡空间精度与时间效率，显著提高物体抓取任务成功率，在混合环境中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 事件触发的DAMMRL方法为复杂上肢康复任务提供了一种安全、自适应的人机协同控制策略，通过分解空间轴和优化控制参数实现了高效康复训练。

Abstract: This paper presents a shared-control rehabilitation policy for a custom 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) upper-limb robot that decomposes complex reaching tasks into decoupled spatial axes. The patient governs the primary reaching direction using binary commands, while the robot autonomously manages orthogonal corrective motions. Because traditional fixed-frequency control often induces trajectory oscillations due to variable inverse-kinematics execution times, an event-driven progression strategy is proposed. This architecture triggers subsequent control actions only when the end-effector enters an admission sphere centred on the immediate target waypoint, and was validated in a semi-virtual setup linking a physical pressure sensor to a MuJoCo simulation. To optimise human--robot co-adaptation safely and efficiently, this study introduces Dual Agent Multiple Model Reinforcement Learning (DAMMRL). This framework discretises decision characteristics: the human agent selects the admission sphere radius to reflect their inherent speed--accuracy trade-off, while the robot agent dynamically adjusts its 3D Cartesian step magnitudes to complement the user's cognitive state. Trained in simulation and deployed across mixed environments, this event-triggered DAMMRL approach effectively suppresses waypoint chatter, balances spatial precision with temporal efficiency, and significantly improves success rates in object acquisition tasks.

</details>


### [39] [DreamToNav: Generalizable Navigation for Robots via Generative Video Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06190)
*Valerii Serpiva,Jeffrin Sam,Chidera Simon,Hajira Amjad,Iana Zhura,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamToNav：基于生成视频模型的机器人自主导航框架，通过自然语言指令生成物理一致的视频序列，从中提取可执行运动轨迹


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于路径点的导航方式不够直观且需要专门工程化，需要一种更自然的人机交互方式，让用户能够通过自然语言指令控制机器人执行复杂任务

Method: 1) 使用Qwen 2.5-VL-7B-Instruct将模糊的自然语言指令精炼为精确的视觉描述；2) 用NVIDIA Cosmos 2.5视频基础模型生成机器人执行任务的物理一致视频序列；3) 从合成视频中通过视觉姿态估计、机器人检测和轨迹恢复提取有效运动学路径

Result: 在轮式移动机器人和四足机器人的室内导航任务中，DreamToNav实现了76.7%的成功率，最终目标误差在0.05-0.10米之间，轨迹跟踪误差低于0.15米

Conclusion: 将视频生成作为规划引擎，DreamToNav为机器人提供了一种统一的障碍物避让和目标导向导航框架，无需任务特定工程，证明了从生成视频预测中提取的轨迹可以在不同运动平台上可靠执行

Abstract: We present DreamToNav, a novel autonomous robot framework that uses generative video models to enable intuitive, human-in-the-loop control. Instead of relying on rigid waypoint navigation, users provide natural language prompts (e.g. ``Follow the person carefully''), which the system translates into executable motion. Our pipeline first employs Qwen 2.5-VL-7B-Instruct to refine vague user instructions into precise visual descriptions. These descriptions condition NVIDIA Cosmos 2.5, a state-of-the-art video foundation model, to synthesize a physically consistent video sequence of the robot performing the task. From this synthetic video, we extract a valid kinematic path using visual pose estimation, robot detection and trajectory recovery. By treating video generation as a planning engine, DreamToNav allows robots to visually "dream" complex behaviors before executing them, providing a unified framework for obstacle avoidance and goal-directed navigation without task-specific engineering. We evaluate the approach on both a wheeled mobile robot and a quadruped robot in indoor navigation tasks. DreamToNav achieves a success rate of 76.7%, with final goal errors typically within 0.05-0.10 m and trajectory tracking errors below 0.15 m. These results demonstrate that trajectories extracted from generative video predictions can be reliably executed on physical robots across different locomotion platforms.

</details>


### [40] [Towards Robotic Lake Maintenance: Integrating SONAR and Satellite Data to Assist Human Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06266)
*Ahmed H. Elsayed,Christoph Manss,Tarek A. El-Mihoub,Andrej Lejman,Frederic Stahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法，结合卫星遥感和无人水面船声纳技术，用于人工水体中水下植被的监测和针对性收割管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工水体需要持续监测和维护以管理其生态系统，其中水下植被生长迅速，必须进行收割以维持生态平衡。传统方法劳动强度大，需要更有效的监测和收割支持系统。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段使用卫星遥感数据（特别是水生植物和藻类指数APA）进行初步检测，识别感兴趣区域；第二阶段使用配备多波束声纳的无人水面船进行高分辨率水深测量，精确定位和量化水生植被。

Result: 初步结果表明，整合卫星影像和水下声学传感来改善人工湖泊植被管理是可行的。该方法实现了有效的人机协作，卫星数据指导无人水面船任务，船工利用详细的声纳地图进行针对性收割。

Conclusion: 这种两阶段方法缩小了搜索空间，减少了人工操作者的工作量，使收割过程对操作者来说劳动强度更低，为人工水体的植被管理提供了一种有效的监测和收割支持系统。

Abstract: Artificial Water Bodies (AWBs) are human-made systems that require continuous monitoring due to their artificial biological processes. These systems demand regular maintenance to manage their ecosystems effectively. As a result of these artificial conditions, underwater vegetation can grow rapidly and must be harvested to preserve the ecological balance. This paper proposes a two-step approach to support targeted weed harvesting for the maintenance of artificial lakes. The first step is the initial detection of Submerged Aquatic Vegetation (SAV), also referred to in this paper as areas of interest, is performed using satellite-derived indices, specifically the Aquatic Plants and Algae (APA) index, which highlights submerged vegetation in water bodies. Subsequently, an Unmanned Surface Vehicle (USV) equipped with multibeam SOund NAvigation and Ranging (SONAR) performs high-resolution bathymetric mapping to locate and quantify aquatic vegetation precisely. This two-stage approach offers an effective human-robot collaboration, where satellite data guides the USV missions and boat skippers leverage detailed SONAR maps for targeted harvesting. This setup narrows the search space and reduces manual workload from human operators, making the harvesting process less labour-intensive for operators. Preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of integrating satellite imagery and underwater acoustic sensing to improve vegetation management in artificial lakes.

</details>


### [41] [SuperSuit: An Isomorphic Bimodal Interface for Scalable Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06280)
*Tongqing Chen,Hang Wu,Jiasen Wang,Xiaotao Li,Zhu Jin,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperSuit是一个双模态数据采集框架，支持机器人遥操作和主动演示，通过共享运动学接口生成结构相同的关节空间轨迹，解决了移动机械臂长时程演示数据获取的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取高质量、长时程的演示数据对于具身AI至关重要，但对于紧密耦合的轮式移动机械臂来说，数据获取仍然是一个基本瓶颈。与固定基座系统不同，移动机械臂需要在SE(2)平面运动和精确操作之间进行连续协调，而现有的遥操作和可穿戴接口在这方面存在局限性。

Method: SuperSuit是一个双模态数据采集框架，支持机器人遥操作和主动演示两种模式，共享相同的运动学接口。两种模式都生成结构相同的关节空间轨迹，可以直接混合数据而无需修改下游策略。对于移动，它将自然的人类步进映射到连续的平面基座速度；对于操作，它使用严格同构的可穿戴臂；策略训练采用平移不变的delta关节表示来减轻校准偏移和结构柔度。

Result: 真实世界实验显示：主动模式下的演示吞吐量比遥操作基线高2.6倍；在固定数据集大小下，用主动演示数据替代遥操作数据可获得可比的策略性能；随着主动数据量的增加，性能呈现单调提升。

Conclusion: 跨采集模态的一致运动学表示能够实现长时程移动操作的可扩展数据采集。SuperSuit框架通过共享接口解决了移动机械臂演示数据获取的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: High-quality, long-horizon demonstrations are essential for embodied AI, yet acquiring such data for tightly coupled wheeled mobile manipulators remains a fundamental bottleneck. Unlike fixed-base systems, mobile manipulators require continuous coordination between $SE(2)$ locomotion and precise manipulation, exposing limitations in existing teleoperation and wearable interfaces. We present \textbf{SuperSuit}, a bimodal data acquisition framework that supports both robot-in-the-loop teleoperation and active demonstration under a shared kinematic interface. Both modalities produce structurally identical joint-space trajectories, enabling direct data mixing without modifying downstream policies. For locomotion, SuperSuit maps natural human stepping to continuous planar base velocities, eliminating discrete command switches. For manipulation, it employs a strictly isomorphic wearable arm in both modes, while policy training is formulated in a shift-invariant delta-joint representation to mitigate calibration offsets and structural compliance without inverse kinematics. Real-world experiments on long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks show 2.6$\times$ higher demonstration throughput in active mode compared to a teleoperation baseline, comparable policy performance when substituting teleoperation data with active demonstrations at fixed dataset size, and monotonic performance improvement as active data volume increases. These results indicate that consistent kinematic representations across collection modalities enable scalable data acquisition for long-horizon mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [42] [Safe Consensus of Cooperative Manipulation with Hierarchical Event-Triggered Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06356)
*Simiao Zhuang,Bingkun Huang,Zewen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分布式控制框架通过分层事件触发控制屏障函数实现多机械臂协同运输的安全共识协调，显著降低计算和通信开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机械臂协同运输重型或大型负载需要在变化环境中实现协调编队跟踪，同时满足严格安全约束，且面临有限通信和实时计算预算的挑战

Method: 提出分布式控制框架：1) 基于局部邻居信息的共识协议确保任务空间平移和旋转一致性；2) 三层分层事件触发安全架构与控制屏障函数集成；3) 风险感知领导者选择和平滑切换策略减少在线计算

Result: 通过两台Franka机械臂硬件实验（静态障碍物）和可扩展多臂协同仿真（动态障碍物）验证，在严格安全约束下实现更高精度协同，相比基线方法显著降低计算成本和通信频率

Conclusion: 所提分层事件触发CBF框架为多机械臂协同运输提供了安全、高效、可扩展的解决方案，在保证安全性的同时大幅减少计算和通信资源需求

Abstract: Cooperative transport and manipulation of heavy or bulky payloads by multiple manipulators requires coordinated formation tracking, while simultaneously enforcing strict safety constraints in varying environments with limited communication and real-time computation budgets. This paper presents a distributed control framework that achieves consensus coordination with safety guarantees via hierarchical event-triggered control barrier functions (CBFs). We first develop a consensus-based protocol that relies solely on local neighbor information to enforce both translational and rotational consistency in task space. Building on this coordination layer, we propose a three-level hierarchical event-triggered safety architecture with CBFs, which is integrated with a risk-aware leader selection and smooth switching strategy to reduce online computation. The proposed approach is validated through real-world hardware experiments using two Franka manipulators operating with static obstacles, as well as comprehensive simulations demonstrating scalable multi-arm cooperation with dynamic obstacles. Results demonstrate higher precision cooperation under strict safety constraints, achieving substantially reduced computational cost and communication frequency compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [43] [Data Analogies Enable Efficient Cross-Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06450)
*Jonathan Yang,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了如何组织异构机器人演示数据以优化跨机器人设置的迁移性能，发现不同形态转移需要数据类比而非简单扩展数据规模


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解如何最有效地组织和扩展异构机器人演示数据，以真正提升在特定目标设置下的性能。虽然通用机器人策略在多种机器人、场景和视角的演示数据上训练，但如何组织这些异构数据以最大化跨机器人设置的迁移效果仍不明确。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）进行受控实验，改变末端执行器形态、机器人平台外观和相机视角；2）比较简单增加演示数量与系统性地以不同方式扩展多样性的效果；3）通过模拟实验分析不同转移类型对数据多样性的响应；4）基于模拟结果改进真实世界的跨具身转移。

Result: 模拟实验结果显示：1）感知转移（如视角变化）从广泛的多样性中获益最大；2）形态转移从非结构化多样性中获益有限，而是从数据类比中获得最大增益，即跨不同具身的场景、任务和/或轨迹对齐的配对演示。基于这些发现，仅通过改变数据组成，在真实世界中将跨具身转移成功率平均提高了22.5%，优于大规模非配对数据集。

Conclusion: 结论表明，对于跨机器人设置的迁移，数据组成比简单扩展数据规模更为关键。不同转移类型需要不同的数据策略：感知转移受益于广泛多样性，而形态转移需要结构化的数据类比。这一发现为优化机器人演示数据集提供了指导，能够更有效地实现跨具身知识迁移。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies are trained on demonstrations collected across a wide variety of robots, scenes, and viewpoints. Yet it remains unclear how to best organize and scale such heterogeneous data so that it genuinely improves performance in a given target setting. In this work, we ask: what form of demonstration data is most useful for enabling transfer across robot set-ups? We conduct controlled experiments that vary end-effector morphology, robot platform appearance, and camera perspective, and compare the effects of simply scaling the number of demonstrations against systematically broadening the diversity in different ways. Our simulated experiments show that while perceptual shifts such as viewpoint benefit most from broad diversity, morphology shifts benefit far less from unstructured diversity and instead see the largest gains from data analogies, i.e. paired demonstrations that align scenes, tasks, and/or trajectories across different embodiments. Informed by the simulation results, we improve real-world cross-embodiment transfer success by an average of $22.5\%$ over large-scale, unpaired datasets by changing only the composition of the data.

</details>


### [44] [History-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06480)
*Qitong Wang,Yijun Liang,Ming Li,Tianyi Zhou,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的时空视觉token剪枝框架，用于视觉-语言-动作模型在视觉语言导航任务中，通过空间token选择和时空压缩显著降低计算延迟，同时保持导航精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型在视觉语言导航中表现出色，但高计算成本导致延迟，限制了实时部署。需要在不重新训练或修改预训练模型的情况下，减少冗余计算，实现高效的长时程推理。

Method: 提出训练免费的时空视觉token剪枝框架：1) 对当前视图应用空间token选择；2) 对历史记忆进行时空压缩；3) 利用基于注意力的token重要性和查询引导的时空过滤，保留导航相关信息；4) 无需重新训练，可即插即用集成到现有VLA系统。

Result: 在标准VLN基准测试中显著优于现有剪枝策略，在极端剪枝场景下成功保持优越的导航精度，同时维持高度竞争力的推理效率。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的实际部署进一步验证了在实用机器人约束下的可靠低延迟指令跟随导航。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于弥合大规模多模态建模与高效实时具身部署之间的差距，为机器人导航系统提供了实用的计算优化解决方案，实现了在不牺牲精度的情况下显著降低延迟的目标。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow natural-language instructions in visually grounded environments, serving as a key capability for embodied robotic systems. Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong navigation performance, but their high computational cost introduces latency that limits real-time deployment. We propose a training-free spatio-temporal vision token pruning framework tailored to VLA-based VLN. We apply spatial token selection to the current view, alongside spatio-temporal compression for historical memories, enabling efficient long-horizon inference while reducing redundant computation. Leveraging attention-based token importance and query-guided spatio-temporal filtering, the proposed approach preserves navigation-relevant information without retraining or modifying pretrained models, allowing plug-and-play integration into existing VLA systems. Through experiments on standard VLN benchmarks, we confirm that our method significantly outperforms existing pruning strategies. It successfully preserves superior navigation accuracy under extreme pruning scenarios, all while maintaining the highly competitive inference efficiency. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates reliable and low-latency instruction-following navigation under practical robotic constraints. We hope this work helps bridge the gap between large-scale multimodal modeling and efficient, real-time embodied deployment in robotic navigation systems.

</details>


### [45] [CFEAR-Teach-and-Repeat: Fast and Accurate Radar-only Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06501)
*Maximilian Hilger,Daniel Adolfsson,Ralf Becker,Henrik Andreasson,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CFEAR-TR：基于单旋转雷达的教导-重复定位系统，在恶劣天气条件下实现高精度、鲁棒的自主导航，定位精度达0.117米/0.096°，相比现有方法提升63%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恶劣天气条件下，光学传感器可能失效，因此需要可靠的地图先验定位方法。现有方法在恶劣天气下的定位精度和鲁棒性不足，需要一种轻量级、易于部署且能在不同季节和天气现象下工作的定位方案。

Method: 使用单旋转雷达的教导-重复定位流程。通过将实时扫描与存储的教导地图扫描以及滑动窗口中的实时关键帧进行联合对齐来实现定位。雷达扫描使用从多普勒补偿测量计算出的稀疏定向表面点集表示，地图存储在姿态图中，在定位过程中遍历该图。

Result: 在Boreas数据集的测试序列上，CFEAR-TR定位精度达到0.117米和0.096°，相比之前的最先进方法提升高达63%。系统运行效率为29Hz，大幅缩小了与激光雷达级别定位的差距，特别是在航向估计方面。

Conclusion: CFEAR-TR提供了一种轻量级、易于部署且鲁棒的雷达定位解决方案，在恶劣天气条件下实现了接近激光雷达级别的定位精度，为自主导航系统提供了可靠的定位能力。

Abstract: Reliable localization in prior maps is essential for autonomous navigation, particularly under adverse weather, where optical sensors may fail. We present CFEAR-TR, a teach-and-repeat localization pipeline using a single spinning radar, which is designed for easily deployable, lightweight, and robust navigation in adverse conditions. Our method localizes by jointly aligning live scans to both stored scans from the teach mapping pass, and to a sliding window of recent live keyframes. This ensures accurate and robust pose estimation across different seasons and weather phenomena. Radar scans are represented using a sparse set of oriented surface points, computed from Doppler-compensated measurements. The map is stored in a pose graph that is traversed during localization. Experiments on the held-out test sequences from the Boreas dataset show that CFEAR-TR can localize with an accuracy as low as 0.117 m and 0.096°, corresponding to improvements of up to 63% over the previous state of the art, while running efficiently at 29 Hz. These results substantially narrow the gap to lidar-level localization, particularly in heading estimation. We make the C++ implementation of our work available to the community.

</details>


### [46] [Underactuated multimodal jumping robot for extraterrestrial exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06525)
*Neil R. Wagner,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 双反应轮欠驱动单腿机器人，通过滚动、跳跃和空中重定向实现低重力环境下的多模态运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低重力天体上的多模态运动能力，设计紧凑轻量的机器人系统以应对不同地形和环境条件

Method: 使用仅两个反应轮控制空间姿态，配备平衡控制器（地面跳跃方向控制）和空中重定向控制器（着陆腿姿态控制），仅三个执行器实现滚动、目标跳跃着陆和自恢复

Result: 成功演示了滚动、目标跳跃着陆和自恢复功能，系统尺寸0.33米、重量1.25公斤，能通过简单模式切换适应不同地形

Conclusion: 欠驱动设计结合多模态运动策略为低重力环境探索提供了紧凑高效的机器人解决方案

Abstract: We present a rolling and jumping underactuated monopedal robot designed to explore multimodal locomotion on low-gravity bodies. It uses only two reaction wheels to control its spatial orientation with two controllers: a balancing controller which can aim the robot's jump direction on the ground, and an aerial reorientation controller which can aim the robot's leg for landing after flight. We demonstrate rolling, targeted jumping and landing, and self-righting using only three actuators total, keeping system size to 0.33m and 1.25kg. Simple switching between locomotion modes enables the system to deal with differing landscapes and environmental conditions.

</details>


### [47] [Unified Learning of Temporal Task Structure and Action Timing for Bimanual Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06538)
*Christian Dreher,Patrick Dormanns,Andre Meixner,Tamim Asfour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从人类演示中学习符号和亚符号时间约束的方法，用于生成双手机器人操作的可执行时间参数化计划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人操作需要同时考虑高层任务结构（符号时间关系）和低层执行协调（具体时间参数），现有方法将这两个层面孤立处理，导致高层任务规划与低层运动同步之间存在鸿沟

Method: 1) 基于多元高斯混合模型的三维时间表示方法，在亚符号层面捕捉动作间的时间关系；2) 基于DPLL算法的符号关系分配方法，为动作对寻找并排序所有无矛盾的Allen关系分配；3) 结合符号和亚符号约束的优化规划系统，生成时间参数化计划

Result: 在多个数据集上评估，该方法生成的时间参数化计划比最具代表性的演示基线更接近人类演示

Conclusion: 该方法成功填补了高层符号任务规划与低层运动同步之间的鸿沟，能够从人类演示中学习完整的时间约束并生成可执行的双手机器人操作计划

Abstract: Temporal task structure is fundamental for bimanual manipulation: a robot must not only know that one action precedes or overlaps another, but also when each action should occur and how long it should take. While symbolic temporal relations enable high-level reasoning about task structure and alternative execution sequences, concrete timing parameters are equally essential for coordinating two hands at the execution level. Existing approaches address these two levels in isolation, leaving a gap between high-level task planning and low-level movement synchronization. This work presents an approach for learning both symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints from human demonstrations and deriving executable, temporally parametrized plans for bimanual manipulation. Our contributions are (i) a 3-dimensional representation of timings between two actions with methods based on multivariate Gaussian Mixture Models to represent temporal relationships between actions on a subsymbolic level, (ii) a method based on the Davis-Putnam-Logemann-Loveland (DPLL) algorithm that finds and ranks all contradiction-free assignments of Allen relations to action pairs, representing different modes of a task, and (iii) an optimization-based planning system that combines the identified symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints to derive temporally parametrized plans for robot execution. We evaluate our approach on several datasets, demonstrating that our method generates temporally parametrized plans closer to human demonstrations than the most characteristic demonstration baseline.

</details>

### [48] [Human-Data Interaction, Exploration, and Visualization in the AI Era: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05542)
*Jean-Daniel Fekete,Yifan Hu,Dominik Moritz,Arnab Nandi,Senjuti Basu Roy,Eugene Wu,Nikos Bikakis,George Papastefanatos,Panos K. Chrysanthis,Guoliang Li,Lingyun Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AI时代下人类数据交互系统面临新挑战：大规模异构多模态数据、基础模型带来的不确定性，需要重新定义人机角色并整合认知设计原则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI快速发展正在变革以人为中心的系统，特别是在人类-AI交互、人类-数据交互和可视分析领域。AI时代的数据分析涉及大规模、异构、多模态的非结构化数据以及基础模型（LLMs和VLMs），这些变化给人类-数据交互系统带来了新的挑战

Method: 本文通过调查AI最新进展带来的挑战，研究这些发展如何重塑用户与数据的交互方式，同时为构建AI时代交互式数据分析的人类中心AI系统提出局限性和开放研究方向

Result: 识别出人类-数据交互系统面临的持久挑战：感知不对齐的延迟、可扩展性限制、现有交互和探索范式的局限性，以及AI生成见解的可靠性和可解释性日益增长的不确定性

Conclusion: 应对这些挑战需要超越传统的效率和可扩展性指标，重新定义分析工作流中人类和机器的角色，并将认知、感知和设计原则整合到人类-数据交互堆栈的每个层面

Abstract: The rapid advancement of AI is transforming human-centered systems, with profound implications for human-AI interaction, human-data interaction, and visual analytics. In the AI era, data analysis increasingly involves large-scale, heterogeneous, and multimodal data that is predominantly unstructured, as well as foundation models such as LLMs and VLMs, which introduce additional uncertainty into analytical processes. These shifts expose persistent challenges for human-data interactive systems, including perceptually misaligned latency, scalability constraints, limitations of existing interaction and exploration paradigms, and growing uncertainty regarding the reliability and interpretability of AI-generated insights. Responding to these challenges requires moving beyond conventional efficiency and scalability metrics, redefining the roles of humans and machines in analytical workflows, and incorporating cognitive, perceptual, and design principles into every level of the human-data interaction stack. This paper investigates the challenges introduced by recent advances in AI and examines how these developments are reshaping the ways users engage with data, while outlining limitations and open research directions for building human-centered AI systems for interactive data analysis in the AI era.

</details>


### [49] [Querying with Conflicts of Interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05704)
*Nischal Aryal,Arash Termehchy,Marianne Winslett*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一个在数据源与用户存在利益冲突时查询偏置数据的正式框架，包括偏置检测算法和查询重写方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据源与用户之间存在利益冲突，导致数据源有动机返回偏置结果（如电商平台为增加收入而操纵产品排名），现有去偏置方案缺乏实施激励

Method: 提出一个形式化框架来处理数据源故意返回偏置答案的场景；开发高效算法检测用户能否从偏置数据源提取相关信息；设计方法检测查询结果中的偏置信息；提出查询重写算法以增加返回结果中的相关信息量

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，所提算法高效且能在大规模数据上返回相关信息

Conclusion: 为解决数据源与用户利益冲突导致的偏置问题提供了一个有效的形式化框架和实用算法，使用户能在偏置数据源中获取相关信息

Abstract: Conflicts of interest often arise between data sources and their users regarding how the users' information needs should be interpreted by the data source. For example, an online product search might be biased towards presenting certain products higher than in its list of results to improve its revenue, which may not follow the user's desired ranking expressed in their query. The research community has proposed schemes for data systems to implement to ensure unbiased results. However, data systems and services usually have little or no incentive to implement these measures, e.g., these biases often increase their profits. In this paper, we propose a novel formal framework for querying in settings where the data source has incentives to return biased answers intentionally due to the conflict of interest between the user and the data source. We propose efficient algorithms to detect whether it is possible for users to extract relevant information from biased data sources. We propose methods to detect biased information in the results of a query efficiently. We also propose algorithms to reformulate input queries to increase the amount of relevant information in the returned results over biased data sources. Using experiments on real-world datasets, we show that our algorithms are efficient and return relevant information over large data.

</details>


### [50] [Efficient Vector Search in the Wild: One Model for Multi-K Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06159)
*Yifan Peng,Jiafei Fan,Xingda Wei,Sijie Shen,Rong Chen,Jianning Wang,Xiaojian Luo,Wenyuan Yu,Jingren Zhou,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OMEGA是一种K泛化学习式top-K搜索方法，通过基于轨迹的特征训练K=1的基础模型，结合动态精炼过程处理不同K值查询，实现高精度、高性能和低预处理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式top-K搜索模型针对特定K值训练，无法泛化到现实世界的多K值查询场景：对于较大K值存在精度下降，对于较小K值存在性能损失。训练泛化模型需要大量预处理时间，不适合实际部署。

Method: 提出OMEGA方法：1) 使用基于轨迹的特征训练K=1的基础模型；2) 通过动态精炼过程准确预测较大K值；3) 对较小K值实现最小性能损失；4) 利用top-K搜索的统计特性减少过度模型调用以提高效率。

Result: 在多个公共和生产数据集上的评估显示：在相同预处理预算下，OMEGA相比最先进学习式搜索方法平均延迟降低6-33%，同时达到相同召回目标。仅用16-30%的预处理时间，OMEGA达到基线最优平均延迟的1.01-1.28倍。

Conclusion: OMEGA是一种K泛化学习式top-K搜索方法，能够同时实现高精度、高性能和低预处理成本，有效解决了现有方法在多K值查询场景中的泛化问题。

Abstract: Learned top-K search is a promising approach for serving vector queries with both high accuracy and performance. However, current models trained for a specific K value fail to generalize to real-world multi-K queries: they suffer from accuracy degradation (for larger Ks) and performance loss (for smaller Ks). Training the model to generalize on different Ks requires orders of magnitude more preprocessing time and is not suitable for serving vector queries in the wild. We present OMEGA, a K-generalizable learned top-K search method that simultaneously achieves high accuracy, high performance, and low preprocessing cost for multi-K vector queries. The key idea is that a base model properly trained on K=1 with our trajectory-based features can be used to accurately predict larger Ks with a dynamic refinement procedure and smaller Ks with minimal performance loss. To make our refinements efficient, we further leverage the statistical properties of top-K searches to reduce excessive model invocations. Extensive evaluations on multiple public and production datasets show that, under the same preprocessing budgets, OMEGA achieves 6-33% lower average latency compared to state-of-the-art learned search methods, while all systems achieve the same recall target. With only 16-30% of the preprocessing time, OMEGA attains 1.01-1.28x of the optimal average latency of these baselines.

</details>


### [51] [Tag-specific Regret Minimization Problem in Outdoor Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06405)
*Dildar Ali,Abishek Salaria,Ansh Jasrotia,Suman Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文提出TRMOA问题，解决户外广告中标签分配的组合优化问题，旨在最小化总遗憾值，满足影响力需求和预算约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外广告因其高投资回报率而流行，电商平台需要有效分配广告标签以满足影响力需求，同时控制预算并最小化遗憾值。

Method: 提出公平感知的贪心轮询方法、随机贪心算法和局部搜索算法来解决NP难的TRMOA问题。

Result: 使用真实世界轨迹和广告牌数据集验证了所提方法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: TRMOA是NP难且不可近似的问题，但提出的算法能够有效解决这一户外广告标签分配优化问题。

Abstract: Recently, out-of-home advertising has become a popular marketing technique, due to its higher return on investment. E-commerce houses approach the influence provider to achieve effective advertising through their tags (advertising content), influence demand, and budgets. The influence provider's goal will be to make proper tag allocations, meet the required influence demand within the budget constraint, and minimize total regret. We formalize this as a combinatorial optimization problem and refer to it as \textsc{Tag-specific Regret Minimization in Outdoor Advertising (TRMOA)}. We show that TRMOA is NP-hard and inapproximable within a constant factor. The regret model we consider is non-monotone and non-submodular, and the simple greedy approach is ineffective. We introduce a fairness-aware greedy round-robin approach that reduces regret with balanced allocation across advertisers. To improve, we also introduce randomized greedy and local search algorithms. We have experimented with all the methodologies using real-world trajectory and billboard datasets to show the effectiveness and efficiency of the solution methodologies.

</details>

### [52] [Information-Theoretic Privacy Control for Sequential Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05520)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究序列多智能体LLM系统中的组合隐私泄露问题，提出隐私正则化训练框架来约束智能体间的信息流


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列多智能体LLM系统在医疗、金融等敏感领域部署时，即使单个智能体满足局部隐私约束，敏感信息仍可能通过序列组合和中间表示被推断，需要研究组合隐私泄露问题

Method: 使用互信息形式化隐私泄露，推导理论边界描述局部泄露如何在序列执行中放大；提出隐私正则化训练框架，直接约束智能体输出与局部敏感变量间的信息流

Result: 在三个基准数据集上评估不同深度的序列智能体管道，展示了稳定的优化动态和一致、可解释的隐私-效用权衡

Conclusion: 智能体LLM系统中的隐私不能仅通过局部约束保证，必须在训练和部署期间作为系统级属性来处理

Abstract: Sequential multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare, finance, and enterprise decision-making, where multiple specialized agents collaboratively process a single user request. Although individual agents may satisfy local privacy constraints, sensitive information can still be inferred through sequential composition and intermediate representations. In this work, we study \emph{compositional privacy leakage} in sequential LLM agent pipelines. We formalize leakage using mutual information and derive a theoretical bound that characterizes how locally introduced leakage can amplify across agents under sequential execution. Motivated by this analysis, we propose a privacy-regularized training framework that directly constrains information flow between agent outputs and agent-local sensitive variables. We evaluate our approach across sequential agent pipelines of varying depth on three benchmark datasets, demonstrating stable optimization dynamics and consistent, interpretable privacy-utility trade-offs. Our results show that privacy in agentic LLM systems cannot be guaranteed by local constraints alone and must instead be treated as a system-level property during both training and deployment.

</details>


### [53] [The Coordination Gap: Alternation Metrics for Temporal Dynamics in Multi-Agent Battle of the Exes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05789)
*Nikolaos Al. Papadopoulos,Konstantinos Psannis*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出新的时间敏感度量指标来评估多智能体协调质量，发现传统指标会严重误判协调动态，即使传统指标显示良好，学习策略在交替协调方面可能比随机基线差81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于结果的协调度量指标（如效率和公平性）存在时间盲区，无法区分结构化交替与垄断或随机访问模式，且随着智能体数量增加会失去区分能力，掩盖了协调中的不平等问题。

Method: 引入完美交替作为参考协调机制，提出六种新的交替度量指标作为时间敏感的协调质量观测值。使用Q-learning智能体作为最小自适应诊断基线，并与随机策略零过程进行比较。

Result: 学习策略在传统度量指标上表现良好（奖励公平性常超过0.9），但在交替度量评估下比随机基线差81%，这种缺陷在两智能体情况下已存在并随智能体数量增加而加剧。

Conclusion: 高聚合回报可能与差的时间协调共存，传统指标可能严重误判涌现的动态。强调时间感知观测值对分析多智能体游戏协调的必要性，以及随机策略基线作为解释协调结果相对于机会水平行为的重要零过程。

Abstract: Multi-agent coordination dilemmas expose a fundamental tension between individual optimization and collective welfare, yet characterizing such coordination requires metrics sensitive to temporal structure and collective dynamics. As a diagnostic testbed, we study a BoE-derived multi-agent variant of the Battle of the Exes, formalizing it as a Markov game in which turn-taking emerges as a periodic coordination regime. Conventional outcome-based metrics (e.g., efficiency and min/max fairness) are temporally blind -- they cannot distinguish structured alternation from monopolistic or random access patterns -- and fairness ratios lose discriminative power as n grows, obscuring inequities.
  To address this limitation, we introduce Perfect Alternation (PA) as a reference coordination regime and propose six novel Alternation (ALT) metrics designed as temporally sensitive observables of coordination quality. Using Q-learning agents as a minimal adaptive diagnostic baseline, and comparing against random-policy null processes, we uncover a clear measurement failure: despite exhibiting deceptively high traditional metrics (e.g., reward fairness often exceeding 0.9), learned policies perform up to 81% below random baselines under ALT-variant evaluation -- a deficit already present in the two-agent case and intensifying as n grows.
  These results demonstrate, in this setting, that high aggregate payoffs can coexist with poor temporal coordination, and that conventional metrics may severely mischaracterize emergent dynamics. Our findings underscore the necessity of temporally aware observables for analyzing coordination in multi-agent games and highlight random-policy baselines as essential null processes for interpreting coordination outcomes relative to chance-level behavior.

</details>


### [54] [Evaluating LLM Alignment With Human Trust Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05839)
*Anushka Debnath,Stephen Cranefield,Bastin Tony Roy Savarimuthu,Emiliano Lorini*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 对GPT-J-6B模型内部信任表征的白盒分析，发现其信任表示与Castelfranchi社会认知模型最为一致


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信任在人类交互和多智能体系统中至关重要，但目前对大型语言模型内部如何概念化和推理信任的理解有限

Method: 使用对比提示在LLM激活空间中生成信任相关嵌入向量，从五个人类信任模型中识别信任相关概念，通过计算60个一般情感概念的余弦相似度确定概念对齐阈值，测量LLM内部信任表示与信任相关概念的相似度

Result: EleutherAI/gpt-j-6B的内部信任表示与Castelfranchi社会认知模型最为一致，其次是Marsh模型

Conclusion: LLM在其激活空间中编码社会认知结构的方式支持有意义的比较分析，为社交认知理论提供信息，并支持人机协作系统的设计

Abstract: Trust plays a pivotal role in enabling effective cooperation, reducing uncertainty, and guiding decision-making in both human interactions and multi-agent systems. Although it is significant, there is limited understanding of how large language models (LLMs) internally conceptualize and reason about trust. This work presents a white-box analysis of trust representation in EleutherAI/gpt-j-6B, using contrastive prompting to generate embedding vectors within the activation space of the LLM for diadic trust and related interpersonal relationship attributes. We first identified trust-related concepts from five established human trust models. We then determined a threshold for significant conceptual alignment by computing pairwise cosine similarities across 60 general emotional concepts. Then we measured the cosine similarities between the LLM's internal representation of trust and the derived trust-related concepts. Our results show that the internal trust representation of EleutherAI/gpt-j-6B aligns most closely with the Castelfranchi socio-cognitive model, followed by the Marsh Model. These findings indicate that LLMs encode socio-cognitive constructs in their activation space in ways that support meaningful comparative analyses, inform theories of social cognition, and support the design of human-AI collaborative systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [55] [Autocorrelation effects in a stochastic-process model for decision making via time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05559)
*Tomoki Yamagami,Mikio Hasegawa,Takatomo Mihana,Ryoichi Horisaki,Atsushi Uchida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析了基于时间序列的决策模型，发现自相关特性对多臂赌博机问题求解性能有重要影响，揭示了自相关符号与奖励环境之间的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前实验表明基于光子混沌动力学的决策系统性能强烈依赖于时间序列的自相关特性，但缺乏最小数学模型来解释这种自相关优势的根本机制。

Method: 采用基于时间序列的决策随机过程模型，结合拔河原理解决两臂赌博机问题，模型中阈值和双值马尔可夫信号共同演化，通过数值模拟分析自相关特性对决策性能的影响。

Result: 数值结果揭示了环境依赖结构：在奖励丰富环境中负自相关最优，在奖励贫乏环境中正自相关最优。当获胜概率之和大于1时负自相关有利，小于1时正自相关有利，等于1时性能与自相关无关。

Conclusion: 该研究为改进无线通信和机器人强化学习应用的决策方案提供了理论基础，阐明了时间序列自相关特性在决策过程中的重要作用及其与环境条件的匹配关系。

Abstract: Decision makers exploiting photonic chaotic dynamics obtained by semiconductor lasers provide an ultrafast approach to solving multi-armed bandit problems by using a temporal optical signal as the driving source for sequential decisions. In such systems, the sampling interval of the chaotic waveform shapes the temporal correlation of the resulting time series, and experiments have reported that decision accuracy depends strongly on this autocorrelation property. However, it remains unclear whether the benefit of autocorrelation can be explained by a minimal mathematical model. Here, we analyze a stochastic-process model of the time-series-based decision making using the tug-of-war principle for solving the two-armed bandit problem, where the threshold and a two-valued Markov signal evolve jointly. Numerical results reveal an environment-dependent structure: negative (positive) autocorrelation is optimal in reward-rich (reward-poor) environments. These findings show that negative autocorrelation of the time series is advantageous when the sum of the winning probabilities is more than $1$, whereas positive autocorrelation is useful when the sum of the winning probabilities is less than $1$. Moreover, the performance is independent of autocorrelation if the sum of the winning probabilities equals $1$, which is mathematically clarified. This study paves the way for improving the decision-making scheme for reinforcement learning applications in wireless communications and robotics.

</details>


### [56] [VDCook:DIY video data cook your MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05539)
*Chengwei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VDCook是一个自演进的视频数据操作系统，通过自然语言查询和参数配置，自动执行查询优化、视频检索和可控合成，生成带有完整溯源和元数据的领域特定数据包，支持持续更新和领域扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态、一次性构建的数据集无法满足视频训练数据的动态需求，研究人员和垂直领域团队需要更灵活、可配置的数据构建平台来降低专业视频数据集构建的门槛。

Method: 用户通过自然语言查询和可调参数发起数据请求，系统自动进行查询优化，并行运行真实视频检索和可控合成模块，生成带有完整溯源、元数据和可复现Notebook的数据包，基于MCP协议实现自动化数据摄取。

Result: 系统能够生成领域特定的视频数据包，提供多维元数据标注（场景分割、运动评分、OCR比率、自动字幕等），支持数据集向动态演进的开放生态系统转变。

Conclusion: VDCook通过基础设施级解决方案显著降低了构建专业视频训练数据集的门槛，支持社区贡献和治理驱动的数据扩展范式，将数据集从静态产品转变为动态生态系统。

Abstract: We introduce VDCook: a self-evolving video data operating system, a configurable video data construction platform for researchers and vertical domain teams. Users initiate data requests via natural language queries and adjustable parameters (scale, retrieval-synthesis ratio, quality threshold). The system automatically performs query optimization, concurrently running real video retrieval and controlled synthesis modules. It ultimately generates in-domain data packages with complete provenance and metadata, along with reproducible Notebooks.
  Unlike traditional static, one-time-built datasets, VDCook enables continuous updates and domain expansion through its automated data ingestion mechanism based on MCP (Model Context Protocol)\cite{mcp2024anthropic}, transforming datasets into dynamically evolving open ecosystems. The system also provides multi-dimensional metadata annotation (scene segmentation, motion scoring, OCR ratio, automatic captioning, etc.), laying the foundation for flexible subsequent data `cooking' and indexing\cite{vlogger}.
  This platform aims to significantly lower the barrier to constructing specialized video training datasets through infrastructure-level solutions, while supporting community contributions and a governance-enabled data expansion paradigm. \textbf{Project demo:} https://screenapp.io/app/v/WP0SvffgsH

</details>


### [57] [NOBLE: Accelerating Transformers with Nonlinear Low-Rank Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06492)
*Ethan Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOBLE是一种在Transformer线性层中添加非线性低秩分支的架构增强方法，用于从头预训练而非微调，通过可学习的非线性激活函数提升训练效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）主要针对微调阶段，缺乏专门为从头预训练设计的架构增强方法。NOBLE旨在通过添加永久性的非线性低秩分支来提升预训练效率，同时保持较低的计算开销

Method: 在Transformer线性层中添加非线性低秩分支：σ(xWdown)Wup，其中σ为可学习的非线性激活函数。研究发现CosNet（两层余弦非线性，具有可学习频率和相位，瓶颈空间中有线性投影）效果最佳。该方法作为架构的永久部分而非临时适配器

Result: NOBLE在多个模型上取得显著改进：达到基线评估损失所需训练步骤减少32%（1.47倍速度提升），仅增加4%参数和7%单步时间开销，净墙钟时间加速达1.22倍。在LLM（250M和1.5B参数）、BERT、VQGAN和ViT上均显示训练效率提升。但发现Mixup/CutMix等随机增强会干扰NOBLE在ImageNet分类中的效果

Conclusion: NOBLE是一种有效的预训练架构增强方法，通过添加非线性低秩分支显著提升训练效率。该方法与现有PEFT方法互补，专门针对从头预训练设计。需要注意某些正则化技术（如Mixup）可能会干扰其效果，这可能是因为NOBLE更专注于目标函数的尖锐特征而非平滑拟合

Abstract: We introduce NOBLE (Nonlinear lOw-rank Branch for Linear Enhancement), an architectural augmentation that adds nonlinear low-rank branches to transformer linear layers. Unlike LoRA and other parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, NOBLE is designed for pretraining from scratch. The branch is a permanent part of the architecture as opposed to an adapter for finetuning on top of frozen weights. The branch computes σ(xWdown)Wup where σ is a learnable nonlinearity. We evaluate several activation functions and find that CosNet, a two-layer cosine nonlinearity with learnable frequency and phase with a linear projection in between them in the bottleneck space, performs best. NOBLE achieves substantial improvements with minimal overhead: up to 1.47x step speedup to reach baseline eval loss (up to 32% fewer training steps), with as low as 4% additional parameters and 7% step time overhead, resulting in up to 1.22x net wallclock speedup. Experiments on LLMs (250M and 1.5B parameters), BERT, VQGAN, and ViT consistently show improved training efficiency. We identify one caveat: Mixup/CutMix augmentation interferes with NOBLE's benefits in Imagenet classification along with other stochastic augmentations, but when disabled, ViT also improves. This discrepancy is possibly explained by regularization techniques that encourage smoother fits to the target function while NOBLE may specialize more in sharper aspects of the target function.

</details>


### [58] [IntSeqBERT: Learning Arithmetic Structure in OEIS via Modulo-Spectrum Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05556)
*Kazuhisa Nakasho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IntSeqBERT：用于OEIS整数序列预测的双流Transformer，通过连续对数尺度幅度嵌入和模数嵌入处理大范围数值，显著提升预测精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OEIS中的整数序列数值范围极大（从个位数到天文级阶乘和指数），标准分词模型无法处理词汇表外值，也无法利用周期性算术结构，需要专门设计模型

Method: 提出IntSeqBERT双流Transformer编码器，每个序列元素沿两个互补轴编码：连续对数尺度幅度嵌入和100个模数（2-101）的正弦/余弦模嵌入，通过FiLM融合。三个预测头联合训练：幅度回归、符号分类和100个模数预测

Result: 大型模型（9150万参数）在测试集上达到95.85%幅度精度和50.38%平均模精度（MMA），比标准分词Transformer基线分别提升8.9和4.5个百分点。消融实验显示模数流贡献了15.2个百分点的MMA增益和6.2个百分点的幅度精度提升。基于概率中国剩余定理的求解器将预测转化为具体整数，使下一项预测比基线提升7.4倍

Conclusion: IntSeqBERT通过双流编码有效处理大范围整数序列，模数分析显示复合模数通过CRT聚合更高效地捕获OEIS算术结构，为整数序列建模提供了新方法

Abstract: Integer sequences in the OEIS span values from single-digit constants to astronomical factorials and exponentials, making prediction challenging for standard tokenised models that cannot handle out-of-vocabulary values or exploit periodic arithmetic structure. We present IntSeqBERT, a dual-stream Transformer encoder for masked integer-sequence modelling on OEIS. Each sequence element is encoded along two complementary axes: a continuous log-scale magnitude embedding and sin/cos modulo embeddings for 100 residues (moduli $2$--$101$), fused via FiLM. Three prediction heads (magnitude regression, sign classification, and modulo prediction for 100 moduli) are trained jointly on 274,705 OEIS sequences. At the Large scale (91.5M parameters), IntSeqBERT achieves 95.85% magnitude accuracy and 50.38% Mean Modulo Accuracy (MMA) on the test set, outperforming a standard tokenised Transformer baseline by $+8.9$ pt and $+4.5$ pt, respectively. An ablation removing the modulo stream confirms it accounts for $+15.2$ pt of the MMA gain and contributes an additional $+6.2$ pt to magnitude accuracy. A probabilistic Chinese Remainder Theorem (CRT)-based Solver converts the model's predictions into concrete integers, yielding a 7.4-fold improvement in next-term prediction over the tokenised-Transformer baseline (Top-1: 19.09% vs. 2.59%). Modulo spectrum analysis reveals a strong negative correlation between Normalised Information Gain (NIG) and Euler's totient ratio $\varphi(m)/m$ ($r = -0.851$, $p < 10^{-28}$), providing empirical evidence that composite moduli capture OEIS arithmetic structure more efficiently via CRT aggregation.

</details>


### [59] [CLAIRE: Compressed Latent Autoencoder for Industrial Representation and Evaluation -- A Deep Learning Framework for Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06361)
*Mohammadhossein Ghahramani,Mengchu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAIRE是一个用于智能制造的混合端到端学习框架，结合无监督深度表示学习和有监督分类，通过深度自编码器提取紧凑潜在特征，再使用分类器进行故障预测，并集成可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维工业环境中的准确故障检测面临数据复杂性、噪声和冗余等挑战，需要更有效的特征提取和解释性方法。

Method: 提出CLAIRE混合框架：1) 使用优化的深度自编码器将原始输入转换为紧凑潜在空间，捕获内在数据结构并抑制噪声；2) 将学习到的表示输入下游分类器进行二元故障预测；3) 集成基于博弈论的可解释性技术分析潜在空间，识别对故障预测最有贡献的输入特征。

Result: 在高维数据集上的实验表明，CLAIRE显著优于直接在原始特征上训练的传统分类器。框架具有模块化和可解释性，可适应其他复杂高维数据领域。

Conclusion: CLAIRE展示了将可解释AI与特征感知正则化相结合在鲁棒故障检测中的潜力，其模块化和可解释性使其在医疗、金融、环境监测等其他高维数据领域具有应用前景。

Abstract: Accurate fault detection in high-dimensional industrial environments remains a major challenge due to the inherent complexity, noise, and redundancy in sensor data. This paper introduces CLAIRE, i.e., a hybrid end-to-end learning framework that integrates unsupervised deep representation learning with supervised classification for intelligent quality control in smart manufacturing systems. It employs an optimized deep autoencoder to transform raw input into a compact latent space, effectively capturing the intrinsic data structure while suppressing irrelevant or noisy features. The learned representations are then fed into a downstream classifier to perform binary fault prediction. Experimental results on a high-dimensional dataset demonstrate that CLAIRE significantly outperforms conventional classifiers trained directly on raw features. Moreover, the framework incorporates a post hoc phase, using a game-theory-based interpretability technique, to analyze the latent space and identify the most informative input features contributing to fault predictions. The proposed framework highlights the potential of integrating explainable AI with feature-aware regularization for robust fault detection. The modular and interpretable nature of the proposed framework makes it highly adaptable, offering promising applications in other domains characterized by complex, high-dimensional data, such as healthcare, finance, and environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [60] [When AI Levels the Playing Field: Skill Homogenization, Asset Concentration, and Two Regimes of Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05565)
*Xupeng Chen,Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式AI压缩任务内技能差异，同时将经济价值转向集中的互补资产，形成个体表现均等化但总体不平等加剧的悖论


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式AI如何通过压缩任务内技能差异和转移经济价值到互补资产，导致个体层面平等化与总体层面不平等加剧的悖论

Method: 构建包含内生教育、雇主筛选和异质性企业的任务基础模型，通过模拟矩方法匹配六个经验目标进行情景分析，使用BLS OEWS数据进行职业层面回归

Result: 模型识别出两个制度边界取决于AI技术结构（专有vs商品化）和劳动力市场制度；校准参数显示总体不平等符号由特定矩和参数决定，AI技术结构独立跨越边界

Conclusion: 主要贡献是机制分析而非符号判断；职业层面数据无法检验模型的任务层面预测，需要任务内面板数据但当前大规模数据尚不存在

Abstract: Generative AI compresses within-task skill differences while shifting economic value toward concentrated complementary assets, creating an apparent paradox: the technology that equalizes individual performance may widen aggregate inequality. We formalize this tension in a task-based model with endogenous education, employer screening, and heterogeneous firms. The model yields two regimes whose boundary depends on AI's technology structure (proprietary vs.\ commodity) and labor market institutions (rent-sharing elasticity, asset concentration). A scenario analysis via Method of Simulated Moments, matching six empirical targets, disciplines the model's quantitative magnitudes; a sensitivity decomposition reveals that the five non-$Δ$Gini moments identify mechanism rates but not the aggregate sign, which at the calibrated parameters is pinned by $m_6$ and $ξ$, while AI's technology structure ($η_1$ vs.\ $η_0$) independently crosses the boundary. The contribution is the mechanism -- not a verdict on the sign. Occupation-level regressions using BLS OEWS data (2019--2023) illustrate why such data cannot test the model's task-level predictions. The predictions are testable with within-occupation, within-task panel data that do not yet exist at scale.

</details>


### [61] [Aligning the True Semantics: Constrained Decoupling and Distribution Sampling for Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05566)
*Xiang Ma,Lexin Fang,Litian Xu,Caiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CDDS算法通过约束解耦和分布采样解决跨模态对齐中的语义一致性挑战，显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统跨模态对齐方法追求嵌入一致性来实现语义一致性，但忽略了嵌入中的非语义信息。需要将嵌入解耦为语义和模态分量，仅对齐语义分量，但面临两个主要挑战：缺乏区分语义和模态信息的标准，以及模态差距可能导致语义对齐偏差或信息丢失。

Method: 提出CDDS算法：1) 引入双路径UNet自适应解耦嵌入，应用多重约束确保有效分离；2) 提出分布采样方法弥合模态差距，确保对齐过程的合理性。

Result: 在多个基准测试和模型骨干上的广泛实验表明CDDS的优越性，相比最先进方法性能提升6.6%到14.2%。

Conclusion: CDDS通过约束解耦和分布采样有效解决了跨模态对齐中的语义一致性挑战，实现了更好的语义对齐效果。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment is a crucial task in multimodal learning aimed at achieving semantic consistency between vision and language. This requires that image-text pairs exhibit similar semantics. Traditional algorithms pursue embedding consistency to achieve semantic consistency, ignoring the non-semantic information present in the embedding. An intuitive approach is to decouple the embeddings into semantic and modality components, aligning only the semantic component. However, this introduces two main challenges: (1) There is no established standard for distinguishing semantic and modal information. (2) The modality gap can cause semantic alignment deviation or information loss. To align the true semantics, we propose a novel cross-modal alignment algorithm via \textbf{C}onstrained \textbf{D}ecoupling and \textbf{D}istribution \textbf{S}ampling (CDDS). Specifically, (1) A dual-path UNet is introduced to adaptively decouple the embeddings, applying multiple constraints to ensure effective separation. (2) A distribution sampling method is proposed to bridge the modality gap, ensuring the rationality of the alignment process. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks and model backbones demonstrate the superiority of CDDS, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 6.6\% to 14.2\%.

</details>


### [62] [Why Depth Matters in Parallelizable Sequence Models: A Lie Algebraic View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05573)
*Gyuryang Heo,Timothy Ngotiaoco,Kazuki Irie,Samuel J. Gershman,Bernardo Sabatini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从李代数控制视角分析序列模型的表达能力边界，建立了模型深度与李代数扩展塔的对应关系，推导了近似误差边界并验证了误差随深度指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可扩展序列模型（如Transformer变体和结构化状态空间模型）在表达能力与序列级并行性之间的权衡，特别关注模型在其表达能力范围之外操作时的误差边界和缩放规律。

Method: 采用李代数控制理论视角，建立序列模型深度与李代数扩展塔的对应关系，分析恒定深度序列模型的李代数类别及其表达能力边界，并解析推导近似误差边界。

Result: 理论分析表明误差随深度增加呈指数衰减，与这些模型的强大经验性能一致。通过在符号词和连续值状态跟踪问题上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 从李代数控制视角为序列模型的表达能力提供了理论框架，揭示了深度与误差衰减的指数关系，为理解可扩展序列模型的性能边界提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Scalable sequence models, such as Transformer variants and structured state-space models, often trade expressivity power for sequence-level parallelism, which enables efficient training. Here we examine the bounds on error and how error scales when models operate outside of their expressivity regimes using a Lie-algebraic control perspective. Our theory formulates a correspondence between the depth of a sequence model and the tower of Lie algebra extensions. Echoing recent theoretical studies, we characterize the Lie-algebraic class of constant-depth sequence models and their corresponding expressivity bounds. Furthermore, we analytically derive an approximation error bound and show that error diminishes exponentially as the depth increases, consistent with the strong empirical performance of these models. We validate our theoretical predictions using experiments on symbolic word and continuous-valued state-tracking problems.

</details>


### [63] [A Novel Hybrid Heuristic-Reinforcement Learning Optimization Approach for a Class of Railcar Shunting Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05579)
*Ruonan Zhao,Joseph Geunes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合启发式-强化学习框架，用于解决铁路调车场中具有双侧分类轨道访问的双机车多列车编组问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路调车场中的车辆编组是核心规划任务，需要分解和重组车辆组以形成出发列车。双侧分类轨道允许从一端添加车辆、另一端移除车辆（FIFO顺序），相比单侧轨道（LIFO顺序）提供了更灵活的访问方式。解决具有双侧访问和双机车的多列车编组问题具有组合复杂性挑战。

Method: 将问题分解为两个子问题，每个子问题具有单侧分类轨道访问和一个机车。提出混合启发式-强化学习框架，将铁路特定启发式方法与强化学习（Q-learning）相结合。该框架利用方法减少状态-动作空间，并在强化学习过程中引导探索。

Result: 通过一系列数值实验证明，HHRL算法在单侧访问单机车问题和双侧访问双机车问题中均表现出高效性和高质量。

Conclusion: 提出的混合启发式-强化学习框架能够有效解决铁路调车场中具有双侧分类轨道访问的双机车多列车编组问题，通过分解策略和状态空间优化方法实现了对复杂组合问题的有效求解。

Abstract: Railcar shunting is a core planning task in freight railyards, where yard planners need to disassemble and reassemble groups of railcars to form outbound trains. Classification tracks with access from one side only can be considered as stack structures, where railcars are added and removed from only one end, leading to a last-in-first-out (LIFO) retrieval order. In contrast, two-sided tracks function like queue structures, allowing railcars to be added from one end and removed from the opposite end, following a first-in-first-out (FIFO) order. We consider a problem requiring assembly of multiple outbound trains using two locomotives in a railyard with two-sided classification track access. To address this combinatorially challenging problem class, we decompose the problem into two subproblems, each with one-sided classification track access and a locomotive on each side. We present a novel Hybrid Heuristic-Reinforcement Learning (HHRL) framework that integrates railway-specific heuristic solution approaches with a reinforcement learning method, specifically Q-learning. The proposed framework leverages methods to decrease the state-action space and guide exploration during reinforcement learning. The results of a series of numerical experiments demonstrate the efficiency and quality of the HHRL algorithm in both one-sided access, single-locomotive problems and two-sided access, two-locomotive problems.

</details>


### [64] [On the Value of Tokeniser Pretraining in Physics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05598)
*Hadi Sotoudeh,Payel Mukhopadhyay,Ruben Ohana,Michael McCabe,Neil D. Lawrence,Shirley Ho,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究tokenizer预训练对物理仿真模型精度和效率的影响，发现在相同物理系统上预训练能最大程度提升性能，并提出了灵活时空压缩操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高分辨率模拟产生海量数据，训练基础模型学习这些数据的动力学有助于建模复杂多物理现象。然而，同时从零开始学习数据压缩表示和物理动力学两个任务会相互阻碍效果。

Method: 采用tokenizer预训练策略，先通过自编码目标预训练tokenizer，再训练动力学模型。引入灵活的时空压缩操作，扩展因果卷积以支持运行时可调整的压缩比。

Result: 在相同物理系统上预训练能最大程度提升性能：域内预训练在10,500训练步后使VRMSE降低64%。跨域预训练也能带来中等增益。这是首次系统研究物理基础模型的tokenizer预训练。

Conclusion: tokenizer预训练能显著提升物理仿真模型的效率和精度，预训练数据的选择策略至关重要。提出的灵活时空压缩操作支持高效适应多样化下游任务。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of tokeniser pretraining on the accuracy and efficiency of physics emulation. Modern high-resolution simulations produce vast volumes of data spanning diverse physical regimes and scales. Training foundation models to learn the dynamics underlying such data enables the modelling of complex multiphysics phenomena, especially in data-limited settings. The emerging class of physics foundation models typically aims to learn two tasks jointly: (i) extracting compact representations of high-resolution spatiotemporal data, and (ii) capturing governing physical dynamics. However, learning both tasks from scratch simultaneously can impede the effectiveness of either process. We demonstrate that pretraining the tokeniser with an autoencoding objective prior to training the dynamics model enhances computational efficiency for downstream tasks. Notably, the magnitude of this benefit depends on domain alignment: pretraining on the same physical system as the downstream task yields the largest improvements, while pretraining on other systems provides moderate gains. In-domain pretraining reduces VRMSE by 64% after 10,500 training steps compared to training from scratch. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic investigation of tokeniser pretraining for physics foundation models. We further introduce flexible spatiotemporal compression operations that extend causal convolutions to support runtime-adjustable compression ratios, enabling efficient adaptation to diverse downstream tasks. Our findings provide practical guidance for training efficient physics emulators and highlight the importance of strategic pretraining data selection.

</details>


### [65] [Identifying Adversary Characteristics from an Observed Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05625)
*Soyon Choi,Scott Alfeld,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种识别攻击者而非攻击本身的防御框架，通过分析观察到的攻击来推断攻击者特征，帮助防御者采取针对性防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御机制主要关注攻击本身（如对抗性正则化）或系统层面（如异常检测），但缺乏对攻击者特征的识别。了解攻击者特征有助于采取更有效的防御措施，包括系统外部缓解和针对性学习算法优化。

Method: 提出一个领域无关的框架，从观察到的攻击中识别最可能的攻击者。首先证明在无额外知识情况下攻击者是不可识别的（多个潜在攻击者可能执行相同攻击），然后通过框架解决这一挑战。框架可应用于多种学习器，帮助防御者进行外部缓解（改变决策系统或限制攻击者能力）和内部优化（改进对抗性正则化等防御方法）。

Result: 证明了攻击者的非可识别性，提出了解决该问题的框架，并通过多种学习器的具体实例展示了框架的适用性。框架能够帮助防御者基于攻击者特征采取更有效的防御策略。

Conclusion: 通过识别攻击者特征而非仅仅防御攻击本身，可以显著提升机器学习系统在对抗性环境中的防御能力。提出的框架为防御者提供了新的视角和工具，能够同时支持系统外部缓解和算法内部优化两种防御策略。

Abstract: When used in automated decision-making systems, machine learning (ML) models are vulnerable to data-manipulation attacks. Some defense mechanisms (e.g., adversarial regularization) directly affect the ML models while others (e.g., anomaly detection) act within the broader system. In this paper we consider a different task for defending the adversary, focusing on the attacker, rather than the attack. We present and demonstrate a framework for identifying characteristics about the attacker from an observed attack.
  We prove that, without additional knowledge, the attacker is non-identifiable (multiple potential attackers would perform the same observed attack). To address this challenge, we propose a domain-agnostic framework to identify the most probable attacker. This framework aids the defender in two ways. First, knowledge about the attacker can be leveraged for exogenous mitigation (i.e., addressing the vulnerability by altering the decision-making system outside the learning algorithm and/or limiting the attacker's capability). Second, when implementing defense methods that directly affect the learning process (e.g., adversarial regularization), knowledge of the specific attacker improves performance. We present the details of our framework and illustrate its applicability through specific instantiations on a variety of learners.

</details>


### [66] [The Value of Graph-based Encoding in NBA Salary Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05671)
*Junhao Su,David Grimsman,Christopher Archibald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将知识图谱嵌入向量空间并整合到表格数据中，能显著提升NBA球员薪资预测的准确性，特别是对老将和高薪球员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NBA球员薪资预测面临挑战：年轻球员基于选秀顺位的预测效果良好，但老将和高薪球员的薪资受多种复杂因素影响，传统基于表格数据的监督学习方法难以准确预测。

Method: 构建包含场上和场外数据的知识图谱，使用多种图嵌入算法将图谱嵌入向量空间，然后将得到的向量整合到表格数据中，最后使用监督学习进行薪资预测。

Result: 实验表明，整合知识图谱嵌入向量的方法能显著提升NBA薪资预测的准确性，特别是对老将和高薪球员的预测效果改善明显。不同图嵌入算法的比较显示了该过程的重要性。

Conclusion: 将知识图谱嵌入向量空间并整合到表格数据中是提升NBA球员薪资预测准确性的有效方法，尤其适用于处理复杂因素影响下的老将和高薪球员薪资预测问题。

Abstract: Market valuations for professional athletes is a difficult problem, given the amount of variability in performance and location from year to year. In the National Basketball Association (NBA), a straightforward way to address this problem is to build a tabular data set and use supervised machine learning to predict a player's salary based on the player's performance in the previous year. For younger players, whose contracts are mostly built on draft position, this approach works well, however it can fail for veterans or those whose salaries are on the high tail of the distribution. In this paper, we show that building a knowledge graph with on and off court data, embedding that graph in a vector space, and including that vector in the tabular data allows the supervised learning to better understand the landscape of factors that affect salary. We compare several graph embedding algorithms and show that such a process is vital to NBA salary prediction.

</details>


### [67] [Improved Scaling Laws via Weak-to-Strong Generalization in Random Feature Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05691)
*Diyuan Wu,Lehan Chen,Theodor Misiakiewicz,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了弱到强泛化现象，发现当强学生模型在弱教师模型生成的标签上训练时，可以显著改善测试误差的缩放规律，甚至在某些情况下达到极小极大最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中常见使用学习模型标注数据，再用这些数据训练更强大的模型。弱到强泛化现象表明，在弱教师生成的标签上训练的强学生模型可以超越教师性能。本文旨在量化这种改进的潜力，特别是研究其对测试误差缩放规律的影响。

Method: 采用随机特征岭回归（RFRR）作为学生和教师模型。主要技术贡献是推导了在教师生成的标签上训练的学生模型的超额测试误差的确定性等价表达式。通过这个确定性等价，识别了学生缩放规律优于教师的机制。

Result: 研究发现学生模型的缩放规律可以显著优于教师模型，这种改进可以在偏置主导和方差主导的设置中实现。令人惊讶的是，无论教师的缩放规律如何，学生都可能达到极小极大最优速率，即使教师的测试误差不随样本量衰减。

Conclusion: 弱到强泛化现象具有实质性改进潜力，能够影响测试误差的缩放规律。学生模型可以在教师生成的标签上训练，达到比教师更好的性能，甚至在教师性能不随样本增长而改善的情况下，学生仍可能达到最优速率。

Abstract: It is increasingly common in machine learning to use learned models to label data and then employ such data to train more capable models. The phenomenon of weak-to-strong generalization exemplifies the advantage of this two-stage procedure: a strong student is trained on imperfect labels obtained from a weak teacher, and yet the strong student outperforms the weak teacher. In this paper, we show that the potential improvement is substantial, in the sense that it affects the scaling law followed by the test error. Specifically, we consider students and teachers trained via random feature ridge regression (RFRR). Our main technical contribution is to derive a deterministic equivalent for the excess test error of the student trained on labels obtained via the teacher. Via this deterministic equivalent, we then identify regimes in which the scaling law of the student improves upon that of the teacher, unveiling that the improvement can be achieved both in bias-dominated and variance-dominated settings. Strikingly, the student may attain the minimax optimal rate regardless of the scaling law of the teacher -- in fact, when the test error of the teacher does not even decay with the sample size.

</details>


### [68] [Revisiting the (Sub)Optimality of Best-of-N for Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05739)
*Ved Sriraman,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了Best-of-N采样方法，在更贴近实际实践的假设下证明其统计最优性，并提出能消除奖励攻击的改进变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Best-of-N采样在实践中被广泛使用，但近期理论研究认为它在统计上是次优的且容易受到奖励攻击。本文旨在在更贴近实际实践的假设下重新评估该方法，特别是关注胜率这一更符合奖励模型训练和评估实践的指标。

Method: 研究在更贴近实践的假设下分析Best-of-N采样：1）使用胜率而非期望真实奖励作为评估指标；2）在参考模型和奖励模型质量满足最小条件下分析；3）提出一个简单实用的变体来消除奖励攻击，同时保持最优统计性能。

Result: 证明在适当调优下，Best-of-N采样在计算和统计上都是最优的，能够实现高胜率，这解释了其广泛的实际成功。同时提出的改进变体能够可证明地消除奖励攻击，而先前的方法在考虑胜率时被证明是次优的。

Conclusion: Best-of-N采样在更贴近实践的假设下具有统计最优性，其提出的改进变体能够解决奖励攻击问题。研究强调了在分析推理时对齐方法时选择适当目标的重要性，胜率比期望真实奖励更适合作为评估指标。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is a widely used inference-time alignment method for language models, whereby N candidate responses are sampled from a reference model and the one with the highest predicted reward according to a learned reward model is selected. Despite its widespread practical use, recent theoretical work has suggested that it is statistically suboptimal and vulnerable to reward hacking, the process by which models exploit weaknesses in the learned reward model to achieve high estimated reward without genuinely improving performance. We revisit this question under assumptions that more closely reflect practice than that of prior work. In particular, in contradistinction to earlier analyses that focused on expected true reward, which may not be meaningful in many practical settings, we investigate how inference-time alignment affects the win-rate, a pairwise comparison-based metric more closely aligned with how reward models are trained and evaluated in practice. We demonstrate that, under minimal conditions on the quality of the reference model and learned reward model, properly tuned BoN is both computationally and statistically optimal in achieving high win-rate, partially explaining its widespread practical success. Because BoN remains susceptible to reward-hacking in this setting, we propose a simple and practical variant that provably eliminates reward-hacking while maintaining optimal statistical performance. Finally, we show that prior approaches are provably suboptimal when considering win-rate, highlighting the importance of choosing appropriate objectives when analyzing inference-time alignment methods.

</details>


### [69] [MIRACL: A Diverse Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Objective Multi-Echelon Combinatorial Supply Chain Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05760)
*Rifny Rachman,Josh Tingey,Richard Allmendinger,Wei Pan,Pradyumn Shukla,Bahrul Ilmi Nasution*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIRACL是一个分层元多目标强化学习框架，通过任务分解和帕累托适应策略实现少样本泛化，在供应链优化中优于传统MORL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标强化学习在动态环境中的部署受到任务特定重训练需求和高计算成本的限制，需要能够快速适应新任务的通用框架。

Method: 提出MIRACL框架：1）将任务分解为结构化子问题以实现高效策略适应；2）使用基于帕累托的适应策略在任务间元学习全局策略，促进元训练和微调中的多样性。

Result: 在简单到中等复杂度任务中，MIRACL优于传统MORL基线，实现高达10%的超体积提升和5%的期望效用改善。

Conclusion: MIRACL展示了在多目标问题中实现鲁棒高效适应的潜力，虽然是针对供应链领域验证，但理论上具有领域无关性，适用于更广泛的动态多目标决策问题。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is effective for multi-echelon combinatorial supply chain optimisation, where tasks involve high dimensionality, uncertainty, and competing objectives. However, its deployment in dynamic environments is hindered by the need for task-specific retraining and substantial computational cost. We introduce MIRACL (Meta multI-objective Reinforcement leArning with Composite Learning), a hierarchical Meta-MORL framework that allows for a few-shot generalisation across diverse tasks. MIRACL decomposes each task into structured subproblems for efficient policy adaptation and meta-learns a global policy across tasks using a Pareto-based adaptation strategy to encourage diversity in meta-training and fine-tuning. To our knowledge, this is the first integration of Meta-MORL with such mechanisms in combinatorial optimisation. Although validated in the supply chain domain, MIRACL is theoretically domain-agnostic and applicable to broader dynamic multi-objective decision-making problems. Empirical evaluations show that MIRACL outperforms conventional MORL baselines in simple to moderate tasks, achieving up to 10% higher hypervolume and 5% better expected utility. These results underscore the potential of MIRACL for robust, efficient adaptation in multi-objective problems.

</details>


### [70] [Score-Guided Proximal Projection: A Unified Geometric Framework for Rectified Flow Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05761)
*Vansh Bansal,James G Scott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP)框架，通过近端优化平衡输入保真度和预训练分数场的真实性，解决Rectified Flow模型在精确控制任务中的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rectified Flow模型虽然生成质量优秀，但在语义编辑、盲图像恢复等精确控制任务中存在挑战。现有方法分为两类：基于反转的引导方法存在"几何锁定"问题，严格遵循源轨迹；后验采样近似方法计算成本高且不稳定。需要一种统一框架来弥合确定性优化和随机采样之间的差距。

Method: 提出Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP)框架，将恢复任务重新表述为近端优化问题，定义了一个平衡输入保真度和预训练分数场真实性的能量景观。该方法具有正态收缩特性，能保证将分布外输入映射到数据流形上，并有效达到流形约束的后验模式。

Result: SGPP在理论上证明了正态收缩特性，几何上保证分布外输入被映射到数据流形。该方法推广了最先进的编辑方法：RF-inversion是其极限情况。通过放宽近端方差，SGPP实现了"软引导"，提供了在严格身份保持和生成自由度之间的连续、无需训练的权衡。

Conclusion: SGPP提供了一个统一框架，弥合了确定性优化和随机采样之间的差距，解决了Rectified Flow模型在精确控制任务中的局限性。该方法通过近端优化平衡保真度和真实性，具有理论保证和实际应用价值，为生成模型的精确控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Rectified Flow (RF) models achieve state-of-the-art generation quality, yet controlling them for precise tasks -- such as semantic editing or blind image recovery -- remains a challenge. Current approaches bifurcate into inversion-based guidance, which suffers from "geometric locking" by rigidly adhering to the source trajectory, and posterior sampling approximations (e.g., DPS), which are computationally expensive and unstable. In this work, we propose Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP), a unified framework that bridges the gap between deterministic optimization and stochastic sampling. We reformulate the recovery task as a proximal optimization problem, defining an energy landscape that balances fidelity to the input with realism from the pre-trained score field. We theoretically prove that this objective induces a normal contraction property, geometrically guaranteeing that out-of-distribution inputs are snapped onto the data manifold, and it effectively reaches the posterior mode constrained to the manifold. Crucially, we demonstrate that SGPP generalizes state-of-the-art editing methods: RF-inversion is effectively a limiting case of our framework. By relaxing the proximal variance, SGPP enables "soft guidance," offering a continuous, training-free trade-off between strict identity preservation and generative freedom.

</details>


### [71] [Tiny, Hardware-Independent, Compression-based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06359)
*Charles Meyers,Aaron MacSween,Erik Elmroth,Tommy Löfstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对客户端机器学习中的隐私保护需求，开发了基于归一化压缩距离的核方法，改进了训练效率，实现了在少量用户数据上高效运行的客户端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户隐私意识增强和监管加强，客户端数据存储与分析成为趋势，但现有机器学习方法需要大量标注数据和计算资源，不适合客户端设备。需要开发能在单个用户数据上高效运行、保护隐私的轻量级机器学习方法。

Method: 1) 证明归一化压缩距离不是度量；2) 将其扩展到核方法中以处理复杂数据；3) 提出改进训练时间的技术；4) 在分类任务中验证该方法，与现有度量和核函数比较。

Result: 归一化压缩距离在分类任务中表现与其他度量和核函数相当甚至更好，计算成本仅略高，且不依赖正式度量性质。该方法能在极少样本上训练出高精度模型，适合在客户端设备上仅使用用户数据运行。

Conclusion: 基于归一化压缩距离的核方法提供了一种有效的客户端机器学习解决方案，能在保护隐私的同时实现高性能，适合在计算受限的硬件上运行，平衡了隐私保护与模型性能的需求。

Abstract: The recent developments in machine learning have highlighted a conflict between online platforms and their users in terms of privacy. The importance of user privacy and the struggle for power over user data has been intensified as regulators and operators attempt to police online platforms. As users have become increasingly aware of privacy issues, client-side data storage, management, and analysis have become a favoured approach to large-scale centralised machine learning. However, state-of-the-art machine learning methods require vast amounts of labelled user data, making them unsuitable for models that reside client-side and only have access to a single user's data. State-of-the-art methods are also computationally expensive, which degrades the user experience on compute-limited hardware and also reduces battery life. A recent alternative approach has proven remarkably successful in classification tasks across a wide variety of data -- using a compression-based distance measure (called normalised compression distance) to measure the distance between generic objects in classical distance-based machine learning methods. In this work, we demonstrate that the normalised compression distance is actually not a metric; develop it for the wider context of kernel methods to allow modelling of complex data; and present techniques to improve the training time of models that use this distance measure. We demonstrate that the normalised compression distance works as well as and sometimes better than other metrics and kernels -- while requiring only marginally more computational costs and in spite of the lack of formal metric properties. The end results is a simple model with remarkable accuracy even when trained on a very small number of samples allowing for models that are small and effective enough to run entirely on a client device using only user-supplied data.

</details>


### [72] [Bridging Domains through Subspace-Aware Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05768)
*Levy Chaves,Chao Zhou,Rebekka Burkholz,Eduardo Valle,Sandra Avila*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCORE方法通过解决子空间冲突来提升模型合并的领域泛化能力，在多种架构和规模上优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型合并研究主要关注同分布或多任务场景，但在领域泛化方面的探索不足。本文旨在研究在不同领域上微调的模型合并后对未见领域的泛化能力

Method: 提出SCORE方法：通过奇异值分解分析任务矩阵中的参数竞争，计算所有模型主要奇异向量的主成分来找到共享正交基，然后将每个任务矩阵投影到共享基中并修剪非对角分量以消除冲突的奇异方向

Result: SCORE在领域泛化设置中平均优于现有模型合并方法，在多种架构和模型规模上表现一致，证明了其有效性和可扩展性

Conclusion: 在不同分布偏移下训练的模型合并会产生比传统多任务设置更强的子空间冲突，SCORE通过解决这些冲突显著提升了合并模型在未见领域的泛化性能

Abstract: Model merging integrates multiple task-specific models into a single consolidated one. Recent research has made progress in improving merging performance for in-distribution or multi-task scenarios, but domain generalization in model merging remains underexplored. We investigate how merging models fine-tuned on distinct domains affects generalization to unseen domains. Through an analysis of parameter competition in the task matrix using singular value decomposition, we show that merging models trained under different distribution shifts induces stronger conflicts between their subspaces compared to traditional multi-task settings. To mitigate this issue, we propose SCORE (Subspace COnflict-Resolving mErging), a method designed to alleviate such singular subspace conflicts. SCORE finds a shared orthogonal basis by computing the principal components of the concatenated leading singular vectors of all models. It then projects each task matrix into the shared basis, pruning off-diagonal components to remove conflicting singular directions. SCORE consistently outperforms, on average, existing model merging approaches in domain generalization settings across a variety of architectures and model scales, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [73] [First-Order Softmax Weighted Switching Gradient Method for Distributed Stochastic Minimax Optimization with Stochastic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05774)
*Zhankun Luo,Antesh Upadhyay,Sang Bin Moon,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于联邦学习的Softmax-Weighted Switching Gradient方法，解决带随机约束的分布式随机极小极大优化问题，在完全和部分客户端参与下均提供理论保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原始-对偶或基于惩罚的方法存在超参数敏感性和收敛振荡问题，需要为联邦学习中的分布式随机约束极小极大优化提供更稳定的替代方案

Method: 提出单循环仅原始切换机制（Softmax-Weighted Switching Gradient），通过随机优势假设量化客户端采样噪声，放宽目标函数的有界性假设，建立更严格的softmax超参数下界

Result: 完全参与下达到标准$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$oracle复杂度，部分参与下建立$\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}δ)$高概率收敛保证，在NP分类和公平分类任务上验证有效性

Conclusion: 单循环仅原始切换机制为优化最坏情况客户端性能提供了稳定替代方案，有效规避了传统方法的超参数敏感性和收敛振荡问题

Abstract: This paper addresses the distributed stochastic minimax optimization problem subject to stochastic constraints. We propose a novel first-order Softmax-Weighted Switching Gradient method tailored for federated learning. Under full client participation, our algorithm achieves the standard $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ oracle complexity to satisfy a unified bound $ε$ for both the optimality gap and feasibility tolerance. We extend our theoretical analysis to the practical partial participation regime by quantifying client sampling noise through a stochastic superiority assumption. Furthermore, by relaxing standard boundedness assumptions on the objective functions, we establish a strictly tighter lower bound for the softmax hyperparameter. We provide a unified error decomposition and establish a sharp $\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}δ)$ high-probability convergence guarantee. Ultimately, our framework demonstrates that a single-loop primal-only switching mechanism provides a stable alternative for optimizing worst-case client performance, effectively bypassing the hyperparameter sensitivity and convergence oscillations often encountered in traditional primal-dual or penalty-based approaches. We verify the efficacy of our algorithm via experiment on the Neyman-Pearson (NP) classification and fair classification tasks.

</details>


### [74] [Sparse Crosscoders for diffing MoEs and Dense models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05805)
*Marmik Chaudhari,Nishkal Hundia,Idhant Gulati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过交叉编码器分析MoE与稠密模型内部表示差异，发现MoE学习更少但更专业化的特征，而稠密模型特征更广泛通用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE通过稀疏专家路由实现参数高效扩展，但其内部表示相比稠密模型仍缺乏深入理解，需要系统比较两者的内部特征组织差异

Method: 使用交叉编码器（稀疏自编码器变体）联合建模多个激活空间，在代码、科学文本和英文故事上训练5层稠密和MoE模型（相等活跃参数），采用BatchTopK交叉编码器并显式指定共享特征

Result: 达到约87%的分数方差解释率，发现MoE学习显著更少的独特特征；MoE特定特征比共享特征具有更高的激活密度，而稠密特定特征显示较低密度

Conclusion: MoE发展出更专业化、聚焦的表示，而稠密模型在更广泛、更通用的特征间分布信息，揭示了两种架构在特征组织上的根本差异

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) achieve parameter-efficient scaling through sparse expert routing, yet their internal representations remain poorly understood compared to dense models. We present a systematic comparison of MoE and dense model internals using crosscoders, a variant of sparse autoencoders, that jointly models multiple activation spaces. We train 5-layer dense and MoEs (equal active parameters) on 1B tokens across code, scientific text, and english stories. Using BatchTopK crosscoders with explicitly designated shared features, we achieve $\sim 87\%$ fractional variance explained and uncover concrete differences in feature organization. The MoE learns significantly fewer unique features compared to the dense model. MoE-specific features also exhibit higher activation density than shared features, whereas dense-specific features show lower density. Our analysis reveals that MoEs develop more specialized, focused representations while dense models distribute information across broader, more general-purpose features.

</details>


### [75] [MoE Lens -- An Expert Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05806)
*Marmik Chaudhari,Idhant Gulati,Nishkal Hundia,Pranav Karra,Shivam Raval*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE模型通过稀疏专家激活实现参数高效扩展，但对其专业化行为的理解有限。研究发现DeepSeekMoE模型主要依赖少数专业化专家，单个顶级专家的输出与完整集成预测高度接近，为推理优化提供了机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）模型通过稀疏专家激活实现参数高效扩展，但对其推理和内存成本的优化仍然具有挑战性，主要原因是缺乏对其专业化行为的深入理解。需要系统分析MoE模型中的专家专业化模式，以探索推理优化的可能性。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：1）领域特定的路由模式分析；2）早期解码框架，跟踪专家对输出表示的贡献。对DeepSeekMoE模型进行系统分析，包括令牌路由分布的系统性分析，以及单专家与集成专家隐藏状态相似性的量化验证。

Result: 研究发现：1）尽管模型有64个路由专家（每层激活6个），但主要依赖少数专业化专家；2）顶级权重专家的输出与完整集成预测高度接近；3）极少数专家处理超过50%的路由决策；4）单专家与集成专家的隐藏状态相似性极高（某些层余弦相似度达0.95）；5）在所有三个领域使用单一专家时，困惑度仅增加5%。

Conclusion: 混合专家模型表现出集中的专业知识分布，这为通过有针对性的专家剪枝进行推理优化提供了机会，同时保持模型性能。研究结果为研究这些模型中学习知识的定位开辟了新途径，表明可以在不显著影响性能的情况下优化MoE模型的推理效率。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) models enable parameter-efficient scaling through sparse expert activations, yet optimizing their inference and memory costs remains challenging due to limited understanding of their specialization behavior. We present a systematic analysis of expert specialization in MoEs through two complementary approaches: domain-specific routing patterns and an early decoding framework that tracks expert contributions to output representations. Our analysis of the DeepSeekMoE model reveals that despite having 64 routed experts with 6 active for each layer's computation, the model predominantly relies on a few specialized experts, with the top-weighted expert's output closely approximating the full ensemble prediction. We quantitatively validate these findings through a systematic analysis of the token routing distribution, demonstrating that very few experts handle over 50\% of routing decisions across different specialized domains. Hidden state similarity between single and ensemble experts for every layer is extremely high, with some layers having cosine similarity as high as 0.95 and perplexity increasing by only 5\% when using a single expert across all three domains. Our results indicate that Mixture of Experts models exhibit concentrated expertise highlighting potential opportunities for inference optimization through targeted expert pruning while maintaining model performance and opening avenues towards studying localization of learned knowledge in these models.

</details>


### [76] [Self-Auditing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Few-Shot 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05822)
*Son Thai Ly,Hien V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEA-PEFT：一种自动化参数高效微调方法，通过搜索-审计-分配循环动态配置适配器，在少样本3D医学图像分割中显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型适应新临床站点面临挑战：领域偏移、标注稀缺，且许多临床团队缺乏AI工程师来手动调整适配器设计和训练方案，导致适应周期长达数周至数月。现有PEFT方法要么需要手动配置，要么自动化搜索在少样本3D场景下计算不可行。

Method: SEA-PEFT将适配器配置视为在线分配问题，在微调过程中动态解决。采用搜索-审计-分配循环：训练活跃适配器，通过暂时关闭每个适配器来估计其Dice效用，然后使用贪心背包分配器在参数预算下重新选择活跃集。结合指数移动平均、四分位距平滑和有限状态排名控制器来稳定循环，提高高噪声少样本场景下的可靠性。

Result: 在TotalSegmentator和FLARE'22数据集上，SEA-PEFT在1/5/10-shot设置下，相比最强的固定拓扑PEFT基线，平均Dice提升了2.4-2.8个点，同时训练参数少于1%。
