Conclusion: SEA-PEFT通过自动化适配器配置解决了临床站点适应的实际挑战，显著减少了人工干预需求，在少样本3D医学图像分割中实现了更好的性能，同时保持参数高效性。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models to new clinical sites remains challenging in practice. Domain shift and scarce annotations must be handled by experts, yet many clinical groups do not have ready access to skilled AI engineers to tune adapter designs and training recipes. As a result, adaptation cycles can stretch from weeks to months, particularly in few-shot settings. Existing PEFT methods either require manual adapter configuration or automated searches that are computationally infeasible in few-shot 3D settings. We propose SEA-PEFT (SElf-Auditing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning) to automate this process. SEA-PEFT treats adapter configuration as an online allocation problem solved during fine-tuning rather than through manual, fixed-topology choices. SEA-PEFT uses a search-audit-allocate loop that trains active adapters, estimates each adapter's Dice utility by momentarily toggling it off, and then reselects the active set under a parameter budget using a greedy knapsack allocator. Exponential Moving Average and Interquartile Range smoothing, together with a Finite-State Ranking controller, stabilize the loop and improve reliability in high-noise few-shot regimes. On TotalSegmentator and FLARE'22, SEA-PEFT improves mean Dice by 2.4--2.8 points over the strongest fixed-topology PEFT baselines across 1/5/10-shot settings while training <1% of parameters. For reproducibility purposes, we made our code publicly available at https://github.com/tsly123/SEA_PEFT

</details>


### [77] [Test-Time Adaptation via Many-Shot Prompting: Benefits, Limits, and Pitfalls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05829)
*Shubhangi Upasani,Chen Wu,Jay Rainton,Bo Li,Changran Hu,Qizheng Zhang,Urmish Thakker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文实证研究了大语言模型中的多示例提示测试时适应方法，分析了其效果、局限性和替代策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时适应使大语言模型能够在推理时调整行为而无需更新参数，多示例提示是常见方法，但其可靠性和限制尚未被充分理解，特别是对于开源模型。

Method: 通过实证研究分析多示例提示在不同任务和模型架构中的表现，考察更新规模、示例顺序和选择策略的影响，并研究动态ICL和强化ICL作为替代测试时更新策略。

Result: 多示例提示在结构化任务中有效，特别是当演示提供高信息增益时，但对选择策略高度敏感，在开放生成任务中效果有限。动态和强化ICL能控制信息注入和约束模型行为。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了基于提示的测试时适应的实际限制，明确了输入空间更新在何时有益或有害，为实际应用提供了指导。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation enables large language models (LLMs) to modify their behavior at inference without updating model parameters. A common approach is many-shot prompting, where large numbers of in-context learning (ICL) examples are injected as an input-space test-time update. Although performance can improve as more demonstrations are added, the reliability and limits of this update mechanism remain poorly understood, particularly for open-source models. We present an empirical study of many-shot prompting across tasks and model backbones, analyzing how performance varies with update magnitude, example ordering, and selection policy. We further study Dynamic and Reinforced ICL as alternative test-time update strategies that control which information is injected and how it constrains model behavior. We find that many-shot prompting is effective for structured tasks where demonstrations provide high information gain, but is highly sensitive to selection strategy and often shows limited benefits for open-ended generation tasks. Overall, we characterize the practical limits of prompt-based test-time adaptation and outline when input-space updates are beneficial versus harmful.

</details>


### [78] [Reference-guided Policy Optimization for Molecular Optimization via LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05900)
*Xuan Li,Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Jiangchao Yao,Yu Rong,Lu Zhang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RePO是一种用于分子优化的新方法，通过结合强化学习和参考指导，解决了传统SFT和RLVR在缺乏轨迹数据时的局限性，实现了更好的探索-利用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督微调(SFT)和带可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)在分子优化任务中表现不佳，因为每个数据点通常只提供一个优化的参考分子，缺乏逐步优化轨迹。仅基于答案的SFT会导致推理能力崩溃，而RLVR在相似性约束下由于模型缺乏有效探索而提供稀疏反馈，限制了优化效果。

Method: 提出Reference-guided Policy Optimization (RePO)方法：1) 从模型中采样候选分子及其中间推理轨迹；2) 使用可验证奖励在强化学习框架下训练模型，奖励衡量在相似性约束下的属性满足程度；3) 应用参考指导，将策略的中间推理轨迹作为上下文，仅以监督方式训练答案部分。RL项促进探索，指导项通过将输出锚定到参考来缓解奖励稀疏性并稳定训练。

Result: 在分子优化基准测试中，RePO持续优于SFT和RLVR基线（如GRPO），在优化指标（成功率×相似性）上取得改进，在竞争目标间实现更好平衡，并对未见指令风格具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RePO通过结合强化学习的探索能力和参考指导的稳定性，有效解决了分子优化中缺乏轨迹数据的问题，实现了更好的探索-利用平衡，为基于指令的分子优化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) benefit substantially from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) in reasoning tasks. However, these recipes perform poorly in instruction-based molecular optimization, where each data point typically provides only a single optimized reference molecule and no step-by-step optimization trajectory. We reveal that answer-only SFT on the reference molecules collapses reasoning, and RLVR provides sparse feedback under similarity constraints due to the model's lack of effective exploration, which slows learning and limits optimization. To encourage the exploration of new molecules while balancing the exploitation of the reference molecules, we introduce Reference-guided Policy Optimization (RePO), an optimization approach that learns from reference molecules without requiring trajectory data. At each update, RePO samples candidate molecules with their intermediate reasoning trajectories from the model and trains the model using verifiable rewards that measure property satisfaction under similarity constraints in an RL manner. Meanwhile, it applies reference guidance by keeping the policy's intermediate reasoning trajectory as context and training only the answer in a supervised manner. Together, the RL term promotes exploration, while the guidance term mitigates reward sparsity and stabilizes training by grounding outputs to references when many valid molecular edits exist. Across molecular optimization benchmarks, RePO consistently outperforms SFT and RLVR baselines (e.g., GRPO), achieving improvements on the optimization metric (Success Rate $\times$ Similarity), improving balance across competing objectives, and generalizing better to unseen instruction styles. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/RePO.

</details>


### [79] [Stock Market Prediction Using Node Transformer Architecture Integrated with BERT Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05917)
*Mohammad Al Ridhawi,Mahtab Haj Ali,Hussein Al Osman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个结合节点Transformer架构和BERT情感分析的集成框架，用于股票价格预测，在S&P 500股票上实现了0.80%的MAPE，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票市场预测面临噪声、非平稳性和行为动态等复杂挑战，传统方法难以捕捉金融市场中的复杂模式和横截面依赖关系。

Method: 提出集成框架：1) 将股票市场表示为图结构（节点为股票，边捕捉行业关联、价格相关性和供应链连接）；2) 使用微调BERT模型从社交媒体提取情感特征；3) 通过注意力机制融合情感和量化市场特征；4) 节点Transformer处理历史市场数据，捕捉时间演化和股票间横截面依赖。

Result: 在1982年1月至2025年3月的20只S&P 500股票上测试：集成模型一日前预测MAPE为0.80%，优于ARIMA(1.20%)和LSTM(1.00%)。情感分析使预测误差总体降低10%，财报期间降低25%；图建模通过捕捉股票间依赖关系带来额外15%改进。方向准确率达65%，高波动期MAPE保持在1.5%以下（基准模型超过2%）。统计检验确认改进显著(p<0.05)。

Conclusion: 集成节点Transformer和BERT情感分析的框架能有效捕捉股票市场的复杂动态和依赖关系，显著提升预测精度，特别是在高波动和事件驱动时期，为金融预测提供了更强大的方法。

Abstract: Stock market prediction presents considerable challenges for investors, financial institutions, and policymakers operating in complex market environments characterized by noise, non-stationarity, and behavioral dynamics. Traditional forecasting methods often fail to capture the intricate patterns and cross-sectional dependencies inherent in financial markets. This paper presents an integrated framework combining a node transformer architecture with BERT-based sentiment analysis for stock price forecasting. The proposed model represents the stock market as a graph structure where individual stocks form nodes and edges capture relationships including sectoral affiliations, correlated price movements, and supply chain connections. A fine-tuned BERT model extracts sentiment from social media posts and combines it with quantitative market features through attention-based fusion. The node transformer processes historical market data while capturing both temporal evolution and cross-sectional dependencies among stocks. Experiments on 20 S&P 500 stocks spanning January 1982 to March 2025 demonstrate that the integrated model achieves a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 0.80% for one-day-ahead predictions, compared to 1.20% for ARIMA and 1.00% for LSTM. Sentiment analysis reduces prediction error by 10% overall and 25% during earnings announcements, while graph-based modeling contributes an additional 15% improvement by capturing inter-stock dependencies. Directional accuracy reaches 65% for one-day forecasts. Statistical validation through paired t-tests confirms these improvements (p < 0.05 for all comparisons). The model maintains MAPE below 1.5% during high-volatility periods where baseline models exceed 2%.

</details>


### [80] [Design Experiments to Compare Multi-armed Bandit Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05919)
*Huiling Meng,Ningyuan Chen,Xuefeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Artificial Replay（AR）作为多臂老虎机算法比较的新实验设计方法，通过重用第一个策略的轨迹记录，在比较两个策略时只需T+o(T)次用户交互而非2T次，显著降低实验成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台需要比较UCB、Thompson Sampling等多臂老虎机算法，但传统方法需要多次独立重启算法，产生大量用户交互成本。每个算法在T个用户上运行只产生一个依赖轨迹，因为算法决策依赖于所有历史交互。可靠推断需要多次独立重启，导致实验成本高昂且部署决策延迟。

Method: 提出Artificial Replay（AR）实验设计：首先运行第一个策略并记录其轨迹；当执行第二个策略时，如果它选择了第一个策略已经采取过的动作，则重用记录的奖励；否则才查询真实环境。开发了新的分析框架来证明该设计的三个关键特性。

Result: 理论证明：AR估计器是无偏的；只需T+o(T)次用户交互而非2T次，当两个策略都有亚线性遗憾时几乎减半实验成本；方差随T亚线性增长，而朴素设计的估计器方差线性增长。数值实验使用UCB、Thompson Sampling和ε-greedy策略验证了这些理论优势。

Conclusion: Artificial Replay为多臂老虎机算法比较提供了一种高效、低成本的实验设计方法，通过重用轨迹记录显著减少所需用户交互次数，同时保证估计的无偏性和亚线性方差增长，为在线平台的算法评估和部署决策提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Online platforms routinely compare multi-armed bandit algorithms, such as UCB and Thompson Sampling, to select the best-performing policy. Unlike standard A/B tests for static treatments, each run of a bandit algorithm over $T$ users produces only one dependent trajectory, because the algorithm's decisions depend on all past interactions. Reliable inference therefore demands many independent restarts of the algorithm, making experimentation costly and delaying deployment decisions. We propose Artificial Replay (AR) as a new experimental design for this problem. AR first runs one policy and records its trajectory. When the second policy is executed, it reuses a recorded reward whenever it selects an action the first policy already took, and queries the real environment only otherwise. We develop a new analytical framework for this design and prove three key properties of the resulting estimator: it is unbiased; it requires only $T + o(T)$ user interactions instead of $2T$ for a run of the treatment and control policies, nearly halving the experimental cost when both policies have sub-linear regret; and its variance grows sub-linearly in $T$, whereas the estimator from a naïve design has a linearly-growing variance. Numerical experiments with UCB, Thompson Sampling, and $ε$-greedy policies confirm these theoretical gains.

</details>


### [81] [Omni-Masked Gradient Descent: Memory-Efficient Optimization via Mask Traversal with Improved Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05960)
*Hui Yang,Tao Ren,Jinyang Jiang,Wan Tian,Yijie Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMGD是一种基于掩码遍历的内存高效优化方法，在非凸设置下实现了改进的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$迭代复杂度，优于标准的${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模语言模型全参数训练中的GPU内存瓶颈问题，现有方法要么缺乏明确的收敛保证，要么在非凸设置下只能达到标准的${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$迭代复杂度。

Method: 提出Omni-Masked Gradient Descent (OMGD)，一种基于掩码遍历的优化方法，通过选择性更新参数来减少内存使用，可以无缝集成到主流优化器中。

Result: 理论分析证明OMGD在非凸设置下达到了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$的改进迭代复杂度，经验验证显示在微调和预训练任务中相比竞争基线有持续改进。

Conclusion: OMGD是一种轻量级、即插即用的内存高效优化方法，具有更好的收敛保证，能够有效缓解大规模语言模型训练中的内存瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Memory-efficient optimization methods have recently gained increasing attention for scaling full-parameter training of large language models under the GPU-memory bottleneck. Existing approaches either lack clear convergence guarantees, or only achieve the standard ${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ iteration complexity in the nonconvex settings. We propose Omni-Masked Gradient Descent (OMGD), an optimization method based on mask traversal for memory efficient training, and provide a nonconvex convergence analysis that establishes a strictly improved iteration complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$ for finding an $ε$-approximate stationary point. Empirically, OMGD is a lightweight, plug-and-play approach that integrates seamlessly into most mainstream optimizers, yielding consistent improvements over competitive baselines in both fine-tuning and pre-training tasks.

</details>


### [82] [Preventing Learning Stagnation in PPO by Scaling to 1 Million Parallel Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06009)
*Michael Beukman,Khimya Khetarpal,Zeyu Zheng,Will Dabney,Jakob Foerster,Michael Dennis,Clare Lyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPO算法在深度在线策略强化学习中常出现性能停滞问题，研究发现这是由于样本估计损失逐渐偏离真实目标，可通过调整外循环步长与噪声比例解决，大规模并行环境（超100万）能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO等深度在线策略强化学习算法常出现性能停滞问题，传统归因于探索、容量或优化挑战，但本文发现根本原因是样本估计损失在训练过程中逐渐偏离真实目标，导致学习停滞。

Method: 将PPO外循环建模为随机优化问题，分析步长（由正则化强度控制）与梯度噪声（由样本数量决定）的关系。提出通过减少步长或增加更新间样本数来解决停滞，并研究超参数对这两个因素的影响。

Result: 验证了模型预测，发现增加并行环境数量能同时减少步长和更新噪声。提出超参数协同缩放方案，避免性能下降。在复杂开放域中，将PPO扩展到超100万并行环境，实现单调性能提升至1万亿转移。

Conclusion: PPO性能停滞源于样本估计损失偏离真实目标，可通过调整外循环步长与噪声比例解决。大规模并行化是有效策略，但需正确协同缩放超参数。该方法在复杂任务中显著超越先前基线。

Abstract: Plateaus, where an agent's performance stagnates at a suboptimal level, are a common problem in deep on-policy RL. Focusing on PPO due to its widespread adoption, we show that plateaus in certain regimes arise not because of known exploration, capacity, or optimization challenges, but because sample-based estimates of the loss eventually become poor proxies for the true objective over the course of training. As a recap, PPO switches between sampling rollouts from several parallel environments online using the current policy (which we call the outer loop) and performing repeated minibatch SGD steps against this offline dataset (the inner loop). In our work we consider only the outer loop, and conceptually model it as stochastic optimization. The step size is then controlled by the regularization strength towards the previous policy and the gradient noise by the number of samples collected between policy update steps. This model predicts that performance will plateau at a suboptimal level if the outer step size is too large relative to the noise. Recasting PPO in this light makes it clear that there are two ways to address this particular type of learning stagnation: either reduce the step size or increase the number of samples collected between updates. We first validate the predictions of our model and investigate how hyperparameter choices influence the step size and update noise, concluding that increasing the number of parallel environments is a simple and robust way to reduce both factors. Next, we propose a recipe for how to co-scale the other hyperparameters when increasing parallelization, and show that incorrectly doing so can lead to severe performance degradation. Finally, we vastly outperform prior baselines in a complex open-ended domain by scaling PPO to more than 1M parallel environments, thereby enabling monotonic performance improvement up to one trillion transitions.

</details>


### [83] [Agnostic learning in (almost) optimal time via Gaussian surface area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06027)
*Lucas Pesenti,Lucas Slot,Manuel Wiedmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了Klivans等人(2008)关于高斯分布下概念类L1多项式逼近的度界，将所需度数从O(Γ²/ε⁴)降低到Õ(Γ²/ε²)，从而在统计查询模型中获得了多项式阈值函数近似学习的最优复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯分布下概念类的学习复杂度与其低次多项式的L1逼近能力密切相关。Klivans等人(2008)证明了对于高斯表面积至多为Γ的概念类，需要度数d=O(Γ²/ε⁴)才能达到ε近似。本文旨在改进这一分析以获得更紧的度界。

Method: 采用Feldman等人(2020)在布尔超立方体上L1逼近构造的直接类比方法，通过改进分析技术来降低所需的多项式度数。

Result: 证明了度数d=Õ(Γ²/ε²)足以实现ε近似，相比之前的O(Γ²/ε⁴)有显著改进。结合Diakonikolas等人(2021)的下界，这一结果在统计查询模型中为多项式阈值函数的近似学习提供了（接近）最优的复杂度界。

Conclusion: 本文改进了高斯分布下概念类L1多项式逼近的度界分析，将所需度数从四次方依赖降低到二次方依赖，从而在统计查询模型中获得了多项式阈值函数近似学习的（接近）最优复杂度结果。

Abstract: The complexity of learning a concept class under Gaussian marginals in the difficult agnostic model is closely related to its $L_1$-approximability by low-degree polynomials. For any concept class with Gaussian surface area at most $Γ$, Klivans et al. (2008) show that degree $d = O(Γ^2 / \varepsilon^4)$ suffices to achieve an $\varepsilon$-approximation. This leads to the best-known bounds on the complexity of learning a variety of concept classes. In this note, we improve their analysis by showing that degree $d = \tilde O (Γ^2 / \varepsilon^2)$ is enough. In light of lower bounds due to Diakonikolas et al. (2021), this yields (near) optimal bounds on the complexity of agnostically learning polynomial threshold functions in the statistical query model. Our proof relies on a direct analogue of a construction of Feldman et al. (2020), who considered $L_1$-approximation on the Boolean hypercube.

</details>


### [84] [Improved high-dimensional estimation with Langevin dynamics and stochastic weight averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06028)
*Stanley Wei,Alex Damian,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Langevin动力学通过迭代平均可以在n≳d^{k*/2}样本下成功恢复隐藏方向，无需显式平滑，噪声注入与迭代平均的组合能模拟景观平滑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明梯度下降需要n≳d^{max(1,k*-1)}样本恢复隐藏方向，而显式平滑可将样本需求降至n≳d^{max(1,k*/2)}。本文旨在探索是否能在不显式平滑的情况下达到相同速率。

Method: 采用Langevin动力学（噪声注入的梯度下降），但使用平均迭代而非最终迭代。理论分析表明噪声注入与迭代平均的组合能模拟景观平滑效果，从而降低样本复杂度。

Result: 证明Langevin动力学在平均迭代下能以n≳d^{k*/2}样本成功恢复隐藏方向，该结果应用于张量PCA和单指标模型设置，并推测小批量SGD也能达到相同速率。

Conclusion: 通过噪声注入与迭代平均的组合，无需显式平滑即可实现最优样本复杂度，为高维统计学习中的优化算法设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: Significant recent work has studied the ability of gradient descent to recover a hidden planted direction $θ^\star \in S^{d-1}$ in different high-dimensional settings, including tensor PCA and single-index models. The key quantity that governs the ability of gradient descent to traverse these landscapes is the information exponent $k^\star$ (Ben Arous et al., (2021)), which corresponds to the order of the saddle at initialization in the population landscape. Ben Arous et al., (2021) showed that $n \gtrsim d^{\max(1, k^\star-1)}$ samples were necessary and sufficient for online SGD to recover $θ^\star$, and Ben Arous et al., (2020) proved a similar lower bound for Langevin dynamics. More recently, Damian et al., (2023) showed it was possible to circumvent these lower bounds by running gradient descent on a smoothed landscape, and that this algorithm succeeds with $n \gtrsim d^{\max(1, k^\star/2)}$ samples, which is optimal in the worst case. This raises the question of whether it is possible to achieve the same rate without explicit smoothing. In this paper, we show that Langevin dynamics can succeed with $n \gtrsim d^{ k^\star/2 }$ samples if one considers the average iterate, rather than the last iterate. The key idea is that the combination of noise-injection and iterate averaging is able to emulate the effect of landscape smoothing. We apply this result to both the tensor PCA and single-index model settings. Finally, we conjecture that minibatch SGD can also achieve the same rate without adding any additional noise.

</details>


### [85] [Dynamic Momentum Recalibration in Online Gradient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06120)
*Zhipeng Yao,Rui Yu,Guisong Chang,Ying Li,Yu Zhang,Dazhou Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SGDF优化器，通过信号处理视角重新解释梯度更新，使用最优线性滤波原理动态调整增益，实现噪声抑制与信号保留的最佳平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD及其动量变体是深度学习优化的核心，但其梯度行为的底层动力学机制理解不足。固定动量系数会固有地扭曲偏差与方差的平衡，导致参数更新偏斜或次优

Method: 提出SGDF（带滤波的SGD）优化器，基于最优线性滤波原理，计算在线时变增益来动态优化梯度估计，通过最小化均方误差实现噪声抑制与信号保留的最佳权衡

Result: 在多种架构和基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SGDF超越了传统动量方法，性能达到或超过了最先进的优化器

Conclusion: SGDF通过信号处理视角重新解释梯度更新，利用最优线性滤波动态调整增益，实现了比固定动量系数更优的偏差-方差权衡，且该方法可扩展到其他优化器框架

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) and its momentum variants form the backbone of deep learning optimization, yet the underlying dynamics of their gradient behavior remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we reinterpret gradient updates through the lens of signal processing and reveal that fixed momentum coefficients inherently distort the balance between bias and variance, leading to skewed or suboptimal parameter updates. To address this, we propose SGDF (SGD with Filter), an optimizer inspired by the principles of Optimal Linear Filtering. SGDF computes an online, time-varying gain to dynamically refine gradient estimation by minimizing the mean-squared error, thereby achieving an optimal trade-off between noise suppression and signal preservation. Furthermore, our approach could extend to other optimizers, showcasing its broad applicability to optimization frameworks. Extensive experiments across diverse architectures and benchmarks demonstrate SGDF surpasses conventional momentum methods and achieves performance on par with or surpassing state-of-the-art optimizers.

</details>


### [86] [DQE: A Semantic-Aware Evaluation Metric for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06131)
*Yuewei Li,Dalin Zhang,Huan Li,Xinyi Gong,Hongjun Chu,Zhaohui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的时间序列异常检测评估指标，解决了现有指标在点级覆盖偏差、近漏检测不敏感、误报惩罚不足和阈值选择不一致等方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测评估指标存在四个主要问题：1)偏向点级覆盖；2)对近漏检测不敏感或不一致；3)对误报惩罚不足；4)阈值或阈值区间选择导致的不一致性。这些问题导致评估结果不可靠或反直觉，阻碍了客观进展。

Method: 首先基于检测语义引入分区策略，将每个异常的局部时间区域分解为三个功能不同的子区域。利用这种分区，评估跨事件的整体检测行为，并为每个子区域设计更细粒度的评分机制。通过系统研究现有指标，识别与阈值区间选择相关的评估偏差，采用在完整阈值谱上聚合检测质量的方法，消除评估不一致性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据上的大量实验表明，该指标提供了稳定、可区分且可解释的评估，与十种广泛使用的指标相比实现了鲁棒评估。

Conclusion: 该论文从检测语义的角度重新审视时间序列异常检测评估，提出了一种更全面的评估指标，解决了现有评估实践的局限性，为时间序列异常检测领域提供了更可靠和可解释的评估框架。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, evaluation practices have received comparatively less attention, despite their critical importance. Existing metrics exhibit several limitations: (1) bias toward point-level coverage, (2) insensitivity or inconsistency in near-miss detections, (3) inadequate penalization of false alarms, and (4) inconsistency caused by threshold or threshold-interval selection. These limitations can produce unreliable or counterintuitive results, hindering objective progress. In this work, we revisit the evaluation of time series anomaly detection from the perspective of detection semantics and propose a novel metric for more comprehensive assessment. We first introduce a partitioning strategy grounded in detection semantics, which decomposes the local temporal region of each anomaly into three functionally distinct subregions. Using this partitioning, we evaluate overall detection behavior across events and design finer-grained scoring mechanisms for each subregion, enabling more reliable and interpretable assessment. Through a systematic study of existing metrics, we identify an evaluation bias associated with threshold-interval selection and adopt an approach that aggregates detection qualities across the full threshold spectrum, thereby eliminating evaluation inconsistency. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that our metric provides stable, discriminative, and interpretable evaluation, while achieving robust assessment compared with ten widely used metrics.

</details>


### [87] [Partial Policy Gradients for RL in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06138)
*Puneet Mathur,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Viet Dac Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在策略梯度中建模策略结构的新方法，通过优化未来奖励的子集来学习更简单可靠的策略，并在多个人格对齐对话问题上进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习是在未知环境中顺序学习行动决策的框架。现有方法在处理复杂策略时，梯度估计可能不够准确，导致学习不稳定。需要一种能够建模不同策略结构并比较其性能的方法，特别是在人格对齐对话等复杂任务中。

Method: 提出在策略梯度中建模策略结构的自然方法：通过优化未来奖励的子集来学习策略。较小的奖励子集代表更简单的策略，其经验梯度估计更准确，学习更可靠。该方法能够建模和比较多种策略类别，包括完全规划、贪婪策略、K步前瞻和分段策略。

Result: 在多个人格对齐对话问题上进行了实证评估。不同策略在不同问题上表现出色，反映了它们各自的特点。结果表明，策略类别的选择对性能有重要影响，没有单一策略在所有问题上都最优。

Conclusion: 通过优化未来奖励子集来建模策略结构的方法有效，能够学习更可靠的策略。不同策略类别在不同问题上的表现差异强调了研究策略类别的重要性。该方法为强化学习中的策略设计提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a framework for learning to act sequentially in an unknown environment. We propose a natural approach for modeling policy structure in policy gradients. The key idea is to optimize for a subset of future rewards: smaller subsets represent simpler policies, which can be learned more reliably because their empirical gradient estimates are more accurate. Our approach allows for modeling and comparison of different policy classes, including full planning, greedy, K-step lookahead, and segment policies. We evaluate the policies empirically on multiple persona-alignment conversational problems. Different policies excel in different problems, reflecting their different characteristics and highlighting the importance of our studied policy class.

</details>


### [88] [Topological descriptors of foot clearance gait dynamics improve differential diagnosis of Parkinsonism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06212)
*Jhonathan Barrios,Wolfram Erlhagen,Miguel F. Gago,Estela Bicho,Flora Ferreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究使用拓扑数据分析（TDA）分析足部间隙时间序列，结合随机森林分类器，用于区分帕金森综合征亚型，在药物开启状态下对特发性帕金森病与血管性帕金森病的分类准确率达83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森综合征的鉴别诊断具有临床挑战性，因为运动症状重叠且步态异常细微。传统步态分析方法常忽略足部间隙模式中隐藏的非线性和结构特征，需要更精确的鉴别工具来指导治疗和预后评估。

Method: 研究采用拓扑数据分析（TDA）分析足部间隙时间序列，通过持续同调生成Betti曲线、持续景观和轮廓图作为特征，使用随机森林分类器进行鉴别。数据集包括15名对照、15名特发性帕金森病（IPD）和14名血管性帕金森病（VaP）患者，采用留一法交叉验证评估模型性能。

Result: Betti曲线描述符表现最佳。在药物开启状态下，IPD与VaP的分类准确率达83%，AUC=0.89。药物开启状态下的性能优于关闭状态，当同时考虑开启和关闭状态时性能进一步提升，表明拓扑特征对左旋多巴相关的步态变化敏感。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析与机器学习相结合能够改善临床步态分析，有助于帕金森综合征的鉴别诊断，为临床决策提供有价值的补充工具。

Abstract: Differential diagnosis among parkinsonian syndromes remains a clinical challenge due to overlapping motor symptoms and subtle gait abnormalities. Accurate differentiation is crucial for treatment planning and prognosis. While gait analysis is a well established approach for assessing motor impairments, conventional methods often overlook hidden nonlinear and structural features embedded in foot clearance patterns. We evaluated Topological Data Analysis (TDA) as a complementary tool for Parkinsonism classification using foot clearance time series. Persistent homology produced Betti curves, persistence landscapes, and silhouettes, which were used as features for a Random Forest classifier. The dataset comprised 15 controls (CO), 15 idiopathic Parkinson's disease (IPD), and 14 vascular Parkinsonism (VaP). Models were assessed with leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV). Betti-curve descriptors consistently yielded the strongest results. For IPD vs VaP, foot clearance variables minimum toe clearance, maximum toe late swing, and maximum heel clearance achieved 83% accuracy and AUC=0.89 under LOOCV in the medicated (On) state. Performance improved in the On state and further when both Off and On states were considered, indicating sensitivity of the topological features to levodopa related gait changes. These findings support integrating TDA with machine learning to improve clinical gait analysis and aid differential diagnosis across parkinsonian disorders.

</details>


### [89] [FedSCS-XGB -- Federated Server-centric surrogate XGBoost for continual health monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06224)
*Felix Walger,Mehdi Ejtehadi,Anke Schmeink,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于XGBoost的分布式机器学习协议，用于可穿戴传感器的人体活动识别，在脊髓损伤监测场景中实现接近集中式训练的性能（差距<1%），同时保持XGBoost的结构优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊髓损伤患者面临压力性损伤和血压不稳定等风险，需要连续监测以实现早期检测和干预。可穿戴传感器结合本地数据处理能够早期发现健康威胁，但分布式环境下的机器学习需要保持模型性能。

Method: 提出基于梯度提升决策树（XGBoost）的分布式机器学习协议，受Party-Adaptive XGBoost（PAX）启发，但明确保留了标准XGBoost的关键结构和优化特性，包括基于直方图的分裂构建和树集成动态。

Result: 理论分析表明在适当数据条件和超参数选择下，分布式协议能收敛到与集中式XGBoost训练等效的解。在代表性可穿戴传感器HAR数据集上的实证评估显示，与集中式XGBoost和IBM PAX相比，该方法性能差距小于1%，同时保持了XGBoost的结构优势。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式XGBoost协议在可穿戴传感器人体活动识别场景中，能够实现接近集中式训练的性能，为远程监测中的异构性和数据碎片化问题提供了有效的分布式机器学习解决方案。

Abstract: Wearable sensors with local data processing can detect health threats early, enhance documentation, and support personalized therapy. In the context of spinal cord injury (SCI), which involves risks such as pressure injuries and blood pressure instability, continuous monitoring can help mitigate these by enabling early deDtection and intervention. In this work, we present a novel distributed machine learning (DML) protocol for human activity recognition (HAR) from wearable sensor data based on gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost). The proposed architecture is inspired by Party-Adaptive XGBoost (PAX) while explicitly preserving key structural and optimization properties of standard XGBoost, including histogram-based split construction and tree-ensemble dynamics. First, we provide a theoretical analysis showing that, under appropriate data conditions and suitable hyperparameter selection, the proposed distributed protocol can converge to solutions equivalent to centralized XGBoost training. Second, the protocol is empirically evaluated on a representative wearable-sensor HAR dataset, reflecting the heterogeneity and data fragmentation typical of remote monitoring scenarios. Benchmarking against centralized XGBoost and IBM PAX demonstrates that the theoretical convergence properties are reflected in practice. The results indicate that the proposed approach can match centralized performance up to a gap under 1\% while retaining the structural advantages of XGBoost in distributed wearable-based HAR settings.

</details>


### [90] [DC-Merge: Improving Model Merging with Directional Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06242)
*Han-Chen Zhang,Zi-Hao Zhou,Mao-Lin Luo,Shimin Di,Min-Ling Zhang,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DC-Merge是一种方向一致性的模型合并方法，通过平滑奇异值平衡任务向量能量分布，并在共享正交子空间中对齐方向几何，实现多任务知识的有效保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法在保留多任务知识时面临两个关键问题：1）任务向量内部能量分布不平衡，少数奇异值主导总能量，导致语义重要但较弱的组件在合并时被忽略；2）参数空间中任务向量的几何不一致性，直接合并会扭曲其底层方向几何。

Method: DC-Merge包含三个步骤：首先通过平滑奇异值平衡每个任务向量的能量分布，确保所有知识组件得到充分表示；然后将这些能量平衡的向量投影到共享正交子空间，以最小重构误差对齐其方向几何；最后在共享正交子空间中聚合对齐的向量，并投影回原始参数空间。

Result: 在视觉和视觉语言基准测试上的广泛实验表明，DC-Merge在完全微调和LoRA设置下均能持续实现最先进的性能。

Conclusion: DC-Merge通过解决任务向量能量分布不平衡和几何不一致性问题，实现了方向一致性的模型合并，有效保留了多任务知识，为模型合并提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Model merging aims to integrate multiple task-adapted models into a unified model that preserves the knowledge of each task. In this paper, we identify that the key to this knowledge retention lies in maintaining the directional consistency of singular spaces between merged multi-task vector and individual task vectors. However, this consistency is frequently compromised by two issues: i) an imbalanced energy distribution within task vectors, where a small fraction of singular values dominate the total energy, leading to the neglect of semantically important but weaker components upon merging, and ii) the geometric inconsistency of task vectors in parameter space, which causes direct merging to distort their underlying directional geometry. To address these challenges, we propose DC-Merge, a method for directional-consistent model merging. It first balances the energy distribution of each task vector by smoothing its singular values, ensuring all knowledge components are adequately represented. These energy-balanced vectors are then projected onto a shared orthogonal subspace to align their directional geometries with minimal reconstruction error. Finally, the aligned vectors are aggregated in the shared orthogonal subspace and projected back to the original parameter space. Extensive experiments on vision and vision-language benchmarks show that DC-Merge consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both full fine-tuning and LoRA settings. The implementation code is available at https://github.com/Tobeginwith/DC-Merge.

</details>


### [91] [Gradient Flow Polarizes Softmax Outputs towards Low-Entropy Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06248)
*Aditya Varre,Mark Rofin,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了基于softmax的value-softmax模型的梯度流动力学，揭示了梯度流会驱动优化趋向低熵输出，这一极化效应在各种损失函数中普遍存在，为注意力机制中的经验现象提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解基于softmax模型的非凸训练动力学对于解释transformer的经验成功至关重要。value-softmax模型作为自注意力机制的核心构建块，分析其梯度流动力学可以直接洞察transformer的训练动态。

Method: 分析value-softmax模型L(Vσ(a))的梯度流动力学，其中V是可学习的值矩阵，a是注意力向量。研究梯度流在这种结构上的优化行为，特别关注其驱动低熵输出的内在机制。

Result: 梯度流在这种结构上固有地驱动优化趋向低熵输出解，这种极化效应在包括逻辑损失和平方损失在内的各种目标函数中具有普遍性。这些理论结果为注意力下沉和大规模激活等经验现象提供了形式化机制。

Conclusion: value-softmax模型的梯度流动力学揭示了transformer训练中固有的低熵输出倾向，为理解注意力机制的经验行为提供了理论基础，并解释了观察到的极化现象。

Abstract: Understanding the intricate non-convex training dynamics of softmax-based models is crucial for explaining the empirical success of transformers. In this article, we analyze the gradient flow dynamics of the value-softmax model, defined as ${L}(\mathbf{V} σ(\mathbf{a}))$, where $\mathbf{V}$ and $\mathbf{a}$ are a learnable value matrix and attention vector, respectively. As the matrix times softmax vector parameterization constitutes the core building block of self-attention, our analysis provides direct insight into transformer's training dynamics. We reveal that gradient flow on this structure inherently drives the optimization toward solutions characterized by low-entropy outputs. We demonstrate the universality of this polarizing effect across various objectives, including logistic and square loss. Furthermore, we discuss the practical implications of these theoretical results, offering a formal mechanism for empirical phenomena such as attention sinks and massive activations.

</details>


### [92] [Learning to Solve Orienteering Problem with Time Windows and Variable Profits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06260)
*Songqun Gao,Zanxi Ruan,Patrick Floor,Marco Roveri,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeCoST是一种用于解决带时间窗和可变收益定向问题(OPTWVP)的两阶段学习优化框架，通过解耦离散和连续变量实现高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法无法有效处理OPTWVP中同时包含离散和连续变量的混合优化问题，需要开发更高效的求解器。

Method: 提出两阶段解耦优化框架：第一阶段使用并行解码结构预测路径和初始服务时间分配；第二阶段通过线性规划优化服务时间，并提供长期结构估计学习。

Result: DeCoST在OPTWVP实例上优于现有构造性求解器和元启发式算法，在少于500节点的实例上实现6.6倍推理加速，且与多种构造性求解器兼容。

Conclusion: DeCoST通过有效解耦离散和连续变量，为OPTWVP问题提供了高效且可学习的优化框架，证明了第二阶段解的全局最优性。

Abstract: The orienteering problem with time windows and variable profits (OPTWVP) is common in many real-world applications and involves continuous time variables. Current approaches fail to develop an efficient solver for this orienteering problem variant with discrete and continuous variables. In this paper, we propose a learning-based two-stage DEcoupled discrete-Continuous optimization with Service-time-guided Trajectory (DeCoST), which aims to effectively decouple the discrete and continuous decision variables in the OPTWVP problem, while enabling efficient and learnable coordination between them. In the first stage, a parallel decoding structure is employed to predict the path and the initial service time allocation. The second stage optimizes the service times through a linear programming (LP) formulation and provides a long-horizon learning of structure estimation. We rigorously prove the global optimality of the second-stage solution. Experiments on OPTWVP instances demonstrate that DeCoST outperforms both state-of-the-art constructive solvers and the latest meta-heuristic algorithms in terms of solution quality and computational efficiency, achieving up to 6.6x inference speedup on instances with fewer than 500 nodes. Moreover, the proposed framework is compatible with various constructive solvers and consistently enhances the solution quality for OPTWVP.

</details>


### [93] [Stem: Rethinking Causal Information Flow in Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06274)
*Lin Niu,Xin Luo,Linchuan Xie,Yifu Sun,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,S Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stem模块，一种基于信息流的因果注意力稀疏化方法，通过位置衰减策略和输出感知度量来降低计算复杂度，同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制的二次计算复杂度限制了大型语言模型向长上下文的扩展，特别是在预填充阶段。现有稀疏方法通常对所有token位置应用统一的top-k选择，忽略了因果架构中token信息的累积依赖性。

Method: 提出Stem模块：1) Token Position-Decay策略：在每层应用位置相关的top-k选择，保留初始token以支持递归依赖；2) Output-Aware Metric：基于近似输出幅度优先选择高影响力token，保留信息丰富的token。

Result: 广泛评估表明，Stem在减少计算量和预填充延迟的同时，实现了优越的准确性。

Conclusion: Stem是一种即插即用的稀疏模块，与信息流对齐，能有效解决因果注意力中的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: The quadratic computational complexity of self-attention remains a fundamental bottleneck for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) to long contexts, particularly during the pre-filling phase. In this paper, we rethink the causal attention mechanism from the perspective of information flow. Due to causal constraints, tokens at initial positions participate in the aggregation of every subsequent token. However, existing sparse methods typically apply a uniform top-k selection across all token positions within a layer, ignoring the cumulative dependency of token information inherent in causal architectures. To address this, we propose Stem, a novel, plug-and-play sparsity module aligned with information flow. First, Stem employs the Token Position-Decay strategy, applying position-dependent top-k within each layer to retain initial tokens for recursive dependencies. Second, to preserve information-rich tokens, Stem utilizes the Output-Aware Metric. It prioritizes high-impact tokens based on approximate output magnitude. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Stem achieves superior accuracy with reduced computation and pre-filling latency.

</details>


### [94] [From Entropy to Calibrated Uncertainty: Training Language Models to Reason About Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06317)
*Azza Jenane,Nassim Walha,Lukas Kuhn,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种三阶段后训练流程，让大语言模型能够高效推断其响应的校准不确定性估计，实现可解释且计算高效的不确定性量化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域，能够表达可解释且校准的不确定性的大语言模型至关重要。现有的事后不确定性计算方法通常是基于采样的，计算成本高且缺乏校准性

Method: 三阶段后训练流程：1) 在训练数据上计算基于嵌入空间分布变化的细粒度熵不确定性分数；2) 通过Platt缩放校准这些分数，生成可靠且人类可解释的不确定性信号；3) 通过强化学习后训练目标LLM，使其策略与这些校准信号对齐，使用可验证的奖励函数

Result: 实验表明，使用该流程训练的模型比基线方法具有更好的校准性，并且能够泛化到未见任务而无需进一步处理，表明模型学习了稳健的不确定性推理行为

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可解释且计算高效的不确定性估计方法，不同于事后不确定性估计方法，能够在测试时提供高效的不确定性估计，使LLM在高风险应用中更加可靠

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) that can express interpretable and calibrated uncertainty are crucial in high-stakes domains. While methods to compute uncertainty post-hoc exist, they are often sampling-based and therefore computationally expensive or lack calibration. We propose a three-stage pipeline to post-train LLMs to efficiently infer calibrated uncertainty estimates for their responses. First, we compute fine-grained entropy-based uncertainty scores on the training data, capturing the distributional variability of model outputs in embedding space. Second, these scores are calibrated via Platt scaling, producing reliable and human-interpretable uncertainty signals. Finally, the target LLM is post-trained via reinforcement learning to align its policy with these calibrated signals through a verifiable reward function. Unlike post-hoc uncertainty estimation methods, our approach provides interpretable and computationally efficient uncertainty estimates at test time. Experiments show that models trained with our pipeline achieve better calibration than baselines and generalize to unseen tasks without further processing, suggesting that they learn a robust uncertainty reasoning behavior.

</details>


### [95] [Toward Generative Quantum Utility via Correlation-Complexity Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06440)
*Chen-Yu Liu,Leonardo Placidi,Eric Brunner,Enrico Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出相关性-复杂性映射作为诊断工具，用于判断真实世界数据分布是否与IQP型量子生成模型结构对齐，并应用于湍流数据建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发实用诊断工具来确定真实世界数据分布何时与IQP型量子生成模型结构对齐，以推进量子生成模型的实用化应用。

Method: 提出相关性-复杂性映射，包含两个指标：量子相关性指标(QCLI)和经典相关性-复杂性指标(CCI)。QCLI基于数据集的相关阶功率谱计算，使用Jensen-Shannon散度量化与i.i.d.二项分布的差异；CCI定义为最优Chow-Liu树近似无法捕获的总相关性比例。应用可逆浮点-比特串表示和潜在参数适应方案，重用紧凑IQP电路学习低维潜在轨迹。

Result: 识别湍流数据同时具有IQP兼容性和经典复杂性（高QCLI/高CCI）。在比较评估中，IQP方法使用更少的训练快照和小型潜在块，实现了与RBM和DCGAN等经典模型竞争性的分布对齐。

Conclusion: QCLI/CCI可作为实用指标定位IQP对齐领域，支持使用IQP型量子生成模型处理复杂真实世界数据，如湍流，为推进量子生成效用提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We propose a Correlation-Complexity Map as a practical diagnostic tool for determining when real-world data distributions are structurally aligned with IQP-type quantum generative models. Characterized by two complementary indicators: (i) a Quantum Correlation-Likeness Indicator (QCLI), computed from the dataset's correlation-order (Walsh-Hadamard/Fourier) power spectrum aggregated by interaction order and quantified via Jensen-Shannon divergence from an i.i.d. binomial reference; and (ii) a Classical Correlation-Complexity Indicator (CCI), defined as the fraction of total correlation not captured by the optimal Chow-Liu tree approximation, normalized by total correlation. We provide theoretical support by relating QCLI to a support-mismatch mechanism, for fixed-architecture IQP families trained with an MMD objective, higher QCLI implies a smaller irreducible approximation floor. Using the map, we identify the classical turbulence data as both IQP-compatible and classically complex (high QCLI/high CCI). Guided by this placement, we use an invertible float-to-bitstring representation and a latent-parameter adaptation scheme that reuses a compact IQP circuit over a temporal sequence by learning and interpolating a low-dimensional latent trajectory. In comparative evaluations against classical models such as Restricted Boltzmann Machine (RBM) and Deep Convolutional Generative Adversarial Networks (DCGAN), the IQP approach achieves competitive distributional alignment while using substantially fewer training snapshots and a small latent block, supporting the use of QCLI/CCI as practical indicators for locating IQP-aligned domains and advancing generative quantum utility.

</details>


### [96] [COLD-Steer: Steering Large Language Models via In-Context One-step Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06495)
*Kartik Sharma,Rakshit S. Trivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COLD-Steer：一种无需训练、基于梯度下降近似表示的激活引导框架，在少量样本下实现高效LLM行为控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法存在根本性权衡：样本高效方法无法充分捕捉引导信号，而能更好提取信号的方法需要大量样本（数百到数千个）。需要一种既能高效利用样本又能有效提取引导信号的框架。

Method: 提出COLD-Steer框架，通过近似梯度下降对上下文示例的表示变化来引导LLM激活。采用两种互补方法：(1) 单元核近似方法，直接使用激活梯度更新激活，并在示例间归一化；(2) 有限差分近似方法，无论示例数量多少仅需两次前向传播。

Result: 在多种引导任务和基准测试中，COLD-Steer达到高达95%的引导效果，同时比最佳基线方法少用50倍样本。在多元化对齐任务中验证了无需大量演示数据即可适应多样化视角的能力。

Conclusion: COLD-Steer通过原理性地近似学习动态而非专门的训练过程，为自适应、上下文感知的模型控制开辟了新可能性，能够灵活应对不同损失驱动的人类偏好。

Abstract: Activation steering methods enable inference-time control of large language model (LLM) behavior without retraining, but current approaches face a fundamental trade-off: sample-efficient methods suboptimally capture steering signals from labeled examples, while methods that better extract these signals require hundreds to thousands of examples. We introduce COLD-Steer, a training-free framework that steers LLM activations by approximating the representational changes that would result from gradient descent on in-context examples. Our key insight is that the effect of fine-tuning on a small set of examples can be efficiently approximated at inference time without actual parameter updates. We formalize this through two complementary approaches: (i) a unit kernel approximation method that updates the activations directly using gradients with respect to them, normalized across examples, and (ii) a finite-difference approximation requiring only two forward passes regardless of example count. Experiments across a variety of steering tasks and benchmarks demonstrate that COLD-Steer achieves upto 95% steering effectiveness while using 50 times fewer samples compared to the best baseline. COLD-Steer facilitates accommodating diverse perspectives without extensive demonstration data, which we validate through our experiments on pluralistic alignment tasks. Our framework opens new possibilities for adaptive, context-aware model control that can flexibly address varying loss-driven human preferences through principled approximation of learning dynamics rather than specialized training procedures.

</details>


### [97] [When One Modality Rules Them All: Backdoor Modality Collapse in Multimodal Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06508)
*Qitong Wang,Haoran Dai,Haotian Zhang,Christopher Rasmussen,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现多模态扩散模型中的后门攻击存在"模态崩溃"现象，即攻击机制退化为主要依赖部分模态而非多模态协同作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散模型在视觉内容生成领域的广泛应用，研究其安全漏洞（如后门攻击）变得至关重要。传统观点认为多模态攻击（如同时攻击文本和图像）会产生互补效应并增强整体攻击效果，但本文挑战了这一假设

Method: 引入两个新指标：触发模态归因（TMA）和跨触发交互（CTI）来量化后门模态崩溃现象。在多模态条件扩散模型的不同训练配置下进行广泛实验

Result: 实验一致观察到后门行为中的"赢家通吃"动态：1）攻击通常崩溃为子模态主导；2）跨模态交互可忽略甚至为负，与协同脆弱性的直觉相矛盾。高攻击成功率往往掩盖了对子模态的根本依赖

Conclusion: 后门模态崩溃现象揭示了当前评估中的关键盲点，为机制分析和未来防御开发建立了原则性基础。多模态攻击并不总是产生协同效应，反而可能退化为单模态依赖

Abstract: While diffusion models have revolutionized visual content generation, their rapid adoption has underscored the critical need to investigate vulnerabilities, e.g., to backdoor attacks. In multimodal diffusion models, it is natural to expect that attacking multiple modalities simultaneously (e.g., text and image) would yield complementary effects and strengthen the overall backdoor. In this paper, we challenge this assumption by investigating the phenomenon of Backdoor Modality Collapse, a scenario where the backdoor mechanism degenerates to rely predominantly on a subset of modalities, rendering others redundant. To rigorously quantify this behavior, we introduce two novel metrics: Trigger Modality Attribution (TMA) and Cross-Trigger Interaction (CTI). Through extensive experiments across diverse training configurations in multimodal conditional diffusion, we consistently observe a ``winner-takes-all'' dynamic in backdoor behavior. Our results reveal that (1) attacks often collapse into subset-modality dominance, and (2) cross-modal interaction is negligible or even negative, contradicting the intuition of synergistic vulnerability. These findings highlight a critical blind spot in current assessments, suggesting that high attack success rates often mask a fundamental reliance on a subset of modalities. This establishes a principled foundation for mechanistic analysis and future defense development.

</details>


### [98] [Hierarchical Industrial Demand Forecasting with Temporal and Uncertainty Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06555)
*Harshavardhan Kamarthi,Shangqing Xu,Xinjie Tong,Xingyu Zhou,James Peters,Joseph Czyzyk,B. Aditya Prakash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于大规模分层概率时间序列预测的新型可解释性方法，通过适应通用可解释性技术并解决分层结构和不确定性相关挑战，为工业供应链场景提供有价值的解释性见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习模型在分层时间序列预测任务上取得了显著准确性和可扩展性，但其预测的可解释性（特别是面向实际应用的可解释性）在很大程度上仍未得到探索。需要弥合这一差距，为工业供应链等实际场景提供有价值的解释性见解。

Method: 引入一种新颖的可解释性方法，专门针对大规模分层概率时间序列预测设计。该方法适应通用可解释性技术，同时解决与分层结构和不确定性相关的特定挑战。通过生成基于真实世界场景的半合成数据集进行评估，该方法能够解释最先进的工业预测方法。

Result: 实验表明，该可解释性方法成功解释了最先进的工业预测方法，并显著提高了可解释性准确性。多个真实世界案例研究证明了该方法在识别重要模式和解释方面的有效性，帮助利益相关者更好地理解预测结果。此外，该方法有助于识别预测需求背后的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该方法为分层时间序列预测提供了有价值的解释性见解，能够识别层次结构中各时间序列和外部变量的重要性、不同变量对预测不确定性的影响，以及训练数据集变化对预测的影响。这有助于建立用户信任和信心，最终促进分层预测模型在实际中的更好采用和利用。

Abstract: Hierarchical time-series forecasting is essential for demand prediction across various industries. While machine learning models have obtained significant accuracy and scalability on such forecasting tasks, the interpretability of their predictions, informed by application, is still largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel interpretability method for large hierarchical probabilistic time-series forecasting, adapting generic interpretability techniques while addressing challenges associated with hierarchical structures and uncertainty. Our approach offers valuable interpretative insights in response to real-world industrial supply chain scenarios, including 1) the significance of various time-series within the hierarchy and external variables at specific time points, 2) the impact of different variables on forecast uncertainty, and 3) explanations for forecast changes in response to modifications in the training dataset. To evaluate the explainability method, we generate semi-synthetic datasets based on real-world scenarios of explaining hierarchical demands for over ten thousand products at a large chemical company. The experiments showed that our explainability method successfully explained state-of-the-art industrial forecasting methods with significantly higher explainability accuracy. Furthermore, we provide multiple real-world case studies that show the efficacy of our approach in identifying important patterns and explanations that help stakeholders better understand the forecasts. Additionally, our method facilitates the identification of key drivers behind forecasted demand, enabling more informed decision-making and strategic planning. Our approach helps build trust and confidence among users, ultimately leading to better adoption and utilization of hierarchical forecasting models in practice.

</details>


### [99] [A recipe for scalable attention-based MLIPs: unlocking long-range accuracy with all-to-all node attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06567)
*Eric Qu,Brandon M. Wood,Aditi S. Krishnapriyan,Zachary W. Ulissi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AllScAIP是一种基于注意力机制的能量守恒机器学习原子间势模型，可扩展到1亿训练样本，通过全节点注意力组件数据驱动地解决长程相互作用问题，在分子系统上达到最先进的能量/力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习原子间势模型在扩展到生物分子和电解质等大系统时，难以准确捕捉长程相互作用，当前方法依赖显式的物理基础项或组件，需要一种能有效处理长程相互作用且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出AllScAIP模型，采用基于注意力的能量守恒架构，包含全节点注意力组件（all-to-all node attention），该组件完全数据驱动，模型可扩展到1亿训练样本规模。

Result: 模型在分子系统上达到最先进的能量/力精度，在物理基础评估（OMol25）中表现优异，在材料（OMat24）和催化剂（OC20）基准上具有竞争力；能够进行稳定、长时间尺度的分子动力学模拟，准确恢复实验观测值如密度和汽化热。

Conclusion: 在低数据/小模型情况下，归纳偏置能提高样本效率，但随着数据和模型规模扩大，这些优势减弱甚至逆转；全节点注意力对于捕捉长程相互作用至关重要，为大规模系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have advanced rapidly, with many top models relying on strong physics-based inductive biases. However, as models scale to larger systems like biomolecules and electrolytes, they struggle to accurately capture long-range (LR) interactions, leading current approaches to rely on explicit physics-based terms or components. In this work, we propose AllScAIP, a straightforward, attention-based, and energy-conserving MLIP model that scales to O(100 million) training samples. It addresses the long-range challenge using an all-to-all node attention component that is data-driven. Extensive ablations reveal that in low-data/small-model regimes, inductive biases improve sample efficiency. However, as data and model size scale, these benefits diminish or even reverse, while all-to-all attention remains critical for capturing LR interactions. Our model achieves state-of-the-art energy/force accuracy on molecular systems, as well as a number of physics-based evaluations (OMol25), while being competitive on materials (OMat24) and catalysts (OC20). Furthermore, it enables stable, long-timescale MD simulations that accurately recover experimental observables, including density and heat of vaporization predictions.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Hybrid Structured Editing: Structures for Tools, Text for Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05644)
*Tom Beckmann,Christoph Thiede,Jens Lincke,Robert Hirschfeld*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出混合结构化编辑方法，在保持文本编辑界面的同时提供结构化保证，支持编程环境的安全扩展


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程环境扩展机制难以实现工具与源代码的紧密集成，工具需要跟踪程序结构变化但文本解析步骤使结构跟踪复杂化

Method: 提出混合结构化编辑方法，允许工具构建者声明程序必须遵守的结构约束并确保其遵守，同时为用户提供熟悉的文本编辑界面

Result: 通过实现和多个案例研究展示了混合结构化编辑的有效性，支持基于结构化代码表示的工具扩展

Conclusion: 混合结构化编辑支持编程环境的安全扩展，使工具能够在结构化代码表示上工作并提供一致可靠的用户体验

Abstract: In programming, better tools often yield better results. For that, modern programming environments offer mechanisms to allow for their extensibility. The closer those tools are to the code, the easier it is for programmers to map the information provided by a tool to the code this information is about.  However, existing extension mechanisms do not facilitate the close integration of tools with textual source code. Tools must be able to track program structures across edits to appear at the right positions but the parsing step of text complicates tracking structures. We propose hybrid structured editing, an approach that supports tool builders by providing structural guarantees while providing tool users with a familiar and consistent text editing interface. Hybrid structured editing allows tool builders to declare constraints on the structure that a program must conform to and ensures their observance. We present an implementation and several case studies of tools based on hybrid structured editing to demonstrate its effectiveness. Hybrid structured editing supports the safe extension of programming environments with tools that work on a structured representation of code and provide a consistent and reliable user experience.

</details>


### [101] [Pitfalls in VM Implementation on CHERI: Lessons from Porting CRuby](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05645)
*Hanhaotian Liu,Tetsuro Yamazaki,Tomoharu Ugawa*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文通过将CRuby移植到CHERI架构的案例研究，系统分析了虚拟机在CHERI上实现时特有的陷阱和挑战，特别是C语言未定义行为在传统架构与CHERI严格内存安全模型之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHERI通过硬件能力取代传统指针来增强内存安全性，虚拟机作为可能包含潜在内存漏洞的系统可以从CHERI保护中受益。然而，将虚拟机移植到CHERI面临许多挑战，特别是基于传统架构对C语言未定义行为的假设与CHERI更严格内存安全模型的冲突，导致意外故障。现有移植指南主要关注通用编程，缺乏针对虚拟机特定问题的指导。

Method: 通过将CRuby移植到CHERI作为案例研究，并调研先前将虚拟机移植到CHERI的工作，对发现的问题按原因进行分类和讨论。具体分析虚拟机实现技术和惯用模式中基于传统架构的假设在CHERI上无效的情况，并为每个类别提供具体示例和解决方案。

Result: 识别并分类了虚拟机在CHERI上的特定陷阱，大多数陷阱源于C语言的未定义行为。提供了针对这些问题的变通方案，并通过在CRuby移植中应用这些方案以及调研先前案例研究的代码库验证了这些方案的有效性。建立了虚拟机在CHERI上开发和移植的知识体系。

Conclusion: 本文系统总结了虚拟机在CHERI架构上的实现陷阱和解决方案，填补了现有指南在虚拟机特定问题方面的空白。这项工作为开发和移植更安全的虚拟机到CHERI架构提供了实用指导，有助于构建更安全的虚拟机系统。

Abstract: CHERI (Capability Hardware Enhanced RISC Instructions) is a novel hardware designed to address memory safety issues. By replacing traditional pointers with hardware capabilities, it enhances security in modern software systems. A Virtual Machine (VM) is one such system that can benefit from CHERI's protection, as it may contain latent memory vulnerabilities. However, developing and porting VMs to CHERI is a non-trivial task. There are many subtle pitfalls from the assumptions on the undefined behaviors of the C language made based on conventional architectures. Those assumptions conflict with CHERI's stricter memory safety model, causing unexpected failures. Although several prior works have discussed the process of porting VMs, they focus on the overall porting process instead of the pitfalls for VM implementation on CHERI. The guide for programming in CHERI exists, but it is for general programming, not addressing VM-specific issues. We have ported CRuby to CHERI as a case study and surveyed previous works on porting VMs to CHERI. We categorized and discussed the issues found based on their causes. In this paper, we illustrate the VM-specific pitfalls for each category. Most of the pitfalls arise from the undefined behaviors in the C language; in particular, implementation techniques and idioms of VMs often assume behaviors of traditional architectures that are invalid on CHERI. We also discuss workarounds for them and the impacts of those workarounds. We verified the validity of the workarounds by applying them to our CRuby port and by surveying the codebases of prior case studies. This work contributes to the body of knowledge on developing and porting VMs to CHERI and will help guide efforts toward constructing safer VMs.

</details>


### [102] [Evaluating LLMs in the Context of a Functional Programming Course: A Comprehensive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05646)
*Yihan Zhang,Brigitte Pientka,Xujie Si*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 评估LLM在低资源编程语言OCaml教育场景中的有效性，构建三个基准测试，发现顶级LLM在函数式编程课程中表现良好，但在低资源环境下解决作业问题的能力远低于Python/Java。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在高资源编程语言（如Java、Python）教育中的表现，但缺乏对低资源编程语言（如OCaml）教育场景的系统评估。需要全面了解LLM在函数式编程课程中的实际能力，为教育者和学生提供指导。

Method: 构建三个基准测试：1) λCodeGen（自然语言作业编程问题）；2) λRepair（包含语法、类型和逻辑错误的实际学生提交程序）；3) λExplain（编程概念理论问题）。评估9个最先进的LLM，使用OCaml编译器和自动评分器评估正确性，并采用人工评分评估响应质量。

Result: 前三名LLM在典型函数式编程课程的所有任务中都表现有效，但在低资源环境下解决的作业问题数量远少于在Python和Java入门编程问题上的成功。LLM的优势在于纠正语法和类型错误以及回答基本概念问题。虽然在某些领域可能还不及专用语言特定工具，但其作为多编程语言一站式工具的便利性可以超越更专业系统的优势。

Conclusion: LLM在低资源编程语言教育中具有实用价值，特别是在错误纠正和概念解释方面。研究构建的基准可用于评估LLM能力演变、帮助学生了解LLM生成解决方案的局限性，并为编程语言研究者提供开发低资源语言代码合成和修复工具的机会。

Abstract: Large-Language Models (LLMs) are changing the way learners acquire knowledge outside the classroom setting. Previous studies have shown that LLMs seem effective in generating to short and simple questions in introductory CS courses using high-resource programming languages such as Java or Python. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of LLMs in the context of a low-resource programming language -- OCaml, in an educational setting. In particular, we built three benchmarks to comprehensively evaluate 9 state-of-the-art LLMs: 1) $λ$CodeGen (a benchmark containing natural-language homework programming problems); 2) $λ$Repair (a benchmark containing programs with syntax, type, and logical errors drawn from actual student submissions); 3) $λ$Explain (a benchmark containing natural language questions regarding theoretical programming concepts). We grade each LLMs responses with respect to correctness using the OCaml compiler and an autograder. And our evaluation goes beyond common evaluation methodology by using manual grading to assess the quality of the responses. 
  Our study shows that the top three LLMs are effective on all tasks within a typical functional programming course, although they solve much fewer homework problems in the low-resource setting compared to their success on introductory programming problems in Python and Java. The strength of LLMs lies in correcting syntax and type errors as well as generating answers to basic conceptual questions. While LLMs may not yet match dedicated language-specific tools in some areas, their convenience as a one-stop tool for multiple programming languages can outweigh the benefits of more specialized systems. We hope our benchmarks can serve multiple purposes: to assess the evolving capabilities of LLMs, to help instructors raise awareness among students about the limitations of LLM-generated solutions, and to inform programming language researchers about opportunities to integrate domain-specific reasoning into LLMs and develop more powerful code synthesis and repair tools for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [103] [JoinActors: A Modular Library for Actors with Join Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05648)
*Ayman Hussein,Philipp Haller,Ioannis Karras,Hernán Melgratti,Alceste Scalas,Emilio Tuosto*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: JoinActors是一个用于Scala 3的模块化join模式匹配库，通过元编程提供直观API，支持多种匹配算法，性能显著优于先前工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有join模式实现多为领域特定语言，难以集成到现有生态系统，且使用预定义匹配算法，无法根据应用需求优化。需要开发既可作为常规库使用，又支持可扩展匹配算法设计的工具包。

Method: 改进JoinActors库，利用Scala 3的元编程技术提供类似标准模式匹配的直观API，采用模块化设计支持集成多种匹配算法，使用公平join模式匹配语义和基准测试套件进行验证。

Result: 新优化的匹配算法与原始JoinActors产生完全相同的匹配结果，但性能显著提升。模块化设计为探索和比较新的join模式匹配算法提供了研究平台。

Conclusion: JoinActors展示了在现有语言中实现复杂协调模式的表达能力、有效性和可用性，其模块化设计不仅提供实用价值，还为研究不同语义的匹配算法提供了实验平台。

Abstract: Join patterns are a high-level programming construct for message-passing applications. They offer an intuitive and declarative approach for specifying how concurrent and distributed components coordinate, possibly depending on complex conditions over combinations of messages. Join patterns have inspired many implementations -- but most of them are not available as libraries: rather, they are domain-specific languages that can be hard to integrate into pre-existing ecosystems. Moreover, all implementations ship with a predefined matching algorithm, which may not be optimal depending on the application requirements. These limitations are addressed by `JoinActors`, a recently published library which integrates join patterns in the off-the-shelf Scala 3 programming language, and is designed to be modular w.r.t. the matching algorithm in use.
  In this work we address the problem of designing, developing, and evaluating a modular join pattern matching toolkit that (1) can be used as a regular library with a developer-friendly syntax within a pre-existing programming language, and (2) has an extensible design that supports the use and comparison of different matching algorithms. We analyse how `JoinActors` achieves goals (1) and (2) above. The paper that introduced `JoinActors` only briefly outlined its design and implementation (as its main goal was formalising its novel fair matching semantics*). In this work we present and discuss in detail an improved version of `JoinActors`, focusing on its use of metaprogramming (which enables an intuitive API resembling standard pattern matching) and on its modular design. We show how this enables the integration of multiple matching algorithms with different optimisations and we evaluate their performance via benchmarks covering different workloads.
  We illustrate a sophisticated use of Scala 3's metaprogramming for the integration of an advanced concurrent programming construct within a pre-existing language. In addition, we discuss the insights and "lessons learned" in optimising join pattern matching, and how they are facilitated by `JoinActors`'s modularity -- which allows for the systematic comparison of multiple matching algorithm implementations. We adopt the fair join pattern matching semantics and the benchmark suite from the paper that originally introduced `JoinActors`. Through extensive testing we ensure that our new optimised matching algorithms produce exactly the same matches as the original `JoinActors` library, while achieving significantly better performance. The improved version of `JoinActors` is the companion artifact of this paper. This work showcases the expressiveness, effectiveness, and usability of join patterns for implementing complex coordination patterns in distributed message-passing systems, within a pre-existing language. It also demonstrates promising performance results, with significant improvements over previous work. Besides the practical promise, `JoinActors`'s modular design offers a research playground for exploring and comparing new join pattern matching algorithms, possibly based on entirely different semantics.

</details>


### [104] [Efficient Selection of Type Annotations for Performance Improvement in Gradual Typing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05649)
*Senxi Li,Feng Dai,Tetsuro Yamazaki,Shigeru Chiba*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数据流选择类型注释的轻量级方法，用于改善渐进类型程序的执行性能，同时保持较短的编译时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进类型语言中，部分类型化程序常出现意外的性能下降问题，现有方法编译时间过长，阻碍了实际应用。

Method: 采用轻量级、摊销的方法，沿数据流选择类型推断得出的类型注释子集，避免值在无类型和类型化代码边界间重复转换导致的昂贵运行时强制转换。

Result: 在Reticulated Python上的实验表明，该方法优于使用所有类型推断注释的朴素策略，与现有方法相比达到相当的执行速度，同时保持更稳定的编译时间。

Conclusion: 该技术在实践中有效缓解了渐进类型程序的性能瓶颈，在Reticulated Python中具有实用性。

Abstract: Gradual typing has gained popularity as a design choice for integrating static and dynamic typing within a single language. Several practical languages have adopted gradual typing to offer programmers the flexibility to annotate their programs as needed. Meanwhile there is a key challenge of unexpected performance degradation in partially typed programs. The execution speed may significantly decrease when simply adding more type annotations. Prior studies have investigated strategies of selectively adding type annotations for better performance. However, they are restricted in substantial compilation time, which impedes the practical usage.
  This paper presents a new technique to select a subset of type annotations derived by type inference for improving the execution performance of gradually typed programs. The advantage of the proposal is shorter compilation time by employing a lightweight, amortized approach. It selects type annotations along the data flows, which is expected to avoid expensive runtime casts caused by a value repeatedly crossing the boundaries between untyped and typed code.
  We demonstrate the applicability of our proposal, and conduct experiments to validate its effectiveness of improving the execution time on Reticulated Python. Our implementation supports a Python subset to select type annotations derived by an implemented, external type inference engine. Experiment results show that our proposal outperforms a naive strategy of using all type annotations derived by type inference among the benchmark programs. In comparison with an existing approach, the proposal achieves comparable execution speed and shows advantage of maintaining a more stable compilation time of deriving and selecting type annotations. Our results empirically indicate that the proposed technique is practical within Reticulated Python for mitigating the performance bottleneck of gradually typed programs.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Enhanced Protein Intrinsic Disorder Prediction Through Dual-View Multiscale Features and Multi-objective Evolutionary Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06292)
*Shaokuan Wang,Pengshan Cui,Yining Qian,An-Yang Lu,Xianpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: D2MOE：一种用于蛋白质无序区域预测的双视图多尺度特征与多目标进化算法，通过进化视图与深度语义视图的集成以及自适应特征融合架构搜索，显著提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质内在无序区域在细胞信号传导和药物发现中至关重要，但其高结构柔性使得残基级预测具有挑战性。现有方法通常依赖单视图表示或僵化的手动融合策略，无法有效平衡局部氨基酸偏好与长程序列模式之间的复杂相互作用。

Method: D2MOE包含两个阶段：1）双视图多尺度特征提取方法，集成进化视图与深度语义视图，采用多尺度提取器捕捉不同感受野的结构信息；2）多目标进化算法，通过协同进化离散特征选择和连续融合权重，自适应探索最优跨特征架构，在提升预测精度的同时保持模型紧凑性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，D2MOE持续优于最先进的方法。该方法结合了深度学习的特征提取能力和进化算法的全局搜索优势，无需手动设计即可实现高效特征集成。

Conclusion: D2MOE为蛋白质无序预测提供了一个强大的计算工具，通过自适应特征融合架构搜索实现了预测精度的显著提升，为蛋白质结构-功能关系研究提供了新的方法学支持。

Abstract: Intrinsically disordered regions of proteins play a crucial role in cell signaling and drug discovery. However, their high structural flexibility makes accurate residue-level prediction challenging. Existing methods often rely on single-view representations or rigid manual fusion strategies, which fail to effectively balance the complex interplay between local amino acid preferences and long-range sequence patterns. To address these limitations, we propose D2MOE, a Dual-View Multiscale Features and Multi-objective Evolutionary Algorithm, which consists of two stages. First, a dual-view multiscale feature extraction method is introduced. This method integrates evolutionary views with deep semantic views and employs multiscale extractors to capture structural information across diverse receptive fields. Second, a multi-objective evolutionary algorithm is designed to adaptively discover optimal fusion architectures. By co-evolving discrete feature selection and continuous fusion weights, the algorithm adaptively explores optimal cross-feature architectures to enhance predictive accuracy while maintaining model compactness. Experimental results across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that D2MOE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. D2MOE combines the feature extraction capabilities of deep learning with the global search advantages of evolutionary algorithms, enabling efficient feature integration without manual design, and providing a robust computational tool for protein disorder prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Model Change for Description Logic Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05562)
*Ana Ozaki,Jandson S. Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文研究描述逻辑概念在模型变化下的修改问题，定义了三种变化类型：驱逐、接收和修正，并证明修正不能简单归结为驱逐和接收的组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究描述逻辑概念在面对以指向解释表示的模型时的修改问题。当知识库需要根据新的模型信息进行更新时，需要形式化的方法来处理概念描述的变化，这在知识表示和推理中具有重要应用价值。

Method: 提出模型变化的形式化框架，定义三种主要变化类型：驱逐（仅移除模型）、接收（仅添加模型）和修正（同时移除和添加模型）。为描述逻辑概念引入修正的形式化概念，并分析这些操作在EL和ALC描述逻辑中的兼容性。

Result: 提供了关于EL和ALC描述逻辑概念中驱逐和接收操作兼容性的正负结果，以及ALC概念修正操作兼容性的结果。关键发现是修正不能简化为驱逐和接收的简单组合，这与直觉相反。

Conclusion: 模型变化是描述逻辑概念修改的重要问题，修正操作具有独特的复杂性，不能简单地分解为驱逐和接收的组合。该研究为描述逻辑中的概念修正提供了理论基础，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: We consider the problem of modifying a description logic concept in light of models represented as pointed interpretations. We call this setting model change, and distinguish three main kinds of changes: eviction, which consists of only removing models; reception, which incorporates models; and revision, which combines removal with incorporation of models in a single operation. We introduce a formal notion of revision and argue that it does not reduce to a simple combination of eviction and reception, contrary to intuition. We provide positive and negative results on the compatibility of eviction and reception for EL and ALC description logic concepts and on the compatibility of revision for ALC concepts.

</details>


### [107] [LTLGuard: Formalizing LTL Specifications with Compact Language Models and Lightweight Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05728)
*Medina Andresel,Cristinel Mateis,Dejan Nickovic,Spyridon Kounoupidis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Stavros Tripakis*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: LTLGuard：一个模块化工具链，通过约束生成和形式一致性检查，使资源高效的开源模型能够从非正式需求生成正确的线性时序逻辑规范


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非正式需求转化为形式规范具有挑战性，因为自然语言存在歧义性和可变性。特别是依赖紧凑（小型和中型）语言模型时，它们可能缺乏对时序逻辑的稳健知识，难以生成语法有效且一致的形式规范

Method: 提出LTLGuard模块化工具链，结合约束生成与形式一致性检查，从非正式输入生成无冲突的LTL规范。该方法将语言模型的生成能力与轻量级自动推理工具集成，迭代细化候选规范，理解冲突来源从而消除不一致性

Result: 展示了方法的可用性和有效性，并对最终框架进行了定量评估

Conclusion: LTLGuard使资源高效的开源模型（4B-14B参数）能够从非正式需求生成正确的线性时序逻辑规范，解决了紧凑模型在形式规范生成中的挑战

Abstract: Translating informal requirements into formal specifications is challenging due to the ambiguity and variability of natural language (NL). This challenge is particularly pronounced when relying on compact (small and medium) language models, which may lack robust knowledge of temporal logic and thus struggle to produce syntactically valid and consistent formal specifications. In this work, we focus on enabling resource-efficient open-weight models (4B--14B parameters) to generate correct linear temporal logic (LTL) specifications from informal requirements. We present LTLGuard, a modular toolchain that combines constrained generation with formal consistency checking to generate conflict-free LTL specifications from informal input. Our method integrates the generative capabilities of model languages with lightweight automated reasoning tools to iteratively refine candidate specifications, understand the origin of the conflicts and thus help in eliminating inconsistencies. We demonstrate the usability and the effectiveness of our approach and perform quantitative evaluation of the resulting framework.

</details>


### [108] [Diagonalizing Through the $ω$-Chain: Iterated Self-Certification on Bounded Turing Machines and its Least Fixed Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06012)
*Miara Sung*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文将图灵机中的有界自认证失败问题转化为域论框架，通过定义推进算子处理有限停机观测，证明有界机器无法达到不动点，但迭代过程形成ω链，其Scott极限解析为算子的最小不动点，代表无界计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图灵机中有界自认证失败的根本原因——自模拟必然产生严格正的时间开销。将这一操作约束转化为域论框架，为停机问题提供新的理论视角。

Method: 定义从时间界i推进到i+1的算子来处理有限停机观测。分析有界机器在该算子下的行为，证明无法达到不动点。研究迭代过程形成的ω链，计算其Scott极限。

Result: 有界机器无法在推进算子下达到不动点，但迭代过程形成上升ω链。该链的Scott极限解析为算子的最小不动点，代表能够完全捕获机器停机行为的无界计算。

Conclusion: 该构造为停机问题提供了新颖视角，将从有限可观测性到最小不动点的过渡框架化为对角线的连续延迟，揭示了有界与无界计算之间的本质联系。

Abstract: Bounded self-certification in Turing machines fails because self-simulation necessarily incurs a strictly positive temporal overhead. We translate this operational constraint into a domain-theoretic framework, defining an operator that advances a finite halting observation from time bound $i$ to $i+1$. While no bounded machine can achieve a fixed point under this operator, the iterative process forms an ascending $ω$-chain. The Scott limit of this chain resolves to the least fixed point of the operator, representing an unbounded computation that fully captures the machine's halting behavior. Our construction provides a novel perspective on the halting problem, framing the transition from finite observability to the least fixed point as the continuous deferral of the diagonal.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Tool-Genesis: A Task-Driven Tool Creation Benchmark for Self-Evolving Language Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05578)
*Bowei Xia,Mengkang Hu,Shijian Wang,Jiarui Jin,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Kexin Li,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Tool-Genesis：一个用于评估语言智能体从抽象需求自主创建工具能力的诊断基准，关注接口合规性、功能正确性和下游效用等多维度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要依赖预定义规范，限制了可扩展性和真正自主进化的能力。虽然近期研究尝试动态生成工具，但主要强调下游性能，导致"黑盒"评估难以追踪失败的具体原因。

Method: 提出Tool-Genesis诊断基准，量化智能体在多个维度上的能力：接口合规性、功能正确性和下游效用。评估智能体能否仅从抽象需求（无预设规范）构建任务相关工具，并用其解决现实问题。

Result: 即使最先进的模型也难以在一次性设置中生成精确的工具接口或可执行逻辑。这些初始的小缺陷在流程中被放大，导致下游指标急剧下降。

Conclusion: Tool-Genesis旨在指导未来研究，训练和引导模型合成持久、通用的工具，以更好地应对现实世界的挑战。

Abstract: Research on self-evolving language agents has accelerated, drawing increasing attention to their ability to create, adapt, and maintain tools from task requirements. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on predefined specifications, which limits scalability and hinders truly autonomous evolution. While recent studies attempt to dynamically generate tools, they primarily emphasize downstream performance, resulting in a "black-box" evaluation that makes it difficult to attribute failures to specific causes. To address this, we propose Tool-Genesis, a diagnostic benchmark designed to quantify agent capabilities across multiple dimensions, including interface compliance, functional correctness, and downstream utility. Tool-Genesis evaluates whether agents can construct task-relevant tools solely from abstract requirements (without preset specifications) and use them to solve realistic problems. Crucially, we find that even state-of-the-art models struggle to produce precise tool interfaces or executable logic in a one-shot setting. These minor initial flaws are amplified through the pipeline, leading to a sharp degradation in downstream metrics. We hope Tool-Genesis will guide future research toward training and steering models to synthesize persistent, general-purpose tools that better address real-world challenges.

</details>


### [110] [Real Faults in Model Context Protocol (MCP) Software: a Comprehensive Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05637)
*Mina Taraghi,Mohammad Mehdi Morovati,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次对MCP服务器故障进行大规模分类研究，识别出5个高层故障类别，并通过从业者调查验证了分类的完整性和普适性，揭示了MCP特有故障与非MCP故障的差异特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的广泛应用虽然提升了软件系统的能力，但也带来了新的可靠性、安全性和鲁棒性挑战。Model Context Protocol (MCP) 被提出用于标准化AI系统与外部工具的交互，但缺乏对MCP系统中实际故障的系统性理解。

Method: 基于实证证据构建了MCP服务器故障的5类高层分类法，并通过调查不同角色和经验的MCP从业者来评估分类的完整性和普适性。

Result: 所有识别的故障类别在实践中都存在，并揭示了MCP特有故障与非MCP故障的显著差异特征。研究识别了MCP系统中最容易出错和最关键的组件。

Conclusion: 该研究为研究人员和从业者提供了可操作的见解，有助于开发更鲁棒、可靠和安全的基于MCP的AI软件系统，并为MCP系统的故障预防和调试提供了基础。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of foundation models has significantly expanded the capabilities of software systems, enabling them to perform complex language, reasoning, and interaction tasks that were previously difficult to automate. However, this progress has also introduced novel challenges that were largely absent in previous generations of software. In particular, the increasing integration of foundation models with external tools and resources raises new concerns regarding reliability, security, and robustness. The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has recently been proposed to standardize interactions between AI-based software systems, software tools, and external resources. Despite its growing adoption, there remains limited systematic understanding of real-world faults in MCP-based software systems.
  In this paper, we present the first large-scale taxonomy of faults in MCP servers, comprising five high-level fault categories derived from empirical evidence. To evaluate the completeness and generalizability of this taxonomy, we conduct a survey of MCP practitioners with diverse roles and experience levels. The results confirm that all identified fault categories occur in practice and reveal distinct characteristics that differentiate MCP-specific faults from non-MCP faults.
  Overall, this study provides actionable insights for researchers and practitioners by identifying the most error-prone and critical components of MCP-based systems. These insights can inform the development of more robust, reliable, and secure AI-enabled software systems that rely on MCP.

</details>


### [111] [Balancing Latency and Accuracy of Code Completion via Local-Cloud Model Cascading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05974)
*Hanzhen Lu,Lishui Fan,Jiachi Chen,Qiuyuan Chen,Zhao Wei,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MCCom是一个用于代码补全的模型级联框架，通过本地小模型与云端大模型的级联，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低延迟和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码补全方法存在准确率与延迟的权衡：大语言模型准确率高但延迟大，小语言模型延迟低但准确率不足。需要一种既能保持高质量建议又能降低延迟和计算成本的解决方案。

Method: 提出MCCom框架，级联本地小模型与云端大模型。利用用户行为作为触发信号，仅在小模型失败时调用大模型。采用两阶段推测解码策略和迭代检索机制增强模型协作。训练了一个1.21亿参数的轻量级模型。

Result: 轻量级模型达到7B SOTA模型73.8%的性能。在RepoEval和StmtEval基准测试中，MCCom将推理延迟降低47.9%，大模型使用减少46.3%，同时通过有效协作将大模型的精确匹配率提升8.9%。

Conclusion: MCCom通过模型级联和创新的触发机制，有效解决了代码补全中准确率与延迟的权衡问题，在保持高质量建议的同时显著降低了计算成本和延迟。

Abstract: Line-level code completion requires a critical balance between high accuracy and low latency. Existing methods suffer from a trade-off: large language models (LLMs) provide high-quality suggestions but incur high latency, while small language models (SLMs) are fast but often suboptimal. We propose MCCom (Model-Cascading-based code Completion), a framework that cascades a local SLM with a cloud-based LLM. To achieve effective cascading, MCCom leverages user actions as a novel signal to trigger the LLM only when the SLM fails, significantly reducing cloud computation costs. Furthermore, we introduce a two-stage speculative decoding strategy and an iterative retrieval mechanism to enhance collaboration between the models. We also train a 121M-parameter lightweight model, which achieves 73.8% of the performance of a 7B state-of-the-art model. Evaluated on RepoEval and a new real-world benchmark StmtEval, MCCom reduces inference latency by up to 47.9% and LLM usage by 46.3%, while improving the LLM's exact match rate by 8.9% through effective collaboration.

</details>


### [112] [Detecting Semantic Alignments between Textual Specifications and Domain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06037)
*Shwetali Shimangaud,Lola Burgueño,Rijul Saini,Jörg Kienzle*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种使用自然语言处理和LLM技术来评估部分领域模型与文本规范对齐程度的方法，能够以高精度识别模型元素的对齐、错位或未分类状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软件工程早期阶段，从文本规范中推导出领域模型非常有用，但创建正确的领域模型并建立与文本规范的清晰链接对新手建模者来说具有挑战性。需要一种自动评估模型与规范对齐程度的方法。

Method: 使用NLP技术预处理文本，为每个模型元素生成人工自然语言规范描述，然后使用LLM比较生成的描述与原始规范中的匹配句子。算法将每个模型元素分类为对齐（正确）、错位（不正确）或未分类（证据不足），并输出提供分类证据的相关句子。

Result: 在文献中的示例集上评估，包含不同领域，每个包含文本规范和参考领域模型，以及通过突变从正确模型系统派生的包含建模错误的模型。结果显示能够以接近1的精度和约78%的召回率识别对齐和错位，每个模型元素的执行时间在18秒到1分钟之间。

Conclusion: 由于算法几乎从不错误分类模型元素，并且能够分类超过3/4的模型元素，因此可以集成到建模工具中提供正面反馈或生成警告，或用于离线验证和质量评估。

Abstract: Context: Having domain models derived from textual specifications has proven to be very useful in the early phases of software engineering. However, creating correct domain models and establishing clear links with the textual specification is a challenging task, especially for novice modelers. Objectives: We propose an approach for determining the alignment between a partial domain model and a textual specification.
