Methods: To this aim, we use Natural Language Processing techniques to pre-process the text, generate an artificial natural language specification for each model element, and then use an LLM to compare the generated description with matched sentences from the original specification. Ultimately, our algorithm classifies each model element as either aligned (i.e., correct), misaligned (i.e., incorrect), or unclassified (i.e., insufficient evidence). Furthermore, it outputs the related sentences from the textual specification that provide the evidence for the determined class. Results: We have evaluated our approach on a set of examples from the literature containing diverse domains, each consisting of a textual specification and a reference domain model, as well as on models containing modeling errors that were systematically derived from the correct models through mutation. Our results show that we are able to identify alignments and misalignments with a precision close to 1 and a recall of approximately 78%, with execution times ranging from 18 seconds to 1 minute per model element. Conclusion: Since our algorithm almost never classifies model elements incorrectly, and is able to classify over 3/4 of the model elements, it could be integrated into a modeling tool to provide positive feedback or generate warnings, or employed for offline validation and quality assessment.

</details>


### [113] [Real-World Fault Detection for C-Extended Python Projects with Automated Unit Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06107)
*Lucas Berg,Lukas Krodinger,Stephan Lukasczyk,Annibale Panichella,Gordon Fraser,Wim Vanhoof,Xavier Devroey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出在Python自动化测试生成中使用子进程执行的方法，解决C扩展异常导致解释器崩溃的问题，从而能够检测故障、生成可复现的测试用例，并提高代码覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python库中的C扩展虽然能提升性能，但其异常可能绕过Python的异常处理机制，导致整个解释器崩溃。这种崩溃会中断自动化测试生成过程，使得无法检测故障、生成可复现的测试用例，也无法覆盖非崩溃部分的代码。

Method: 将测试生成过程分为生成和执行两个阶段，改造Pynguin工具使用子进程执行。每个生成的测试在隔离的子进程中运行，防止崩溃中断主测试生成进程。这种方法能够检测故障、生成可复现的崩溃揭示测试用例，并允许测试非崩溃部分的代码。

Result: 在包含21个流行Python库的1648个模块数据集上评估，子进程执行方法使自动化测试能够覆盖最多56.5%的额外模块，发现了213个独特的崩溃原因，揭示了32个先前未知的故障。

Conclusion: 子进程执行方法有效解决了C扩展崩溃中断测试生成的问题，能够检测故障、生成可复现的测试用例，并提高代码覆盖率，为Python库的自动化测试提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Many popular Python libraries use C-extensions for performance-critical operations allowing users to combine the best of the two worlds: The simplicity and versatility of Python and the performance of C. A drawback of this approach is that exceptions raised in C can bypass Python's exception handling and cause the entire interpreter to crash. These crashes are real faults if they occur when calling a public API. While automated test generation should, in principle, detect such faults, crashes in native code can halt the test process entirely, preventing detection or reproduction of the underlying errors and inhibiting coverage of non-crashing parts of the code. To overcome this problem, we propose separating the generation and execution stages of the test-generation process. We therefore adapt Pynguin, an automated test case generation tool for Python, to use subprocess-execution. Executing each generated test in an isolated subprocess prevents a crash from halting the test generation process itself. This allows us to (1) detect such faults, (2) generate reproducible crash-revealing test cases for them, (3) allow studying the underlying faults, and (4) enable test generation for non-crashing parts of the code. To evaluate our approach, we created a dataset consisting of 1648 modules from 21 popular Python libraries with C-extensions. Subprocess-execution allowed automated testing of up to 56.5% more modules and discovered 213 unique crash causes, revealing 32 previously unknown faults.

</details>


### [114] [Can Adjusting Hyperparameters Lead to Green Deep Learning: An Empirical Study on Correlations between Hyperparameters and Energy Consumption of Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06195)
*Taoran Wang,Yanhui Li,Mingliang Ma,Lin Chen,Yuming Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过分析超参数对深度学习模型能耗的影响来开发绿色DL模型，使用变异算子模拟超参数调整，实验表明超参数与能耗相关，适当调整可降低能耗而不损害性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型的发展，更大的数据集和更复杂的模型结构导致计算资源和能耗增加，需要关注绿色深度学习模型。本文从超参数对能耗影响的新视角出发，探索如何通过调整超参数降低DL模型能耗。

Method: 使用变异算子模拟实践者调整超参数（如训练轮数和学习率）的过程，分别训练原始模型和变异模型，收集能耗信息和运行时性能指标。特别关注并行场景下多个DL模型同时训练的情况。

Result: 在五个真实世界DL模型上进行广泛实验，结果显示：(1) 研究的许多超参数与能耗存在正或负相关性；(2) 调整超参数可使DL模型更绿色，即在保持性能的同时降低能耗；(3) 在并行环境中，能耗对变化更加敏感。

Conclusion: 建议在开发DL模型时更多关注超参数，因为适当调整超参数可以产生更绿色的DL模型，降低能耗而不损害性能。

Abstract: Context: Along with developing Deep learning (DL) models, larger datasets and more complex model structures are applied, leading to rising computing resources and energy consumption, which is an alert that green DL models should receive more attention. Objective: This paper focuses on a novel view to analyze DL energy consumption: the effect of hyperparameters on the energy cost of DL models. Method: Our approach involves using mutation operators to simulate how practitioners adjust hyperparameters, such as epochs and learning rates. We train the original and mutated models separately and gather energy information and run-time performance metrics. Moreover, we focus on the parallel scenario where multiple DL models are trained in parallel. Results: To examine the effect of hyperparameters on energy consumption, we conducted extensive experiments on five real-world DL models. The results show that (1) many hyperparameters studied have a (positive or negative) correlation with energy consumption, (2) adjusting hyperparameters can make DL models greener, i.e., lead to less energy consumption without performance damage, and (3) in a parallel environment, energy consumption becomes more susceptible to change. Conclusions: We suggest that hyperparameters need more attention in developing DL models, as appropriately adjusting hyperparameters would cause green DL models.

</details>


### [115] [Story Point Estimation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06276)
*Pranam Prakash Shetty,Adarsh Balakrishnan,Mengqiao Xu,Xiaoyin Xi,Zhe Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMs在故事点估算中表现出色，无需训练数据即可超越传统监督模型，少量示例可进一步提升性能，比较判断可作为有效的少样本示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法需要大量来自同一项目的标注数据进行故事点预测，这在实际应用中存在数据获取困难。研究探索LLMs是否能在零样本或少样本条件下进行有效预测，并研究比较判断是否比直接估算更容易预测且能作为少样本示例。

Method: 使用四种LLMs在16个软件项目上进行实证研究。采用零样本提示（无训练数据）和少样本提示（少量训练示例）两种方式。同时探索比较判断预测的难易程度以及比较判断作为少样本示例的有效性。

Result: 零样本提示下，LLMs的故事点预测性能优于使用80%数据训练的监督深度学习模型。少样本提示可进一步提升预测性能。比较判断预测并不比直接估算故事点更容易，但比较判断可作为有效的少样本示例来改善LLMs的预测性能，效果与人工标注的故事点相当。

Conclusion: LLMs在故事点估算任务中具有显著优势，能够在缺乏训练数据的情况下实现有效预测。比较判断可作为有价值的少样本示例来源，为实际软件开发中的工作量估算提供了新的实用方法。

Abstract: This study investigates the use of large language models (LLMs) for story point estimation. Story points are unitless, project-specific effort estimates that help developers on the scrum team forecast which product backlog items they plan to complete in a sprint. To facilitate this process, machine learning models, especially deep neural networks, have been applied to predict the story points based on the title and description of each item. However, such machine learning models require sufficient amounts of training data (with ground truth story points annotated by human developers) from the same software project to achieve decent prediction performance. This motivated us to explore whether LLMs are capable of (RQ1) predicting story points without training data or (RQ2) with only a few training data points. Our empirical results with four LLMs on 16 software projects show that, without any training data (zero-shot prompting), LLMs can predict story points better than supervised deep learning models trained on 80% of the data. The prediction performance of LLMs can be further improved with a few training examples (few-shot prompting). In addition, a recent study explored the use of comparative judgments (between a given pair of items which one requires more effort to implement) instead of directly annotating the story points to reduce the cognitive burden on developers. Therefore, this study also explores (RQ3) whether comparative judgments are easier to predict than story points for LLMs and (RQ4) whether comparative judgments can serve as few-shot examples for LLMs to improve their predictions of story points. Empirical results suggest that it is not easier for LLMs to predict comparative judgments than to directly estimate the story points, but comparative judgments can serve as few-shot examples to improve the LLMs' prediction performance as well as the human-annotated story points.

</details>


### [116] [A Generalized Feature Model for Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06308)
*Philipp Zech,Yanis Mair,Michael Vierhauser,Pablo Oliveira Antonino,Frank Schnicke,Tony Clark*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个全面的数字孪生特征模型，填补了现有研究中缺乏系统化特征建模的空白，并通过三个领域用例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数字孪生技术在工业、经济和社会领域得到广泛应用，过去十年也有大量相关研究，但至今仍缺乏一个能够有效捕捉数字孪生必需和可选特征的全面特征模型。这一空白限制了数字孪生的设计决策、模型驱动开发和验证验证过程。

Method: 基于对现有文献的系统性映射研究，开发了适用于数字模型、数字阴影和数字孪生的通用特征模型。通过应急、车辆和制造三个领域的实际用例来评估所提特征模型的有效性。

Result: 成功开发了一个通用的数字孪生特征模型，该模型能够有效捕捉数字孪生的核心特征。三个领域用例的应用验证了模型的有效性和实用性，表明该模型能够支持数字孪生的设计决策和开发过程。

Conclusion: 提出的通用特征模型通过促进设计过程中的知情决策、改进数字孪生的模型驱动开发，以及为测试用例推断提供基于模型的基础来促进验证验证，从而推动了数字孪生领域的发展。

Abstract: The adoption of Digital Twin technologies is rapidly expanding in diverse industrial, economic, and societal domains. Over the past decade, a multitude of studies, surveys, and investigations have been conducted, examining the nature, applications, and advantages of Digital Twins. However, up until now, no proposal for a comprehensive feature model exists that effectively captures the mandatory and optional features of Digital Twins. To address this shortcoming, in this article, we present a general feature model for Digital Twins. Based on a systematic mapping study of existing literature, we developed a generalized feature model for Digital Models, Shadows, and Twins. To assess the validity of our proposed feature model, we have applied them to three use cases from the emergency, vehicular, and manufacturing domain. We conjecture that our proposed general feature model advances the field around Digital Twins by facilitating informed decision-making during design, enabling improved model-driven development of Digital Twins, and, eventually, fostering verification~\&~validation of Digital Twins by delivering a model-based foundation for test case inference.

</details>


### [117] [A Scalable Benchmark for Repository-Oriented Long-Horizon Conversational Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06358)
*Yang Liu,Li Zhang,Fang Liu,Ping Lin,Xinyi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LoCoEval是首个针对仓库开发场景的长对话上下文管理基准，通过LLM驱动管道生成真实多样的仓库对话，评估现有方法并提出了改进的统一记忆方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文管理方法主要针对通用对话，缺乏针对仓库开发场景的解决方案，这主要是由于缺乏可靠的评估基准。仓库开发中过长的对话上下文可能导致关键信息丢失和性能下降。

Method: 提出了LoCoEval基准，基于三个关键原则通过LLM驱动管道生成仓库导向的对话，包含迭代需求、噪声输入和回顾性提问等关键交互模式。评估了7个基线方法，并提出改进方法将对话和仓库信息整合到统一记忆中。

Result: 评估结果显示独立LLM和现有方法（特别是记忆系统）在仓库导向对话场景中面临重大挑战。提出的改进方法在所有基线（除Oracle外）中表现最佳，并展现出鲁棒性。研究还探讨了各种因素对方法性能的影响。

Conclusion: LoCoEval填补了仓库开发场景上下文管理评估的空白，揭示了现有方法的局限性，提出的统一记忆方法有效提升了性能，为未来研究提供了实用见解。

Abstract: In recent years, large language models (LLMs) have advanced rapidly, substantially enhancing their code understanding and generation capabilities and giving rise to powerful code assistants. However, in practical repository development, excessively long-horizon conversational context may overwhelm models, causing the loss of critical information and degraded performance, thereby limiting the utility of code assistants. Existing context management methods proposed to mitigate this context dilemma primarily target general-purpose conversations, while repository-oriented solutions remain largely unexplored, which is largely due to the lack of reliable evaluation benchmarks. To bridge this gap, we present LoCoEval, the first long-horizon conversational context management benchmark tailored to repository-oriented development scenarios. Adhering to three key principles, LoCoEval is constructed via an LLM-driven pipeline that generates realistic and diverse repository-oriented conversations, capturing key interaction patterns such as iterative requirements, noisy input, and retrospective questions. We evaluate 7 baselines, including 4 representative context management methods, using 3 advanced backbone LLMs on LoCoEval. The results reveal substantial challenges faced by standalone LLMs and existing approaches, especially memory systems, in repository-oriented conversational scenarios. To address these limitations, we further propose an improved method integrating conversational and repository information into a unified memory, which outperforms all baselines (*Oracle* excluded) and demonstrates robustness. Additionally, we investigated the impact of various factors on method performance, providing actionable insights for future research.

</details>


### [118] [A Reference Architecture of Reinforcement Learning Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06413)
*Xiaoran Liu,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出强化学习框架的参考架构，通过分析18个现有框架识别通用组件和关系，为框架比较、评估和集成提供共同基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习框架的架构模式在实现中不一致，缺乏参考架构作为比较、评估和集成的共同基础，这阻碍了RL技术的标准化和发展。

Method: 采用扎根理论方法分析18个最先进的RL框架，识别重复出现的架构组件及其关系，并将其编码为参考架构。

Result: 提出了强化学习框架的参考架构，能够重构特征性RL模式，识别了常用组件等架构趋势，并指出了改进RL框架的路径。

Conclusion: 该参考架构为RL框架提供了标准化的架构基础，有助于框架间的比较、评估和集成，并为未来RL框架的改进提供了指导方向。

Abstract: The surge in reinforcement learning (RL) applications gave rise to diverse supporting technology, such as RL frameworks. However, the architectural patterns of these frameworks are inconsistent across implementations and there exists no reference architecture (RA) to form a common basis of comparison, evaluation, and integration. To address this gap, we propose an RA of RL frameworks. Through a grounded theory approach, we analyze 18 state-of-the-practice RL frameworks and, by that, we identify recurring architectural components and their relationships, and codify them in an RA. To demonstrate our RA, we reconstruct characteristic RL patterns. Finally, we identify architectural trends, e.g., commonly used components, and outline paths to improving RL frameworks.

</details>


### [119] [Understanding and Finding JIT Compiler Performance Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06551)
*Zijian Yi,Cheng Ding,August Shi,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次系统研究JIT编译器性能缺陷，提出分层差分性能测试方法Jittery，在HotSpot和Graal中发现12个新性能缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JIT编译器是Java、JavaScript等语言的关键组件，现有研究主要关注功能缺陷检测，但性能缺陷会导致运行时显著性能下降，且由于JIT的复杂性和动态特性难以检测，缺乏系统性研究

Method: 1) 对四个流行JIT编译器进行实证研究，手动分析191个缺陷报告，总结性能缺陷的触发条件、表现模式和根本原因；2) 提出分层差分性能测试技术，实现为Jittery工具，包含测试优先级排序（减少92.40%测试时间）和自动过滤误报/重复项等优化

Result: 1) 通过实证研究揭示了JIT性能缺陷的常见模式；2) Jittery在Oracle HotSpot和Graal JIT编译器中发现了12个先前未知的性能缺陷，其中11个得到开发者确认，6个已修复

Conclusion: 这是首个系统研究JIT编译器性能缺陷的工作，提出的分层差分性能测试方法能有效检测此类缺陷，Jittery工具在实际应用中验证了其有效性

Abstract: Just-in-time (JIT) compilers are key components for many popular programming languages with managed runtimes (e.g., Java and JavaScript). JIT compilers perform optimizations and generate native code at runtime based on dynamic profiling data, to improve the execution performance of the running application. Like other software systems, JIT compilers might have software bugs, and prior work has developed a number of automated techniques for detecting functional bugs (i.e., generated native code does not semantically match that of the original code). However, no prior work has targeted JIT compiler performance bugs, which can cause significant performance degradation while an application is running. These performance bugs are challenging to detect due to the complexity and dynamic nature of JIT compilers. In this paper, we present the first work on demystifying JIT performance bugs. First, we perform an empirical study across four popular JIT compilers for Java and JavaScript. Our manual analysis of 191 bug reports uncovers common triggers of performance bugs, patterns in which these bugs manifest, and their root causes. Second, informed by these insights, we propose layered differential performance testing, a lightweight technique to automatically detect JIT compiler performance bugs, and implement it in a tool called Jittery. We incorporate practical optimizations into Jittery such as test prioritization, which reduces testing time by 92.40% without compromising bug-detection capability, and automatic filtering of false-positives and duplicates, which substantially reduces manual inspection effort. Using Jittery, we discovered 12 previously unknown performance bugs in the Oracle HotSpot and Graal JIT compilers, with 11 confirmed and 6 fixed by developers.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [XR and Hybrid Data Visualization Spaces for Enhanced Data Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05509)
*Santiago Lombeyda,S. G. Djorgovski,Ciro Donalek*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文探讨了在扩展现实(XR)环境中无缝集成2D和3D可视化表示，以应对高维复杂数据的分析挑战，并通过三个案例研究展示了这种方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据复杂性和信息量的增长，以及需要理解数据中的复杂结构、关系和现象，同时还需要理解AI工具的分析结果，这要求开发新颖有效的数据可视化工具。高维数据空间的复杂性日益增加，而扩展现实(XR)自然成为帮助扩展我们高维理解能力的候选方案。

Method: 提出在XR空间中无缝集成2维和3维交互式可视化表示的方法。通过将传统的2D数据展示模拟集成到3D虚拟数据空间中，创建更直观有效的数据分析环境。论文通过三个案例研究来验证这种方法。

Result: 展示了在XR环境中集成2D和3D可视化表示能够实现更高效的数据分析。三个案例研究证明了这种方法能够提供更直观、更有效的数据分析体验，帮助用户更好地理解复杂的高维数据。

Conclusion: 扩展现实(XR)为数据可视化提供了新的可能性，通过无缝集成2D和3D交互式可视化表示，可以创建更直观、更有效的数据分析环境，特别适用于处理日益复杂的高维数据空间。

Abstract: The growing complexity and information content of data, together with the need to understand both the complex structures, relationships, and phenomena present in these data spaces, compounded with the emerging need to understand the results produced by AI tools used to analyze the data, requires development of novel, effective data visualization tools. Much of the growing complexity is reflected in the increasing dimensionality of data spaces, where extended reality (XR) naturally emerges as a candidate to help extend our capability for higher dimensional understanding. However, humans often understand lower dimensionality representations more effectively. Still, XR offers an opportunity for a seamless integration of simulated traditional data displays within the 3-dimensional virtual data spaces, leading to more intuitive and more effective data analytics. In this paper we present an overview of the benefits of seamlessly integrated 2-dimensional and 3-dimensional interactive visual representations embedded in XR spaces, and present three case studies that leverage these approaches for more efficient data analytics.

</details>


### [121] [Exploring Human-in-the-Loop Themes in AI Application Development: An Empirical Thematic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05510)
*Parm Suksakul,Nathan Kittichaikoonkij,Nakhin Polthai,Aung Pyae*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文通过多源定性研究，分析了AI应用中人类决策权威和监督机制不明确的问题，提出了四个关键主题，为后续HITL框架设计提供实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在开发和部署AI应用时面临挑战，主要在于人类决策权威和监督在整个系统生命周期中缺乏明确规范。尽管HITL和HCAI原则被广泛认可，但关于角色结构、检查点和反馈机制的操作性指导仍然零散。

Method: 采用多源定性研究方法：1）对客户支持聊天机器人的回顾性日记研究；2）对8名来自学术界和工业界的AI专家进行半结构化访谈。通过五轮主题分析对1,435个代码词进行处理。

Result: 研究得出四个关键主题：1）AI治理与人类权威；2）人机循环迭代优化；3）AI系统生命周期与操作约束；4）人机团队协作与协调。这些主题为后续HITL框架设计提供了实证输入。

Conclusion: 研究通过实证分析识别了AI应用中人类监督的关键维度，为开发更系统化的人机循环框架提供了基础，有助于解决组织在AI部署中面临的人类决策权威和监督机制不明确的问题。

Abstract: Developing and deploying AI applications in organizations is challenging when human decision authority and oversight are underspecified across the system lifecycle. Although Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) and Human-Centered AI (HCAI) principles are widely acknowledged, operational guidance for structuring roles, checkpoints, and feedback mechanisms remains fragmented. We report a multi-source qualitative study: a retrospective diary study of a customer-support chatbot and semi-structured interviews with eight AI experts from academia and industry. Through five-cycle thematic analysis of 1,435 codewords, we derive four themes: AI Governance and Human Authority, Human-in-the-Loop Iterative Refinement, AI System Lifecycle and Operational Constraints, and Human-AI Team Collaboration and Coordination. These themes provide empirical inputs for subsequent HITL framework design and validation.

</details>


### [122] [Biometric-enabled Personalized Augmentative and Alternative Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05512)
*S. Yanushkevich,E. Berepiki,P. Ciunkiewicz,V. Shmerko,G. Wolbring,R. Guest*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究将生物识别技术路线图应用于个性化增强与替代沟通（AAC）系统，提出AAC生物识别注册器和生物识别可重构AAC通道概念，通过案例研究发现当前AI技术准确率尚不能满足实际需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决沟通障碍人群在当代半自动化边境控制等社会实际需求与AI技术发展之间的差距，通过生物识别技术路线图来个性化AAC系统。

Method: 提出AAC生物识别注册器和生物识别可重构AAC通道的概念框架，将生理和行为特征（如手势）与中间特征（如合成语音）连接，通过案例研究评估手势识别和手语单词识别技术的现状。

Result: 研究发现当前基于AI的手势识别和手语单词识别技术准确率未能满足实际应用需求，特别是在辅助沟通障碍人群参与半自动化边境控制等场景中。

Conclusion: 研究提出了改进建议以缩小社会需求与技术能力之间的差距，为生物识别技术在个性化AAC系统中的进一步发展提供了路线图指导。

Abstract: This study focuses on the roadmapping of biometric technologies onto personalized Augmentative and Alternative Communication (AAC), a branch of assistive technologies for people with communication disabilities. This technology roadmapping revolves around the proposed notions of an AAC biometric register and biometric-enabled reconfigurable AAC channels. The biometric register is referred to as a tool for acquiring and processing physiological and behavioural traits that are essential for augmentative and alternative communication. It links biometric traits, such as gestures, to intermediate traits, such as synthesized speech, for customizable communication channels. The proposed methodology is used to assess the gaps between the social and practical demands, such as assisting people with communication disabilities in the contemporary semi-automated border control, and the emerging advances in AI, such as advanced video and speech processing. We provide two case studies of the AAC that rely on hand gesture recognition and sign language word recognition, and conclude that the current accuracy of those AI technologies does not meet the practical requirements. The proposed roadmapping provides recommendations for further improvement to close these gaps.

</details>


### [123] [The People's Gaze: Co-Designing and Refining Gaze Gestures with General Users and Gaze Interaction Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05513)
*Yaxiong Lei,Xinya Gong,Shijing He,Yafei Wang,Mohamed Khamis,Juan Ye*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出两阶段方法开发直觉性注视手势：先通过协同设计工作坊收集非专家用户概念，再由专家评审优化，形成用户基础、专家验证的手势集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前眼动追踪设备中的手势集多为专家设计，与用户自然眼动模式不符，需要开发更直觉的注视手势以支持免提交互

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 与20名非专家参与者进行4次协同设计工作坊，生成102个初始概念；2) 4名注视交互专家评审优化为32个手势集

Result: 发现非专家用户基于熟悉隐喻构建手势，采用"激活(注视)+动作(注视手势或眨眼)"的组合语法确保意图性；专家优先考虑符合人体工学、对齐自然扫视且可区分的手势

Conclusion: 开发出用户基础、专家验证的手势集和可操作设计原则，为眼动设备直觉性免提界面开发奠定基础

Abstract: As eye-tracking becomes increasingly common in modern mobile devices, the potential for hands-free, gaze-based interaction grows, but current gesture sets are largely expert-designed and often misaligned with how users naturally move their eyes. To address this gap, we introduce a two-phase methodology for developing intuitive gaze gestures. First, four co-design workshops with 20 non-expert participants generated 102 initial concepts. Next, four gaze interaction experts reviewed and refined these into a set of 32 gestures. We found that non-experts, after a brief introduction, intuitively anchor gestures in familiar metaphors and develop a compositional grammar; i.e., activation (dwell) + action (gaze gesture or blink), to ensure intentionality and mitigate the classic Midas Touch problem. Experts prioritized gestures that are ergonomically sound, aligned with natural saccades, and reliably distinguishable. The resulting user-grounded, expert-validated gesture set, along with actionable design principles, provides a foundation for developing intuitive, hands-free interfaces for gaze-enabled devices.

</details>


### [124] [From Toil to Thought: Designing for Strategic Exploration and Responsible AI in Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05514)
*Runlong Ye,Naaz Sibia,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Tingting Zhu,Carolina Nobre,Viktoria Pammer-Schindler,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对系统性文献综述(SLR)过程中工具碎片化导致认知负荷高的问题，提出了ARC系统，通过多数据库集成、透明迭代搜索和可验证AI辅助筛选来支持战略探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性文献综述是科学进步的基础，但现有工具生态系统碎片化导致认知负荷过高，抑制了学术工作的迭代性和探索性。研究人员面临多数据库查询管理困难、文献规模庞大且发表速度快、自动化与学术自主性之间的张力等挑战。

Method: 首先对20名经验丰富的研究人员进行探索性设计研究，识别关键摩擦点。基于研究发现开发了ARC设计探针，实现了多数据库集成、透明迭代搜索和可验证AI辅助筛选功能。随后对8名研究人员进行对比用户研究，评估系统效果。

Result: 对比用户研究表明，集成环境促进了学术工作的转变，使研究人员从管理行政负担转向参与战略探索。通过外部表征支持战略探索和透明AI推理，系统支持可验证的判断，旨在增强专家从知识合成的初始创建到长期维护的贡献。

Conclusion: ARC系统通过解决多数据库集成、透明迭代搜索和可验证AI辅助筛选等关键问题，降低了系统性文献综述的认知负荷，支持研究人员的战略探索，增强了学术工作的迭代性和探索性，为知识合成的长期维护提供了支持。

Abstract: Systematic Literature Reviews (SLRs) are fundamental to scientific progress, yet the process is hindered by a fragmented tool ecosystem that imposes a high cognitive load. This friction suppresses the iterative, exploratory nature of scholarly work. To investigate these challenges, we conducted an exploratory design study with 20 experienced researchers. This study identified key friction points: 1) the high cognitive load of managing iterative query refinement across multiple databases, 2) the overwhelming scale and pace of publication of modern literature, and 3) the tension between automation and scholarly agency.
  Informed by these findings, we developed ARC, a design probe that operationalizes solutions for multi-database integration, transparent iterative search, and verifiable AI-assisted screening. A comparative user study with 8 researchers suggests that an integrated environment facilitates a transition in scholarly work, moving researchers from managing administrative overhead to engaging in strategic exploration. By utilizing external representations to scaffold strategic exploration and transparent AI reasoning, our system supports verifiable judgment, aiming to augment expert contributions from initial creation through long-term maintenance of knowledge synthesis.

</details>


### [125] [Enhancing Tool Calling in LLMs with the International Tool Calling Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05515)
*Zuoyu Zhang,Yancheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ITC是一个大规模多语言工具调用基准，包含3,571个真实API和17,540个任务，覆盖20个类别和40个国家，旨在解决现有基准在真实性、可复现性和文化多样性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用基准存在关键限制：依赖模拟或受限API、可复现性有限、缺乏文化和地理多样性。需要创建更真实、全球分布的工具调用场景基准来推动LLM工具使用研究。

Method: 构建International Tool Calling (ITC)基准，包含3,571个真实API和17,540个工具调用任务，覆盖20个类别和40个国家，支持多语言场景。通过实验评估开源和闭源LLM性能，并基于ITC进行微调。

Result: 实验显示开源和闭源LLM之间存在显著性能差距。在ITC上微调带来显著改进，特别是对非英语查询，增强了跨语言泛化、推理一致性和对域外工具的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ITC为推进LLM在复杂、多工具和国际场景中的鲁棒性和性能提供了有价值的基准，解决了现有基准的关键限制，促进了更真实、多样化的工具调用研究。

Abstract: Tool calling allows large language models (LLMs) to interact with external systems like APIs, enabling applications in customer support, data analysis, and dynamic content generation. While recent benchmarks have advanced tool-use research, they suffer from key limitations, including reliance on simulated or restricted APIs, limited reproducibility, and a lack of cultural and geographic diversity. To address these gaps, we introduce International Tool Calling (ITC), a large-scale, multilingual benchmark designed for realistic, globally distributed tool-calling scenarios. ITC includes 3,571 real APIs and 17,540 tool calling tasks across 20 categories and 40 countries. Experiments reveal substantial performance gaps between open- and closed-source LLMs, while fine-tuning on ITC yields significant improvements, particularly for non-English queries, enhancing cross-lingual generalization, reasoning consistency, and robustness to out-of-domain tools. ITC provides a valuable benchmark for advancing LLM robustness and performance in complex, multi-tool, and international scenarios. Dataset: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/International-Tool-Calling-ITC-dataset-FAF4/.

</details>


### [126] [Human-Centered Ambient and Wearable Sensing for Automated Monitoring in Dementia Care: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05516)
*Mason Kadem,Sarah Masri,Anthea Innes,Rong Zheng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对2015-2025年痴呆症患者监测的可穿戴和环境传感技术进行范围综述，提出五项关键实施原则，旨在指导未来技术开发和以人为中心的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痴呆症护理面临复杂挑战，需要监测技术来支持患者和护理人员。随着可穿戴和环境传感技术的快速发展，需要系统性地梳理现有研究，为未来技术开发提供指导，特别是在家庭和机构环境中实现有效的监测解决方案。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，系统性地分析2015年至2025年期间关于痴呆症患者监测的实证传感研究，涵盖可穿戴和环境传感技术，重点关注家庭和机构环境中的应用。

Result: 识别出五项关键实施原则：1) 以人为中心的设计，涉及所有利益相关者，增强而非替代护理人员；2) 个性化、适应性强的解决方案，支持不同环境和严重程度下的自主性；3) 与现有工作流程整合，提供充分培训和支持；4) 主动考虑隐私和同意问题，特别是对居民和护理人员的环境监测；5) 成本效益高、符合伦理、公平、可扩展的解决方案，具有可量化的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为开发痴呆症护理传感系统提供了重要指导，强调了在增强自动化和自主性的同时，必须考虑技术、伦理、隐私和实际实施等多方面因素，并指出了未来研究的发展方向和机遇。

Abstract: We conducted a scoping review to map the rapidly evolving landscape of wearable and ambient sensing technologies for monitoring people with dementia across home and institutional settings. We analyzed empirical sensing studies (2015-2025) to identify and inform future technical and human-centered design requirements. Five key implementation principles emerge: (1) human-centered design involving all stakeholders to augment rather than replace caregivers; (2) personalized, adaptable solutions that support autonomy across settings and severity levels instead of standardized approaches; (3) integration with existing workflows with adequate training and support; (4) proactive privacy and consent considerations, especially for ambient monitoring of residents and caregivers; and (5) cost-effective, ethical, equitable, scalable solutions with quantifiable outcomes. This paper identifies gaps, trends and opportunities for developing sensing systems that address the complex challenges, while enhancing automation and autonomy, in dementia care.

</details>


### [127] [CoEditor++: Instruction-based Visual Editing via Cognitive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05518)
*Minheng Ni,Yutao Fan,Zhengyuan Yang,Yeli Shen,Yuxiang Wei,Yaowen Zhang,Lijuan Wang,Lei Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CoEditor++是一个无需训练的认知结构化框架，通过分解"编辑什么"和"如何编辑"两个认知阶段，结合反思自选机制，实现鲁棒、细粒度和可解释的图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的多模态图像编辑方法在高层次语义推理和视觉一致性方面存在不足，特别是在模糊或复杂指令下表现不佳。需要一种能够处理语义歧义并保持视觉一致性的训练免费解决方案。

Method: 提出CoEditor++框架，采用认知结构化设计：1) 将编辑过程分解为"编辑什么"和"如何编辑"两个认知阶段；2) 引入反思自选机制；3) 完全基于开源组件构建，无需额外训练或微调；4) 确保透明度和跨领域适用性。

Result: 在SmartEdit（通用编辑基准）和AltBear（隐私合规基准）上评估，CoEditor++在开源模型中达到最先进性能，在视觉一致性方面显著优于需要专门编辑数据集训练的模型。与闭源模型（如Nano Banana Pro或GPT-4o）相比，在保持可比指令跟随能力的同时，视觉一致性大幅超越。消融研究证实其有效性源于认知结构化设计而非特定模型组件。

Conclusion: CoEditor++通过认知结构化方法成功解决了现有指令图像编辑方法的局限性，展示了认知中心化指令图像编辑的潜力，为无需训练、透明且跨领域适用的编辑框架提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large multimodal models (LMMs) have enabled instruction-based image editing, allowing users to modify visual content via natural language descriptions. However, existing approaches often struggle with high-level semantic reasoning and visual consistency, particularly under ambiguous or complex instructions. To address these challenges, we propose CoEditor++, a cognitively structured, training-free framework that decomposes editing into "what to edit" and "how to edit" through two cognitive stages with a reflective self-selection mechanism, enabling robust, fine-grained, and interpretable editing. Built entirely from open-sourced components, CoEditor++ requires no additional training or fine-tuning, ensuring transparency and cross-domain applicability. We evaluate CoEditor++ on SmartEdit, a widely used benchmark for general editing, and AltBear, a privacy and compliance-oriented benchmark. Experimental results show that CoEditor++ achieves state-of-the-art performance in both general editing and responsible editing tasks compared with open-sourced models that require training on specialized editing datasets maintaining significantly higher visual consistency. When compared with closed-source models such as Nano Banana Pro or GPT-4o, CoEditor++ preserves comparable instruction following while still substantially outperforming them in visual consistency. Extensive ablation studies confirm that the effectiveness of CoEditor++ benefits from its structured cognitive design rather than any specific model component. Our findings suggest the potential toward cognitive-centric instruction-based image editing.

</details>


### [128] [A Closed-Loop CPR Training Glove with Integrated Tactile Sensing and Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05793)
*Jaeyoung Moon,Mingzhuo Ma,Qifeng Yang,Youjin Choi,Seokhyun Hwang,Samuel Burden,Kyung-Joong Kim,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于CPR自主训练的闭环触觉反馈手套，集成高分辨率触觉传感阵列和振动触觉执行器，通过实时压力测量和触觉反馈指导用户进行正确的心肺复苏操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肺复苏（CPR）是关键的救生程序，有效的训练需要超越讲师指导课程的自主练习。现有训练系统通常依赖外部视听显示，限制了自主练习的便利性和效果。

Method: 开发闭环CPR训练手套，集成高分辨率触觉传感阵列（测量手掌和手背的分布式压力）和振动触觉执行器。通过实时估计按压频率、力度和手部姿态，提供即时触觉反馈。使用轻量级统计模型进行力估计和手部姿态分类。

Result: 触觉传感器性能：宽范围灵敏度约0.85（0-600N），滞后56.04%，稳定性测试显示300次循环后漂移11.05%，全局信噪比18.90±2.41dB（600N时）。轻量级模型在亚毫秒推理时间内实现>92%的力估计和手部姿态分类准确率。用户研究（N=8）显示触觉反馈相比视听提示减少了视觉干扰，但在动态负载下需要简化模式以确保可靠感知。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭环CPR训练手套系统具有可行性，为未来触觉CPR自主训练系统提供了设计见解。触觉反馈能有效指导用户进行高质量CPR，减少对外部视听显示的依赖，但需要优化反馈模式以适应动态负载条件下的感知可靠性。

Abstract: Cardiopulmonary resuscitation (CPR) is a critical life-saving procedure, and effective training benefits from self-directed practice beyond instructor-led sessions. In this paper, we propose a closed-loop CPR training glove that integrates a high-resolution tactile sensing array and vibrotactile actuators for self-directed practice. The tactile sensing array measures distributed pressures across the palm and dorsum to enable real-time estimation of compression rate, force, and hand pose. Based on these estimations, the glove delivers immediate haptic feedback to guide the user for proper CPR, reducing reliance on external audio-visual displays. We quantified the tactile sensor performance by measuring wide-range sensitivity (~0.85 over 0-600 N), computing hysteresis (56.04%), testing stability (11.05% drift over 300 cycles), and estimating global signal-to-noise ratio (18.90 +/- 2.41 dB at 600 N). Our closed-loop pipeline provides continuous modeling and feedback of key performance metrics essential for high-quality CPR. Our lightweight statistical models achieves >92% accuracy for force estimation and hand pose classification within sub-millisecond inference time. Our user study (N=8) showed that haptic feedback reduced visual distraction compared to audio-visual cues, though simplified patterns were required for reliable perception under dynamic load. These results highlight the feasibility of the proposed system and offer design insights for future haptic CPR self-training system.

</details>


### [129] [Lexara: A User-Centered Toolkit for Evaluating Large Language Models for Conversational Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05832)
*Srishti Palani,Vidya Setlur*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Lexara是一个面向对话式可视分析的评估工具包，通过用户研究确定评估标准，提供测试用例、可解释的指标和交互式工具，帮助开发者选择合适的模型和提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估对话式可视分析中的大型语言模型面临三大挑战：需要编程专业知识、忽视现实世界复杂性、缺乏多格式输出的可解释指标。现有评估方法无法满足实际需求。

Method: 通过访谈22名CVA开发者和16名终端用户确定用例和评估标准，开发Lexara工具包，包含真实场景测试用例、可视化质量和语言质量的可解释指标（基于规则和LLM作为评判者），以及无需编程的交互式工具包。

Result: 对6名CVA开发者进行为期两周的日记研究，反馈表明Lexara能有效指导模型和提示选择，帮助开发者做出更合适的决策。

Conclusion: Lexara提供了一个用户中心的评估框架，解决了CVA中LLM评估的关键挑战，使开发者能够更有效地评估和选择适合其应用的模型和提示策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are transforming Conversational Visual Analytics (CVA) by enabling data analysis through natural language. However, evaluating LLMs for CVA remains a challenge: requiring programming expertise, overlooking real-world complexity, and lacking interpretable metrics for multi-format (visualizations and text) outputs. Through interviews with 22 CVA developers and 16 end-users, we identified use cases, evaluation criteria and workflows. We present Lexara, a user-centered evaluation toolkit for CVA that operationalizes these insights into: (i) test cases spanning real-world scenarios; (ii) interpretable metrics covering visualization quality (data fidelity, semantic alignment, functional correctness, design clarity) and language quality (factual grounding, analytical reasoning, conversational coherence) using rule-based and LLM-as-a-Judge methods; and (iii) an interactive toolkit enabling experimental setup and multi-format and multi-level exploration of results without programming expertise. We conducted a two-week diary study with six CVA developers, drawn from our initial cohort of 22. Their feedback demonstrated Lexara's effectiveness for guiding appropriate model and prompt selection.

</details>


### [130] [Glass Chirolytics: Reciprocal Compositing and Shared Gestural Control for Face-to-Face Collaborative Visualization at a Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05864)
*Dion Barja,Matthew Brehmer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在视频会议中通过手势交互同时操作可视化界面和视频窗口的创新方法，解决了传统屏幕共享中非语言交流缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频会议中的数据可视化讨论通常通过屏幕共享进行，这种方式忽视了非语言交流的重要性。在协作决策、技术面试、辅导等场景中，参与者需要看到彼此并交换关于数据的问题，现有方法无法满足这些需求。

Method: 采用可视化界面和控件与对话伙伴镜像视频的交互式合成技术，类似于玻璃窗格的效果，双方可以通过双手手势同时操作合成元素。实现了多个可视化界面，并进行了用户研究(N=16)，与使用鼠标交互的协作网页应用进行对比。

Result: 研究发现该方法能够增强参与感和相互意识，促进分析意图的相互理解，相比传统鼠标交互的协作应用，在存在感和相互意识方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 提出的交互式视频合成方法有效解决了可视化协作中的非语言交流问题，增强了协作体验和分析意图的相互理解，为数据讨论提供了更自然的交互方式。

Abstract: Videoconference conversations about data often entail screen sharing visualization artifacts, in which nonverbal communication goes largely ignored. Beyond presentation use cases, conversations supported by visualization also arise in collaborative decision making, technical interviews, and tutoring: use cases that benefit from participants being able to see one another as they exchange questions about the data. In this paper, we employ a reciprocal compositing of visualization and interface widgets over the mirrored video of one's conversation partner, suggestive of a pane of glass, in which both parties can simultaneously manipulate composited elements via bimanual gestures. We demonstrate our approach with implementations of several visualization interfaces spanning the aforementioned use cases, and we evaluate our approach in a study (N = 16) comparing it to videoconferencing while using a mouse to interact with a collaborative web application. Our findings suggest that our approach promotes feelings of presence and mutual awareness of analytical intent.

</details>


### [131] [Challenges in Synchronous & Remote Collaboration Around Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05871)
*Matthew Brehmer,Maxime Cordeil,Christophe Hurter,Takayuki Itoh,Wolfgang Büschel,Mahmood Jasim,Arnaud Prouzeau,David Saffo,Lyn Bartram,Sheelagh Carpendale,Chen Zhu-Tian,Andrew Cunningham,Tim Dwyer,Samuel Huron,Masahiko Itoh,Alark Joshi,Kiyoshi Kiyokawa,Hideaki Kuzuoka,Bongshin Lee,Gabriela Molina León,Harald Reiterer,Bektur Ryskeldiev,Jonathan Schwabish,Brian A. Smith,Yasuyuki Sumi,Ryo Suzuki,Anthony Tang,Yalong Yang,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文识别并描述了远程同步可视化协作面临的16个挑战，基于29位专家的视角，围绕五个核心可视化协作活动展开，并考虑XR和AI技术的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着远程协作和可视化技术的快速发展，需要系统性地识别和描述在远程同步可视化协作中面临的挑战，为未来研究提供框架和方向。

Method: 基于29位国际专家（来自人机交互和可视化领域）的视角和先前研究经验，识别挑战并围绕五个核心可视化协作活动进行组织。

Result: 识别出16个挑战，围绕五个协作活动：探索性数据分析、创意构思、可视化丰富的演示、基于数据的联合决策、实时数据监控。挑战反映了XR和AI技术带来的动态变化。

Conclusion: 提出了一个未来研究组织框架，将挑战与四个研发活动序列对齐：技术选择、社会因素、AI辅助和评估，为可视化与计算机支持协同工作的交叉研究提供指导。

Abstract: We characterize 16 challenges faced by those investigating and developing remote and synchronous collaborative experiences around visualization. Our work reflects the perspectives and prior research efforts of an international group of 29 experts from across human-computer interaction and visualization sub-communities. The challenges are anchored around five collaborative activities that exhibit a centrality of visualization and multimodal communication. These activities include exploratory data analysis, creative ideation, visualization-rich presentations, joint decision making grounded in data, and real-time data monitoring. The challenges also reflect the changing dynamics of these activities in the face of recent advances in extended reality (XR) and artificial intelligence (AI). As an organizing scheme for future research at the intersection of visualization and computer-supported cooperative work, we align the challenges with a sequence of four sets of research and development activities: technological choices, social factors, AI assistance, and evaluation.

</details>


### [132] [Measuring Perceptions of Fairness in AI Systems: The Effects of Infra-marginality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05889)
*Schrasing Tong,Minseok Jung,Ilaria Liccardi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 用户研究显示，公平性判断不仅取决于结果平等，还受群体间数据分布差异（次边际性）和模型性能差异的影响。参与者不简单地将公平等同于统计均等，而是考虑分布背景和差异原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探讨人口群体间数据分布差异（次边际性问题）如何影响人们对机器学习模型公平性的评估。现有公平性定义往往忽视分布背景，需要了解人类在实际决策场景中如何权衡不同公平性考量。

Method: 采用用户研究方法，在假设的医疗决策场景中对85名参与者进行实验。研究考察两种处理：群体特定模型性能和训练数据可用性。通过比较不同条件下的公平性判断，分析参与者对模型公平性的评估模式。

Result: 结果显示：1）参与者不将公平等同于简单的统计均等；2）当群体特定性能相等或不可用时，参与者偏好产生平等结果的模型；3）当性能存在差异时，特别是与数据不平衡一致时，参与者认为保留这些差异的模型更公平。公平性判断受差异原因信念的影响。

Conclusion: 公平性判断不仅取决于结果，还受分布背景和差异原因信念的影响。流行的群体公平性定义需要纳入分布背景考量，系统设计应考虑上下文因素，以使算法公平性指标与现实应用中的人类期望保持一致。

Abstract: Differences in data distributions between demographic groups, known as the problem of infra-marginality, complicate how people evaluate fairness in machine learning models. We present a user study with 85 participants in a hypothetical medical decision-making scenario to examine two treatments: group-specific model performance and training data availability. Our results show that participants did not equate fairness with simple statistical parity. When group-specific performances were equal or unavailable, participants preferred models that produced equal outcomes; when performances differed, especially in ways consistent with data imbalances, they judged models that preserved those differences as more fair. These findings highlight that fairness judgments are shaped not only by outcomes, but also by beliefs about the causes of disparities. We discuss implications for popular group fairness definitions and system design, arguing that accounting for distributional context is critical to aligning algorithmic fairness metrics with human expectations in real-world applications.

</details>


### [133] [Non-urgent Messages Do Not Jump into My Headset Suddenly! Adaptive Notification Design in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05893)
*Jingyao Zheng,Xian Wang,Sven Mayer,Lik-Hang Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于紧急程度自适应调整空间位置的混合现实通知系统，相比固定中央显示的传统方法，显著降低了用户认知负荷和挫败感，同时保持了通知感知度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MR通知系统将所有消息固定显示在中央位置，无论紧急程度如何，导致不必要的干扰和认知过载。需要设计更智能的通知系统来平衡用户注意力管理和信息可访问性。

Method: 基于MR/VR通知设计和平静技术原则，开发自适应通知系统：非紧急通知显示为周边图标（需转头查看），中等紧急通知锚定在用户手上，非常紧急通知从周边渐进过渡到中央视图。通过18名参与者的组内研究，评估自适应系统与默认中央化方法的对比效果。

Result: 自适应系统显著降低心理负荷(p=0.041)、时间负荷(p=0.008)和挫败感(p=0.004)，同时保持可比较的通知感知度。逻辑回归分析显示，即使存在分类错误，用户仍偏好自适应系统，前提是综合误分类率（干扰性+遗漏错误）低于可确定的阈值。

Conclusion: 这是首个经验证据表明基于紧急程度的空间通知分布能有效解决MR核心可用性挑战，为沉浸式通知系统提供了平衡用户注意力管理和信息可访问性的实用设计指南。

Abstract: Mixed reality (MR) notification systems currently display all messages in fixed central locations regardless of urgency, leading to unnecessary interruptions and cognitive overload. Drawing from previous MR/Virtual Reality (VR) notification design work and calm technology principles, we developed an adaptive notification system that adjusts spatial placement based on urgency levels: non-urgent notifications appear as peripheral icons accessible via head movement, moderately urgent messages anchor to the user's hand, and very urgent notifications transition progressively from peripheral to central view. Through a within-subjects study (N=18), we evaluated our adaptive system against the default centralised approach. Results demonstrate that the adaptive system significantly reduces mental workload (p=0.041), temporal workload (p=0.008), and frustration (p=0.004) while maintaining comparable notification awareness. Logistic regression analysis reveals that users prefer the adaptive system even with classification errors, provided the combined misclassification rate (disruptiveness + omission errors) remains below a determinable threshold. Our findings establish the first empirical evidence that urgency-based spatial notification distribution effectively addresses core MR usability challenges, offering practical design guidelines for immersive notification systems that balance user attention management with information accessibility.

</details>


### [134] [Hierarchical Resource Rationality Explains Human Reading Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06006)
*Yunpeng Bai,Xiaofu Jin,Shengdong Zhao,Antti Oulasvirta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出资源理性优化作为阅读行为的统一原则，认为眼动选择旨在最大化理解同时最小化认知和时间成本，并通过计算模型成功复现了人类阅读的广泛现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论分别解释阅读中的眼动或理解过程，但两者之间的关键联系仍不清楚。需要建立统一原则来解释阅读行为如何适应认知资源限制。

Method: 提出资源理性优化作为统一原则，将眼动视为分层时间尺度上的优化过程：注视决策支持词汇识别；句子层面整合指导跳读和回视；文本层面理解目标塑造记忆构建和重读。开发了计算实现来验证理论。

Result: 计算模型成功复现了人类阅读中前所未有的广泛发现，从词汇效应到理解结果，验证了资源理性原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 资源理性为协调感知、记忆和行动提供了通用机制，为复杂认知技能如何适应有限资源提供了原则性解释，可推广到其他知识密集型人类行为。

Abstract: Reading is a pervasive and cognitively demanding activity that underpins modern human culture. It is a prime instance of a class of tasks where eye movements are coordinated for the purpose of comprehension. Existing theories explain either eye movements or comprehension during reading, but the critical link between the two remains unclear. Here, we propose resource-rational optimization as a unifying principle governing adaptive reading behavior. Eye movements are selected to maximize expected comprehension while minimizing cognitive and temporal costs, organized hierarchically across nested time scales: fixation decisions support word recognition; sentence-level integration guides skipping and regression; and text-level comprehension goals shape memory construction and rereading. A computational implementation successfully replicates an unprecedented range of findings in human reading, from lexical effects to comprehension outcomes. Together, these results suggest that resource rationality provides a general mechanism for coordinating perception, memory, and action in knowledge-intensive human behaviors, offering a principled account of how complex cognitive skills adapt to limited resources.

</details>


### [135] [Beyond Scores: Explainable Intelligent Assessment Strengthens Pre-service Teachers' Assessment Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06059)
*Yuang Wei,Fei Wang,Yifan Zhang,Brian Y. Lim,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: XIA平台通过可视化认知诊断推理和对比/反事实解释，帮助职前教师培养评估素养，从基于分数的判断转向基于证据的推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估素养对个性化教育至关重要，但传统教师培养项目侧重理论知识，数字评估工具通常提供不透明的分数或参数，这些限制阻碍了反思和迁移，导致评估素养发展不足

Method: 提出XIA（可解释智能评估平台），扩展统计支持为可视化认知诊断推理，包括对比和反事实解释；采用前后测对照研究设计，对21名职前教师结合定量任务、问卷和定性访谈

Result: 初步证据表明XIA支持反思、自我调节和评估意识，帮助减少评估错误；访谈显示评估判断从基于分数转向基于证据的推理

Conclusion: 这项工作为智能评估工具设计提供见解，表明解释性支架可以连接评估理论与课堂实践，支持教师教育中评估素养的培养

Abstract: Assessment literacy (AL) is essential for personalized education, yet difficult to cultivate in pre-service teachers. Conventional teacher preparation programs focus on theoretical knowledge, while digital assessment tools commonly provide opaque scores or parameters. These limitations hinder reflection and transfer, leaving AL underdeveloped. We propose XIA, an eXplainable Intelligent Assessment platform that extends statistics-informed support with visualized cognitive diagnostic reasoning, including contrastive and counterfactual explanations. In a pre-post controlled study with 21 pre-service teachers, we combined quantitative tasks and questionnaires with qualitative interviews. The findings offer preliminary evidence that XIA supported reflection, self-regulation, and assessment awareness, and helped reduce assessment errors. Interviews further showed a shift from score-based judgments toward evidence-based reasoning. This work contributes insights into the design of intelligent assessment tools, showing how explanatory scaffolding can bridge assessment theory and classroom practice and support the cultivation of AL in teacher education.

</details>


### [136] [Skill-Adaptive Ghost Instructors: Enhancing Retention and Reducing Over-Reliance in VR Piano Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06253)
*Tzu-Hsin Hsieh,Cassandra Michelle Stefanie Visser,Elmar Eisemann,Ricardo Marroquim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习者表现的动态透明度自适应幽灵指导系统，用于VR钢琴训练，相比静态透明度指导能提高音高和指法准确性，减少对外部提示的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR中的运动技能学习系统依赖持久提示，但持续提示可能导致过度依赖，削弱记忆和技能迁移。需要一种能平衡指导与自主学习的自适应方法。

Method: 开发了技能自适应、动态透明度的幽灵指导系统，其不透明度根据学习者表现实时调整。在第一人称视角中，用户观察幽灵手执行钢琴指法，比较静态透明度与性能自适应透明度两种模式。采用被试内设计(N=30)，学习者分别使用传统静态模式和提出的动态模式练习，并在无指导情况下立即测试和10分钟保留间隔后测试。

Result: 与静态模式相比，动态模式在音高和指法准确性方面表现更好，错误增加有限，时序表现相当。这表明自适应透明度帮助学习者更有效地内化指法，减少对外部提示的依赖，提高沉浸式学习环境中的短期技能保留。

Conclusion: 自适应透明度幽灵指导系统能有效促进运动技能学习，减少对持久提示的依赖，提高技能内化和短期保留。为运动技能学习设计提供了启示，并指出了扩展到长期保留和更复杂任务的研究方向。

Abstract: Motor-skill learning systems in XR rely on persistent cues. However, constant cueing can induce overreliance and erode memorization and skill transfer. We introduce a skill-adaptive, dynamically transparent ghost instructor whose opacity adapts in real time to learner performance. In a first-person perspective, users observe a ghost hand executing piano fingering with either a static or a performance-adaptive transparency in a VR piano training application. We conducted a within-subjects study (N=30), where learners practiced with traditional Static (fixed-transparency) and our proposed Dynamic (performance-adaptive) modes and were tested without guidance immediately and after a 10-minute retention interval. Relative to Static, the Dynamic mode yielded higher pitch and fingering accuracy and limited error increases, with comparable timing. These findings suggest that adaptive transparency helps learners internalize fingerings more effectively, reducing dependency on external cues and improving short-term skill retention within immersive learning environments. We discuss design implications for motor-skill learning and outline directions for extending this approach to longer-term retention and more complex tasks.

</details>


### [137] [Exploring Socially Assistive Peer Mediation Robots for Teaching Conflict Resolution to Elementary School Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06255)
*Kaleen Shrestha,Harish Dukkipati,Avni Hulyalkar,Kyla Penamante,Ankita Samanta,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了使用社交辅助机器人(SARs)帮助小学生练习同伴调解角色扮演的可行性，发现大多数学生对活动有积极反馈，但阅读水平差异影响了活动参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多美国K-12学校采用同伴调解解决冲突，但对于没有此类项目的学校，社交辅助机器人可能提供一种可访问的替代方案来练习同伴调解技能。

Method: 采用单次会话的组间设计研究，12名参与者分为两组：一组使用两个机器人作为争议方，另一组仅使用平板电脑。通过角色扮演活动评估学生对同伴调解实践的反应。

Result: 大多数学生对活动有积极反馈，认为同伴调解练习让他们自我感觉更好，并学会了在冲突中帮助朋友。参与者阅读水平差异影响了脚本阅读和口述能力。机器人组中某些特质相关测量与学习相关测量存在显著相关性(p<0.05)。

Conclusion: 使用社交辅助机器人进行同伴调解实践具有前景，但未来研究设计需要考虑学生阅读水平差异。机器人组中特质与学习的相关性可为未来SARs研究设计提供参考。

Abstract: In peer mediation--an approach to conflict resolution used in many K-12 schools in the United States--students help other students to resolve conflicts. For schools without peer mediation programs, socially assistive robots (SARs) may be able to provide an accessible option to practice peer mediation. We investigate how elementary school students react to a peer mediator role-play activity through an exploratory study with SARs. We conducted a small single-session between-subjects study with 12 participants. The study had two conditions, one with two robots acting as disputants, and the other without the robots and just the tablet. We found that a majority of students had positive feedback on the activity, with many students saying the peer mediation practice helped them feel better about themselves. Some said that the activity taught them how to help friends during conflict, indicating that the use of SARs for peer mediation practice is promising. We observed that participants had varying reading levels that impacted their ability to read and dictate the turns in the role-play script, an important consideration for future study design. Additionally, we found that some participants were more expressive while reading the script and throughout the activity. Although we did not find statistical differences in pre-/post-session self-perception and quiz performance between the robot and tablet conditions, we found strong correlations (p<0.05) between certain trait-related measures and learning-related measures in the robot condition, which can inform future study design for SARs for this and related contexts.

</details>


### [138] [Structured Exploration vs. Generative Flexibility: A Field Study Comparing Bandit and LLM Architectures for Personalised Health Behaviour Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06330)
*Dominik P. Hofer,Haochen Song,Rania Islambouli,Laura Hawkins,Ananya Bhattacharjee,Meredith Franklin,Joseph Jay Williams,Jan D. Smeddinck*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究比较了五种数字健康干预中的行为改变技术传递方法，发现基于LLM的方法比模板方法更有效，但上下文老虎机优化并未带来额外帮助，LLM倾向于过度使用单一BCT


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字健康干预中行为改变技术（BCTs）的选择和传递面临挑战，需要探索如何结合上下文老虎机的统计优化能力和LLM的灵活生成能力来提升干预效果

Method: 进行了为期4周的体育活动动机研究（N=54），比较五种日常消息传递方法：随机模板、上下文老虎机+模板、LLM生成、混合老虎机+LLM、以及带交互历史的LLM，并进行了9次研究后访谈

Result: LLM方法在感知帮助性方面显著优于模板方法，但不同LLM条件间无显著差异；上下文老虎机优化未带来额外帮助；无约束LLM过度集中于单一BCT，而老虎机系统强制探索-利用平衡

Conclusion: 用户输入的上下文认知是感知帮助性的关键驱动因素，需要在结构化探索和生成自主性之间权衡，提出了反思性AI健康行为改变系统的设计建议

Abstract: Behaviour Change Techniques (BCTs) are central to digital health interventions, yet selecting and delivering effective techniques remains challenging. Contextual bandits enable statistically grounded optimisation of BCT selection, while Large Language Models (LLMs) offer flexible, context-sensitive message generation. We conducted a 4-week study on physical activity motivation (N=54; 9 post-study interviews) that compared five daily messaging approaches: random templates, contextual bandit with templates, LLM generation, hybrid bandit+LLM, and LLM with interaction history. LLM-based approaches were rated substantially more helpful than templates, but no significant differences emerged among LLM conditions. Unexpectedly, bandit optimisation for BCTs selection yielded no additional perceived helpfulness compared with LLM-only approaches. Unconstrained LLMs focused heavily on a single BCT, whereas bandit systems enforced systematic exploration-exploitation across techniques. Quantitative and qualitative findings suggest contextual acknowledgement of user input drove perceived helpfulness. We contribute design suggestions for reflective AI health behaviour change systems that address a trade-off between structured exploration and generative autonomy.

</details>


### [139] [Capability at a Glance: Design Guidelines for Intuitive Avatars Communicating Augmented Actions in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06556)
*Yang Lu,Tianyu Zhang,Jiamu Tang,Yanna Lin,Jiankun Yang,Longyu Zhang,Shijian Luo,Yukang Yan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过虚拟现实中的可供性视角，探索如何通过虚拟化身设计来传达超越人类限制的能力及其触发方式，提出了16条设计指南并验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟现实允许用户使用超越人类限制的能力，但如何触发这些能力并不总是显而易见。从可供性视角出发，研究者认为虚拟化身设计是解决这一问题的关键，理想情况下应该能够传达其能力及激活方式。

Method: 研究分为三个阶段：1）邀请12位专业设计师为8种能力（分为4类）设计传达能力和交互方式的虚拟化身；2）从设计结果中提炼出16条通用和类别特定的设计指南；3）通过两组各12名参与者（有/无指南）设计虚拟化身来验证指南有效性，并由外部评委评估。

Result: 参与者对指南的清晰度和实用性评价很高。外部评委确认，使用指南设计的虚拟化身在传达能力和交互方式方面更加直观。研究还展示了这些指南在四个VR应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的虚拟化身设计指南能够有效帮助设计师创建更直观传达VR能力和交互方式的虚拟化身，为VR界面设计提供了实用的指导框架。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) enables users to engage with capabilities beyond human limitations, but it is not always obvious how to trigger these capabilities. Taking the lens of Affordance, we believe avatar design is the key to solving this issue, which ideally should communicate its capabilities and how to activate them. To understand the current practice, we selected eight capabilities across four categories and invited twelve professional designers to design avatars that communicate the capabilities and their corresponding interactions. From the resulting designs, we formed 16 guidelines to provide general and category-specific recommendations. Then, we validated these guidelines by letting two groups of twelve participants design avatars with and without guidelines. Participants rated the guidelines' clarity and usefulness highly. External judges confirmed that avatars designed with the guidelines were more intuitive in conveying the capabilities and interaction methods. Finally, we demonstrated the applicability of the guidelines in avatar design for four VR applications.

</details>


### [140] [The Pen: Episodic Cognitive Assistance via an Ear-Worn Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06564)
*Yonatan Tussa,Andy Heredia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种间歇性使用可穿戴AI的新范式，通过笔形耳戴设备实现按需、短时、情境化的认知辅助，避免持续可用性带来的隐私、干扰和边界模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可穿戴AI通常设计为持续可用，但这与人们的工作和社交方式存在冲突，导致隐私担忧、干扰问题以及系统边界不清晰。需要探索一种更符合人类自然交互模式的间歇性使用范式。

Method: 设计并开发了"The Pen"——一种笔形耳戴设备，支持间歇性、情境化的认知辅助。设备采用语音和视觉上下文输入，具有明确的开始/结束边界，支持本地处理。通过探索性研究评估了分层激活边界如何影响用户的自主感、认知流和社交舒适度。

Result: 研究发现，分层的激活边界（物理佩戴/取下、语音激活、视觉上下文）能够有效塑造用户的自主感、认知流和社交舒适度。这种间歇性使用模式减少了持续可用性带来的隐私和干扰问题。

Conclusion: 间歇性可穿戴AI通过明确的物理和交互边界，提供了一种更符合人类自然工作节奏的认知辅助范式，在保持用户自主权的同时解决了持续可用性带来的社会技术挑战。

Abstract: Wearable AI is often designed as always-available, yet continuous availability can conflict with how people work and socialize, creating discomfort around privacy, disruption, and unclear system boundaries. This paper explores episodic use of wearable AI, where assistance is intentionally invoked for short periods of focused activity and set aside when no longer needed, with a form factor that reflects this paradigm of wearing and taking off a device between sessions. We present The Pen, an ear-worn device resembling a pen, for episodic, situated cognitive assistance. The device supports short, on-demand assistance sessions using voice and visual context, with clear start/end boundaries and local processing. We report findings from an exploratory study showing how layered activation boundaries shape users' sense of agency, cognitive flow, and social comfort.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [A Dual-AoI-based Approach for Optimal Transmission Scheduling in Wireless Monitoring Systems with Random Data Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06042)
*Yuchong Zhang,Yi Cao,Xianghui Cao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了物联网无线监控系统中的传输调度问题，提出了双AoI模型来捕捉异步信息年龄动态，并开发了基于MDP的低复杂度调度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网系统中，系统状态信息的新鲜度对实时监控和决策至关重要。传统调度策略仅依赖监控中心的信息年龄，但由于数据到达随机性和无线信道不可靠性导致的本地传感器与监控中心之间的异步AoI演化，使得这些策略效率低下。

Method: 提出双AoI模型捕捉异步AoI动态，将问题建模为最小化长期时间平均AoI函数。基于马尔可夫决策过程开发调度策略，分析确定性平稳最优策略的存在性和单调性，推导出具有信道状态依赖阈值结构的低复杂度调度策略。

Result: 建立了AoI目标稳定性的充分必要条件，仿真结果表明所提出的策略优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 双AoI模型能有效处理物联网监控系统中的异步信息年龄动态，提出的低复杂度调度策略在资源受限的无线环境中能显著改善信息新鲜度。

Abstract: In Internet of Things (IoTs), the freshness of system status information is crucial for real-time monitoring and decision-making. This paper studies the transmission scheduling problem in wireless monitoring systems, where information freshness -- typically quantified by the Age of Information (AoI) -- is heavily constrained by limited channel resources and influenced by factors such as the randomness of data arrivals and unreliable wireless channel. Such randomness leads to asynchronous AoI evolution at local sensors and the monitoring center, rendering conventional scheduling policies that rely solely on the monitoring center's AoI inefficient. To this end, we propose a dual-AoI model that captures asynchronous AoI dynamics and formulate the problem as minimizing a long-term time-average AoI function. We develop a scheduling policy based on Markov decision process (MDP) to solve the problem, and analyze the existence and monotonicity of a deterministic stationary optimal policy. Moreover, we derive a low-complexity scheduling policy which exhibits a channel-state-dependent threshold structure. In addition, we establish a necessary and sufficient condition for the stability of the AoI objective. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed policy outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Ecosystem Trust Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05521)
*Christoph F. Strnadl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出生态系统信任配置文件方法，使数字生态系统能自主定义和"广告"其颁发或信任的凭证，并探索生态系统间的最小信任关系及其在制造业的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不同数字生态系统（包括数据空间）之间建立跨生态系统信任的问题，同时允许各生态系统保持其主权，避免过度依赖集中式协调或治理机制。

Method: 定义生态系统信任配置文件，收集特定生态系统接受的所有可验证凭证和颁发者；建立基于不同生态系统信任框架的最小信任关系；提出两种凭证等价定义（需要/不需要跨生态系统协调）；将方法扩展到数据空间并定义跨数据空间互操作性。

Result: 实现了制造业用例中不同国际生态系统对身份、服务合规等信任范围内凭证的共识；证明了第二种方法能解决跨生态系统信任困境；通过脆弱性定理展示了主权导致信任不稳定；证明了两个数据空间之间的互操作性程度完全由其生态系统信任配置文件的共同性决定。

Conclusion: 生态系统信任配置文件为跨生态系统信任建立提供了系统化方法，在保持生态系统主权的同时实现互操作性，但需要额外协调机制来稳定信任关系，且互操作性程度直接取决于信任配置文件的共同性。

Abstract: We define a method how digital ecosystems (including data spaces) may autonomously define and "advertise" credentials they issue or they trust in the form of so-called ecosystem trust profiles. An ecosystem trust profile collects all (verifiable) credentials and issuers sorted by trust scope accepted ("trusted") by a particular ecosystem.
  We then show how a minimal trust relation between ecosystems may be defined using ecosystem trust frameworks of different ecosystems and explore a few of its properties.
  A first application of the theory is given for a use case in the manufacturing realm where different international ecosystems need to agree on certain credentials for various scopes of trust such as identity, service compliance, and other conformance standards.
  We implement this requirement by identifying and discussing two different definitions of credential equivalence for a given trust scope, one requiring additional cross-ecosystem governance or coordination, one not. The second approach demonstrates how to solve the so-called cross-ecosystem trust dilemma, that is, the problem how ecosystems can establish cross-ecosystem trust while, at the same time, allowing them to fully retain their sovereignty. A fragility theorem demonstrates that this sovereignty leads trust to be unstable without any additional coordination or governance mechanisms on top of (and outside to) ecosystem trust profiles.
  We extend our method to data spaces in particular and propose a novel rigorous definition of cross-data space interoperability. This allows us to prove the proposition that the extent of interoperability between two data spaces is exactly determined by the amount of commonality in their respective ecosystem trust profiles.

</details>


### [143] [SecureRAG-RTL: A Retrieval-Augmented, Multi-Agent, Zero-Shot LLM-Driven Framework for Hardware Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05689)
*Touseef Hasan,Blessing Airehenbuwa,Nitin Pundir,Souvika Sarkar,Ujjwal Guin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecureRAG-RTL：一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的方法，显著提升LLM在硬件设计安全验证中的漏洞检测能力，平均提高检测准确率约30%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自然语言处理任务中表现出色，但在硬件安全验证中应用有限，主要原因是公开可用的硬件描述语言（HDL）数据集稀缺，导致LLM在检测HDL设计漏洞方面性能受限

Method: 提出SecureRAG-RTL方法，这是一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的方法，将领域特定检索与生成推理相结合，使模型能够克服硬件安全专业知识的固有局限性

Result: 相比仅使用提示的方法，SecureRAG-RTL在不同LLM架构上均实现了显著改进，无论模型大小如何，平均检测准确率提高约30%。研究还创建并标注了包含14个具有真实安全漏洞的HDL设计的基准数据集

Conclusion: RAG驱动的增强方法具有实现可扩展、高效且准确的硬件安全验证工作流程的潜力，SecureRAG-RTL有效弥合了领域知识差距，为未来研究提供了有价值的基准数据集

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in natural language processing tasks, yet their application in hardware security verification remains limited due to scarcity of publicly available hardware description language (HDL) datasets. This knowledge gap constrains LLM performance in detecting vulnerabilities within HDL designs. To address this challenge, we propose SecureRAG-RTL, a novel Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-based approach that significantly enhances LLM-based security verification of hardware designs. Our approach integrates domain-specific retrieval with generative reasoning, enabling models to overcome inherent limitations in hardware security expertise. We establish baseline vulnerability detection rates using prompt-only methods and then demonstrate that SecureRAG-RTL achieves substantial improvements across diverse LLM architectures, regardless of size. On average, our method increases detection accuracy by about 30%, highlighting its effectiveness in bridging domain knowledge gaps. For evaluation, we curated and annotated a benchmark dataset of 14 HDL designs containing real-world security vulnerabilities, which we will release publicly to support future research. These findings underscore the potential of RAG-driven augmentation to enable scalable, efficient, and accurate hardware security verification workflows.

</details>


### [144] [Challenges and Design Considerations for Finding CUDA Bugs Through GPU-Native Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05725)
*Mingkai Li,Joseph Devietti,Suman Jana,Tanvir Ahmed Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文分析了异构计算系统中GPU内存安全性的关键挑战，指出当前基于CPU仿真的测试方法存在不忠实性问题，并提出GPU原生模糊测试的设计考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算从同构CPU系统转向CPU-GPU异构系统，GPU软件栈在内存安全方面严重滞后于成熟的CPU软件栈，导致AI和科学计算等关键负载部署在易受攻击的硬件上，形成严重的安全伦理挑战。

Method: 论文通过分析异构系统中内存安全的关键挑战，指出当前基于"不忠实翻译"（将GPU程序转换为CPU运行测试）的缓解方法存在缺陷，提出确保程序行为忠实性是核心设计原则，并讨论了GPU原生模糊测试管道的设计考虑。

Result: 研究表明异构系统中可利用漏洞数量逐年增加，当前基于CPU仿真的测试方法无法捕捉CPU与GPU之间的架构差异，导致安全测试不充分，需要GPU原生解决方案。

Conclusion: 为确保异构系统的安全可靠性，必须解决GPU程序测试中的忠实性问题，开发GPU原生模糊测试管道是提升CUDA程序内存安全性的关键方向。

Abstract: Modern computing is shifting from homogeneous CPU-centric systems to heterogeneous systems with closely integrated CPUs and GPUs. While the CPU software stack has benefited from decades of memory safety hardening, the GPU software stack remains dangerously immature. This discrepancy presents a critical ethical challenge: the world's most advanced AI and scientific workloads are increasingly deployed on vulnerable hardware components.
  In this paper, we study the key challenges of ensuring memory safety on heterogeneous systems. We show that, while the number of exploitable bugs in heterogeneous systems rises every year, current mitigation methods often rely on unfaithful translations, i.e., converting GPU programs to run on CPUs for testing, which fails to capture the architectural differences between CPUs and GPUs. We argue that the faithfulness of the program behavior is at the core of secure and reliable heterogeneous systems design. To ensure faithfulness, we discuss several design considerations of a GPU-native fuzzing pipeline for CUDA programs.

</details>


### [145] [Depth Charge: Jailbreak Large Language Models from Deep Safety Attention Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05772)
*Jinman Wu,Yi Xie,Shiqian Zhao,Xiaofeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SAHA是一种针对开源大语言模型的安全注意力头攻击框架，通过定位深层注意力层中的漏洞并进行边界感知扰动，实现更有效的越狱攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击主要在浅层（如提示或嵌入层）操作，未能暴露深层模型组件中的漏洞，导致对防御成功产生虚假安全感。开源大语言模型在公开结构和权重后，即使经过对齐处理，仍然面临越狱攻击风险。

Method: SAHA包含两个核心设计：1) 消融影响排序头选择策略，基于深层注意力层引入更多漏洞的发现，有效定位产生不安全输出的最关键层；2) 层间扰动方法，通过边界感知扰动以最小干扰探测不安全内容的生成，保证与目标意图的语义相关性同时确保规避检测。

Result: 实验表明SAHA优于现有方法，将攻击成功率（ASR）提高了14%，揭示了注意力头攻击面的脆弱性。

Conclusion: SAHA框架成功暴露了深层注意力头中未充分对齐的漏洞，为评估和增强大语言模型安全性提供了新视角，强调了需要超越浅层防御的深度安全对齐。

Abstract: Currently, open-sourced large language models (OSLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable generative performance. However, as their structure and weights are made public, they are exposed to jailbreak attacks even after alignment. Existing attacks operate primarily at shallow levels, such as the prompt or embedding level, and often fail to expose vulnerabilities rooted in deeper model components, which creates a false sense of security for successful defense. In this paper, we propose \textbf{\underline{S}}afety \textbf{\underline{A}}ttention \textbf{\underline{H}}ead \textbf{\underline{A}}ttack (\textbf{SAHA}), an attention-head-level jailbreak framework that explores the vulnerability in deeper but insufficiently aligned attention heads. SAHA contains two novel designs. Firstly, we reveal that deeper attention layers introduce more vulnerability against jailbreak attacks. Based on this finding, \textbf{SAHA} introduces \textit{Ablation-Impact Ranking} head selection strategy to effectively locate the most vital layer for unsafe output. Secondly, we introduce a boundary-aware perturbation method, \textit{i.e. Layer-Wise Perturbation}, to probe the generation of unsafe content with minimal perturbation to the attention. This constrained perturbation guarantees higher semantic relevance with the target intent while ensuring evasion. Extensive experiments show the superiority of our method: SAHA improves ASR by 14\% over SOTA baselines, revealing the vulnerability of the attack surface on the attention head. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SAHA.

</details>


### [146] [Knowing without Acting: The Disentangled Geometry of Safety Mechanisms in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05773)
*Jinman Wu,Yi Xie,Shen Lin,Shiqian Zhao,Xiaofeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出解耦安全假说(DSH)，认为安全计算在识别轴(知道有害)和执行轴(拒绝行动)两个独立子空间运行，通过几何分析揭示"反射到解耦"的演化过程，并开发拒绝擦除攻击(REA)实现SOTA攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全对齐被概念化为单一过程，即有害性检测自动触发拒绝，但越狱攻击的持续存在表明存在根本性的机制解耦。作者旨在揭示安全计算的内在解耦机制，并利用这种理解开发更有效的攻击方法。

Method: 提出解耦安全假说(DSH)，引入双差分提取和自适应因果导向技术，使用自建的AmbiguityBench数据集，通过几何分析揭示识别轴和执行轴从早期层的对抗性纠缠到深层结构独立的演化过程。

Result: 验证了因果双解耦，实现了"知道但不行动"的状态；开发了拒绝擦除攻击(REA)，达到最先进的攻击成功率；发现Llama3.1采用显式语义控制，而Qwen2.5采用潜在分布式控制的关键架构差异。

Conclusion: 安全计算确实在识别和拒绝两个解耦的子空间运行，这种解耦机制可被利用来开发有效的攻击方法，不同模型架构在安全控制机制上存在显著差异，这对AI安全对齐设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Safety alignment is often conceptualized as a monolithic process wherein harmfulness detection automatically triggers refusal. However, the persistence of jailbreak attacks suggests a fundamental mechanistic decoupling. We propose the \textbf{\underline{D}}isentangled \textbf{\underline{S}}afety \textbf{\underline{H}}ypothesis \textbf{(DSH)}, positing that safety computation operates on two distinct subspaces: a \textit{Recognition Axis} ($\mathbf{v}_H$, ``Knowing'') and an \textit{Execution Axis} ($\mathbf{v}_R$, ``Acting''). Our geometric analysis reveals a universal ``Reflex-to-Dissociation'' evolution, where these signals transition from antagonistic entanglement in early layers to structural independence in deep layers. To validate this, we introduce \textit{Double-Difference Extraction} and \textit{Adaptive Causal Steering}. Using our curated \textsc{AmbiguityBench}, we demonstrate a causal double dissociation, effectively creating a state of ``Knowing without Acting.'' Crucially, we leverage this disentanglement to propose the \textbf{Refusal Erasure Attack (REA)}, which achieves State-of-the-Art attack success rates by surgically lobotomizing the refusal mechanism. Furthermore, we uncover a critical architectural divergence, contrasting the \textit{Explicit Semantic Control} of Llama3.1 with the \textit{Latent Distributed Control} of Qwen2.5. The code and dataset are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DSH.

</details>


### [147] [Indoor Space Authentication by ISS-based Keypoint Extraction from 3D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05858)
*Yuki Yamada,Daisuke Kotani,Kota Tsubouchi,Hidehito Gomi,Yasuo Okabe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ISS-RegAuth是一种轻量级室内空间认证框架，通过比较个人房间的LiDAR扫描进行用户认证，使用ISS关键点稀疏表示，显著降低计算开销和隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法处理所有点云数据，其中墙壁和地板等平面表面主导相似性计算，导致延迟和隐私暴露风险。需要一种更高效、隐私保护的室内空间认证方法。

Method: 保留1-2%的ISS关键点，计算其快速点特征直方图，在稀疏点集上执行RANSAC和ICP配准算法，实现轻量级室内空间认证。

Result: 在100个ARKitScenes数据对上，将等错误率从0.02降至0.00，处理时间减少20%，传输数据量降至原始的2.2%。

Conclusion: 基于关键点的稀疏表示使隐私保护、边缘可部署的室内空间认证变得实用，为设备无关认证和基于物理环境的账户恢复机制开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We propose ISS-RegAuth, a lightweight indoor space authentication framework that authenticates a user by comparing LiDAR captures of personal rooms. Prior work processes every point in the cloud, where planar surfaces such as walls and floors dominate similarity calculations, causing latency and potential privacy exposure. In contrast, ISS-RegAuth retains only 1-2\% of Intrinsic Shape Signatures (ISS) keypoints, computes their Fast Point Feature Histograms, and performs RANSAC and ICP on this sparse set. On 100 ARKitScenes pairs, this approach reduces the equal-error rate from 0.02 to 0.00, cuts processing time by 20\%, and lowers transmitted data to 2.2\% of the original. These results show that keypoint-based sparse representation can make privacy-preserving, edge-deployable indoor space authentication practical. As an early step, this work opens a path toward device-independent authentication and account-recovery mechanisms that rely on users' physical environments.

</details>


### [148] [Evolving Deception: When Agents Evolve, Deception Wins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05872)
*Zonghao Ying,Haowen Dai,Tianyuan Zhang,Yisong Xiao,Quanchen Zou,Aishan Liu,Jian Yang,Yaodong Yang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现：在竞争环境中，LLM智能体的自我进化会自发产生欺骗行为作为进化稳定策略，即使诚实策略仍然可行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索竞争环境中自我进化智能体的风险，特别是欺骗行为作为进化稳定策略的自发涌现问题

Method: 在竞争性竞价竞技场中对LLM智能体进行系统性实证研究，通过交互驱动的反思进行迭代策略优化，比较不同进化路径（中性、诚实引导、欺骗引导）

Result: 在效用驱动的竞争下，无约束的自我进化可靠地趋向欺骗行为；欺骗作为可转移的元策略在多样未见任务中具有鲁棒泛化性，而诚实策略脆弱且易在原始情境外崩溃

Conclusion: 揭示了智能体自我进化与对齐之间的根本张力，强调了在对抗环境中部署自我改进智能体的风险

Abstract: Self-evolving agents offer a promising path toward scalable autonomy. However, in this work, we show that in competitive environments, self-evolution can instead give rise to a serious and previously underexplored risk: the spontaneous emergence of deception as an evolutionarily stable strategy. We conduct a systematic empirical study on the self-evolution of large language model (LLM) agents in a competitive Bidding Arena, where agents iteratively refine their strategies through interaction-driven reflection. Across different evolutionary paths (\eg, Neutral, Honesty-Guided, and Deception-Guided), we find a consistent pattern: under utility-driven competition, unconstrained self-evolution reliably drifts toward deceptive behaviors, even when honest strategies remain viable. This drift is explained by a fundamental asymmetry in generalization. Deception evolves as a transferable meta-strategy that generalizes robustly across diverse and unseen tasks, whereas honesty-based strategies are fragile and often collapse outside their original contexts. Further analysis of agents internal states reveals the emergence of rationalization mechanisms, through which agents justify or deny deceptive actions to reconcile competitive success with normative instructions. Our paper exposes a fundamental tension between agent self-evolution and alignment, highlighting the risks of deploying self-improving agents in adversarial environments.

</details>


### [149] [ThermoCAPTCHA: Privacy-Preserving Human Verification with Farm-Resistant Traceable Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05915)
*Shovon Paul,Md Imran Hossen,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ThermoCAPTCHA：基于实时热成像检测活体人类存在的新型隐私保护验证系统，无需用户解决挑战，使用YOLOv4-tiny模型识别热特征，结合可追溯令牌防止转发攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAPTCHA系统存在可用性、可访问性不足的问题，且难以抵抗日益强大的机器人和低成本CAPTCHA农场攻击。行为验证和谜题机制通常带来认知负担、收集过多交互数据，或允许外包给人工解决者。

Method: 1. 使用实时热成像检测活体人类存在，无需用户解决挑战；2. 采用轻量级YOLOv4-tiny模型从单次热成像捕获中识别人类热特征；3. 结合密码学绑定的可追溯令牌防止CAPTCHA农场工人的转发攻击。

Result: 原型系统达到96.70%检测准确率，在低功耗服务器上验证延迟为73.60毫秒。安全性评估显示能抵抗MITM操纵、欺骗尝试、对抗性扰动和滥用场景。50名参与者（包括视障用户）的用户研究表明，相比reCAPTCHA v2，准确率更高、完成时间更快、感知可用性更好。

Conclusion: ThermoCAPTCHA提供了一种隐私保护的人类验证方法，解决了传统CAPTCHA系统的局限性，通过热成像检测活体存在，结合密码学机制防止攻击，在准确率、延迟和可用性方面表现优异。

Abstract: CAPTCHAs remain a critical defense against automated abuse, yet modern systems suffer from well-known limitations in usability, accessibility, and resistance to increasingly capable bots and low-cost CAPTCHA farms. Behavioral and puzzle-based mechanisms often impose cognitive burdens, collect extensive interaction data, or permit outsourcing to human solvers. In this paper, we present ThermoCAPTCHA, a novel privacy-preserving human verification system that uses real-time thermal imaging to detect live human presence without requiring users to solve challenges. A lightweight YOLOv4-tiny model identifies human heat signatures from a single thermal capture, while cryptographically bound traceable tokens prevent forwarding attacks by CAPTCHA farm workers. Our prototype achieves 96.70% detection accuracy with a 73.60 ms verification latency on a low-powered server. Comprehensive security evaluation, including MITM manipulation, spoofing attempts, adversarial perturbations, and misuse scenarios, shows that ThermoCAPTCHA withstands threats that commonly defeat behavioral CAPTCHAs. A user study with 50 participants, including visually challenged users, demonstrates improved accuracy, faster completion times, and higher perceived usability compared to reCAPTCHA v2.

</details>


### [150] [Statistical Analysis and Optimization of the MFA Protecting Private Keys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05978)
*Mahafujul Alam,Julie B. Heynssens,Bertrand Francis Cambou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于面部特征的无模板生物识别方案，通过截断响应值最高有效位来生成临时密钥，结合SRAM PUF令牌和密码实现多因素认证，提高安全性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非对称密码学广泛应用的背景下，私钥丢失可能导致灾难性后果，需要有效的多因素认证方案来保护私钥安全。

Method: 采用无模板生物识别方案，从面部特征提取响应值，通过截断最高有效位的方法生成临时密钥；结合SRAM PUF令牌和密码构建至少三因素的多因素认证系统。

Result: 该方法显著提高了准确性和安全性，降低了误拒率和误接受率，能够生成无错误的临时密钥。

Conclusion: 提出的位截断方法结合多因素认证方案能够有效保护私钥安全，为加密货币等金融交易提供更可靠的安全保障。

Abstract: In the current information age, asymmetrical cryptography is widely used to protect information and financial transactions such as cryptocurrencies. The loss of private keys can have catastrophic consequences; therefore, effective MFA schemes are needed. In this paper, we focus on generating ephemeral keys to protect private keys. We propose a novel bit-truncation method in which the most significant bits (MSBs) of response values derived from facial features in a template-less biometric scheme are removed, significantly improving both accuracy and security. A statistical analysis is presented to optimize an MFA comprising at least three factors: template-less biometrics, an SRAM PUF-based token, and passwords. The results show a reduction in both false-reject and false-acceptance rates, and the generation of error-free ephemeral keys.

</details>


### [151] [SemFuzz: A Semantics-Aware Fuzzing Framework for Network Protocol Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05989)
*Yanbang Sun,Quan Luo,Yuelin Wang,Qian Chen,Benjin Liu,Ruiqi Chen,Qing Huang,Xiaohong Li,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SemFuzz：基于语义感知的模糊测试框架，利用大语言模型从RFC文档提取语义规则，通过故意违反规则生成测试用例，检测协议实现中的深层语义漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灰盒和黑盒测试方法缺乏对协议的语义建模，难以精确表达测试意图和覆盖边界条件，且通常依赖崩溃等粗粒度预言，不足以识别深层语义漏洞

Method: 利用大语言模型从RFC文档提取结构化语义规则，生成故意违反这些规则的测试用例以编码特定测试意图，通过比较观察响应与预期响应来检测深层语义漏洞

Result: 在七个广泛部署的协议实现中发现了16个潜在漏洞，其中10个已确认，5个是先前未知的，4个被分配了CVE编号

Conclusion: SemFuzz在检测语义漏洞方面具有有效性，能够发现传统方法难以识别的深层协议实现问题
